2a1(tsa): sems da kyiganas nga ma sgrubs par gnas pa'i glu smras na/ cu da/
2a2(tsa): gcig pa'i sangs rgyas dang /sA la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams dang /nyid kyi ngo bo lus sprul pa'i lam mi gnas
2a3(tsa): pa'i rig 'dzin rnams dang /sku'i tshogs dang /nye bar gnas pa'i 'khor gshin rje gshed kyi dge 'dun du ma dang /khrag
2a4(tsa): byugs pa ma ma tshogs dang khams gsusu lha'i tshogs dang /'bras pa bdu gyi tshogs dang /zang bya
2b1(tsa): pa'i tshogs dang /ma skyes lam la lta ba'i tshogs dang /'byed pa mi mnga' ba'i thugs rje chen pos thabs gcig tu bzhugaso//de nas bcom
2b2(tsa): ldan 'das la 'khor rnams mdun par gyur nas gsol ba bta'o//lhun grub brdal ba'i pho brang nas//bde chen mnyam pa'i ngang nyid las//rdo rje
2b3(tsa): 'chang ba rdzogs pa'i sku//sprul pas 'gro ba'i don mdzod cig/ces gsol to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis 'khor rnams yongasu 'dun par
2b4(tsa): gzigs nas//chos nyid mi g.yo ba las bzhengs nas/dbang du bsdu ba'i ting 'dzin la bzhugaso//'jig rten khams thams cad g.yos par
2b5(tsa): gyur te/'uram 'ur rab tu 'ar ro//ches ches rab tu ches so//'khor thams cad rab tu brgyal bar gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis 'khor rnam
3a1(tsa): la gzugs nas//nyid kyi rang bzhin 'di spyan to//e su chos su byung chub sems kyi rang bzhin las/rkyen med pa'i rang thung sems//tham
3a2(tsa): cad 'byung ba'i snying po las//thams cad thugs rje shugs kyis sgrol//zhes gsung so//de nas 'khor de rnams rang gi rang bzhin gyis mthong nas/sems
3a3(tsa): yongasu grol bar gyur te/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang chen po la 'di skad ces gsol to//e ma rdo rje snying po khyod//snang stong gnyis med ye shes skus/sna tshogs
3a4(tsa): sbyor ba rdzogs pa'i bdag/yin par bdag cag yongs mi rtogs//rgyu rkyen med par 'thung mi 'tshal//zhes gsol to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis rdo rje
3a5(tsa): brdal gsor nas/'jig rten gyi khams rab tu g.yos te/'khor thaMd skrag nas rang rang gi srog gi snying po mdongs sol du bu'o//bcom ldan 'das 'ang
3b1(tsa): pa'i rdo rje des/srog gi snying po thams cad skye ba med pa'i dbyings na mkha' drag po'i khong du bsdus te gcig tu dri'o//las thad sgra'i dang po'i dang po la bsdus te gcig tu dri'o//de nas nyid kyi rang bzhin/
3b2(tsa): las ma g.yos pa'i 'khor bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal gzhon nu des/bcom ldan 'das mi gsol ba 'di bta'o//kun gyi rang bzhin nyid las byung //sems nyid rdo rje 'dzin pa khyod//mi rtog/
3b3(tsa): rang bzhin ting 'dzin la//lhun gyis grub pa'i dkyil 'khor dang /phyag brgya chen po rdzogs par mdzod//ces gsol to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis bde bar mnyam pa'i ngang las bzhengs te/sang
3b4(tsa): tham cad kyis skul bang dgongs nas/phyag rgya chen po rang bzhin lhun gyis grub pa'i dkyil 'khor 'di gsung so//rang bzhin cho sku mnyam nyid las//lhun gyis grub pa'i 'o brang brtag/yis chen po'i gzhal
3b5(tsa): yas khang //kun 'byung mi g.yo brtan par gnas//gru bzhi sgo bzhi chen po che//rta babs tA ra rgyan rnams ldan//klu brgyad lha brgyad tshangs pa'i zhing //rul sbal glang chen sgra gzigs 'brug/zhing dang
3b6(tsa): zhing chung thod pa sprul//gnam lcags khro chu chu srin thog/rgyu mi'i gling bu keng rus rgyan//e yi pho brang thong 'khor ba ni/lcags mkhar bcu gnyis bcu drug ldan//khyams dang gru chang sgo bzhi ldan//rang bzhin lhun gyi/
4a1(tsa): grub par gnas//de la gnas pa'i phyag rgya'i mu//rgyal chen sa yi pho brang du//klu 'jus gtan bris bdag sbyong //sto bye phyogs thong byed dang rgyang/ma rkye n+d+ha la sa kye+em+bu'i gangan//nu
4a2(tsa): dug chen daskyil ba'i sku//dbu gsum phyag drug zhabs bzhir sprad//g.yas dkar g.yon dmar mthing nag dbus//klu gnyan sa bdag zhing chung sbrul//'khor lo ral gri rdo rje g.yas//be gcon tun shing sdig 'jug g.yon//9
4a3(tsa): chen zhing stong stag lpags smad//dbul rin chen gzhal yas nang //rigs bzhi klu brgyad thod pas mdzes//bdud srin mu stegs rgyal po btsan//mgo dang tsid+has do shal byas//rin po che dang nor bus mdzes//snyan cha mgur mu
4a4(tsa): phyag zhabs sbrul//bdud mo 'thing nag thod sbrul brgyan//tsit+ta ban d+ha mkhyud dang stobs//thugs khar gsod byed dmug nag gcer//rigs bzhi'i brgyan dang zod pas mdzes//'khor lo tsit+ta g.yas g.yon gnas//zheng
4a5(tsa): dang sbrul dang lU gdum 'brul//ya ma'i thugs dkar badz+ra ni//ljang nag rdo rje snying phreng bsnams//klu brgya'i gdan la gnyen khri gsol//rin chen dbu brgyan rigs bzhis mdzes//gdug pa 'joms mdzad dregs pa'i sku//dregs
4a6(tsa): pa'i thugs khang rus sbrul gnas//sme ba thig le khrag dang dug/mtshon tsi sbrul sder mo ljags//tsit+ta 'od kyi gsang mdzod na//lha srin mi yi snying po 'chang //dbu la bya khyung gdugs pa 'dul//spu dang gshog pa mtshan
4a7(tsa): ma men mchu dang rjer mo gnam lcags 'bar//nyi zla khro mu'i spyani dang tshems//gdug pa za zhing zhe sdang sbrul//phrag so khrung mo smug na gnyis//spu dang gshog pa mtshon cha me//gdug pa za zhing de bzhin 'phrul//nu mar seng+ge brjid pa
4b1(tsa): wA / gas pa ma/ rdzu rgyu na sa da/ma gas sa sa / skyeng sa smra ra sgyu /
4b2(tsa): gdusu pa'i gzugs/dusu sbrul me 'bang dug lkug pag/dug lnga rnams chags skegs cing 'jos sa//gling migs ca khrag kyang khrag 'thung //dug sbrus rnangs pa dgral 'gro//mgung dang zhabs kyi long branas//mes khre bo tsho
4b3(tsa): raM ye kar ma tsha 'bre//sU r+ya'i gdan la phye nang srung ni//mes 'khor lo nyi ma'i 'od/bad gro snying nang rol pa/phyag rgya chen po bstan par brjod//dbus na brkyang bskus tshul du bzhugs//thugs kyi sprul pa mngon byung //
4b4(tsa): mon pa dza~Mg chen dreg pa'i lus//dbu dgu phyag dgu tsit+ta zhabs//phyag dgu blang dang don snying khrag/drod dang dbang po lus srog 'bral//bdud bzhi pho nya las la bskul//dri chen rak+Sha keng rus 'char//dbugs sogs bkrag mdangs
4b5(tsa): tshe sogs 'chang //yuM dang bcas pa thod mkhar bzhir//lhun gyis grub pa'i tshul du gnas//lcags mkhar phyogs bzhi'i gzhal yas na//sro gcod phyi yi mon pa gshed po bzhi//dug mda' lcags gzer gnam la 'tsher//
4b6(tsa): khyab pa spyi bcings sro 'dar gnag/pho brang phyogs bzhir lhun gyis grub//lcags mkhar brgyad kyi khyams khang na//las kyi sprul pa gshed pho bzhi//yuM dang bcas te tshogs brgyad 'joms//sgo med bcu drug lcags mkhar na//
4b7(tsa): ko kang sha za dregs pa'i tshogs/gnag pa gzhan tshogs lhun gyis grub//dregs pa'i las dang sbyongs pa'i grangs//bzhi dang bcu drug bcu gnyis brgyad//de bzhin lnga dang bcu drug brgyad//snying po don la bcu gnyis gsum//de yi snying
5a1(tsa): @po mchog bsdus lnga//dbang rim brtul zhug bgrod dang lnga//de srog dam tshig gnyis dang gsum//chos gnyis mi g.yo bsam gtad lus//rdzas dang 'dod pa bskul ba'i rkyen//sgrog dang las dang /
5a2(tsa): e ka la//bsdus nas rang dbang gcol phul//byin rlabs dang bcas thig le bsgrims//sangs rgyas kun bdag de bzhin gshegs//rdzogs pa'i tshul du lhun gyis grug/drug sngags mngon du byung ba'i rgyud lus
5a3(tsa): khro bo gling bzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi drag sngags 'byung ba'i gleng gzhi 'di gsungaso//rtsa ba mi 'gyur byang chub sems//gcig ni kun bzhi'i rang bzhin yin/chos kyi dbyis
5a4(tsa): kyi skye med pas//sangs rgyas sku gsum yis thugs//sems dang lus la bag chags brdal//snang dang srid pa'i chos rnams kun//byang chub sems dang dbyingasu 'dus//mtshar mar mi gnas rang bzhin gcig/spros
5a5(tsa): med bde chen dag pa'i sku//'gag med rang bzhin dag pa'i gsung //ma bsgribs don rnams rdzogs pa'i thugs//sems lus bag chags gsol ba'i ngag/bde dang sdug bsngal gnyis kar snang //don gcig byang
5a6(tsa): nub sems las byung //'gro ba'i rgyud las bag chags las//rgya rkyen med pag chags las/'byung ba rnam shes phung por 'dzin//sngang srid mi rtogs rtswa sbyin bral//nga ba skye med dang gis stong //rgyu rgyan 'a
5a7(tsa): sa su migs seM chus tshu gas mang sa khris/'angapa nga rdzo chung slo ming chen/sems panas rgyam panyis ma ni ri du rdzub nyis med/pa da ga ma
5b1(tsa): gsal//dbyings las yis rig pa yi//rang bzhin lhun gyis grub phyag rgyang gsal/rgyud drug gnas pa'i 'gro ba dang //mya ngan 'das pa'i sangs rgyas don//rgyu 'bras dbyed med chos skur gsal//ye shes rol pa'i phyag rgya bas
5b2(tsa): rang byung thugs kyi rol pa las//drag sngags yis rol par shad/yis bde ba'i thig le las//drag sngags thugs rje sprul par shar//chos sku bar ba'i dbyings nyid las//drag sngags sprul par rang byung shar//skye
5b3(tsa): med rnam dag rang bzhin las//drag sngags byung sprul par shar//skye med rnam dag rang bzhin las//drag sngags skye ba'i tshe phrul shar/sbyor ba kun 'byung rdzogs pa'i gzhi//drag sngags mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud las//drag sngo
5b4(tsa): 'byung ba'i bzhi bstan pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas yang bac+doMs kyis chos sku skye ba med pa'i ngang las/skye ba'i cho 'phrul drag po mngon du phyung ba'i rtsa ba'i sbyor ba 'di gsungaso//drag po nyer bar sbyor ba ni//
5b5(tsa): dur khrod gnyan ri'i dben gnasu//'brel pa gsum dang ldan pa yi//grogs dang dpa' rtul rmod pa bca'//de yi sbyor ba 'di lta ste//de bar gcig tu dril bsdebs pa'o//las kyi 'khrul 'khor chen po ni//tshe srog
5b6(tsa): bdag po ya ma dres sa//srid pa'i khyams khang tsit+ta la//dngas ma bzhi dang si ra brgyad//rtsa bran drug dang brgyad gnyis phyi/mdun pa gsum dang srog shing gsum//ldan pa'i thig le 'od kyi tshul//sa chu mi rlung na mkha'
5b7(tsa): dbus//ba zag mtshon gang rang sgra'i 'od//kun 'dus snying po bcu gnyis gnas//dbus kyi rnamakha' spyi mong las//stong 'og dngas ma bag chags tshal//rten pa'i sbubs na dgyes dang bdud//drod kyi sbubs nam gshid 'bru/
6a1(tsa): su'i mA Nar+dz+dza kA r+m+ma zlas smra sa sa skye na snang/gles su 'dudu /
6a2(tsa): kre ga ng+ya rnams na 'thun gyi sa sa rdzu sa phyi sa ya ga na
6a3(tsa): thug pa 'byin//thad gzir bzhin rang gzing nas//yang logs bcu gnyis rgya mdung la//gdug pa'i sgying gzer rdzogs par gnas//ser bcu gnyis sbubs dag la//gab pa sngags kyi phreng bas gang //de bzhid kyi mi 'du /
6a4(tsa): dbang bsdus sngags kyi de bzhin gang //dngam ma bzhi yi mtshams bzhi la//srog bzhi la thog 'joms pa yi//drag sngags gnas lcags thog chen gnas//rus sbal lto ba'i gsang mdzod la//lha srin mi yi snying po bsngal//'bru bsdus
6a5(tsa): nas gong bur gnas//de las yon du ma byung //las kyi 'khrul 'khor gang brtsams 'grub//gang mos las la nye ba yi//nye bar gcig tu dril bsde bru'i//las kyi 'khrul 'khor chen po ni//yang dag rtsa ba'i 'khor lo ste//zhing ras las/
6a6(tsa): rgyu bzhi lakA/thig sgor bzhi dang ldan par bskor//khar khyams sbrul bcas rdzogs byas la//dbus la sogs pa'i phyogs pani dang //de bzhin phyogs mtshams brgyad du brtag/sring ba'i khyams khang lto ba na//rgya gram rlung ji skrag mar gnas//
6a7(tsa): tshangs pa'i thig gnyis dang //zung theg de bzhin ma snyams la//dbusu gru gsum zla khams gsum/byi mi phyogs bzhir rgya gras na//dbus kyi snying po phyogs pani l-i/'khrul mig lcags mkhar gsum gsuM gnas//mya grams mtshams bzhi
6b1(tsa): khrag gi mtsho//rlung ni rtsa ba 'khrul 'khor yin//yang log mu khyud zlum po chu/skyom zhing g.yeng ba bsdud cing this/lho skyong las kyi 'khor lo yin//yang logs dbus mtshams phyogs brgyad du//rlung 'i rdo rje rtse mo re//snying po 'gyu
6b2(tsa): mig gnyis gnyis ldan//las rnams rdzogs pa'i spu gri gnas//rlung rig shed kyi 'khong lo yin//zung brgyad mu khyud 'khrul 'khor bcas//gsong byed drugu po rnams pa yi//drag po las kyi 'khor lo son//byang khrum snying po spu gri sngags/
6b3(tsa): /rtse ba gcig tu dril ba yin//gru gsum ming rus zla kham lha//bsrung ba kun ldan rdzogs par bri/ka/rgya gram bzhi yi 'phrul mig la//rtsa ba'i snying po drag sngags dgod//'khor yug rbad dang gsad pa'i sngags//gang sgrub lho sbyor gnas mthun dgod///
6b4(tsa): rim rdo rtse brgyad laka ming gi 'bru gsum dang por bri//gsod cing gnon dang 'phen pa yi//spu gri swa gsum rdzogs pa dgod//mtsho gling bar khyams rwa dbus la//sdang dang gnod pa de bzhin bri//ming gi 'bru gnyis srog dang sbyar//gang 'dod mtshamasu/
6b5(tsa): /de bzhin blang //b+hu ra nA ga gdug pa'i ha la'i mtshon//b+hi la khong 'dus khong dregs can//ya ma ki li rkarma bya//kyo bA kAM ma b+ha gur b+ha sha/s+yai raM e dbu ts+tsha ma d+hi/mA ra ya ni sbyor ba'i mchog/lto sbyor bcas la thod par gzhug/zhag bdun sduT sbyor/
6b6(tsa): sgrol ba bril//rus sbrel po zas 'khrul 'khor bsal//thong ba rgya grus rus sbal 'dod //sbyor ba gan+d+ha ldan kA la zad//sngags kyi rtsa ba dril la brdeg/zhag gsum nyi na gcig nyin zhag gnyis//gsad dang gnan pa zer gyi su 'i dzin
6b7(tsa): nga zas la sa'i mang 'khong la rtsa ba'i spyong ba yin/drag sdugaso sbyong ba 'di lta ste/e ga ti na ya ni rtsegs sum chos shig la//dgra zer srog sa ral phyogs cad/srogasum pa yanan /srog la thun phyogagasang 'a jug pa'i thar 'am tra~M/
7a1(tsa): las byung ba'i rgyam po yin//de nas padzas kyis drag par byung ba 'ang gsung /drug po san du byung ba las//gas can sngags me pa phyug/ gas rjen pa na sbyong //dU klongs pa hU~M sa /
7a2(tsa): khyud bdun dang ldan par bskor//de la bri zhing dgod pa ni//dbus gru gsum drag po las//brnag pa pho mo'i ming rus bri//zla kham bsrung ba de bzhin bri//mtshon cha'i 'khor lo rtsibs bzhi la//'dron pa'i stobs kyis 'bru bzhi dgod//mtshon
7a3(tsa): cha'i rgyal po bcu gnyis la//khro bo stobs kyi 'bru gcig dang //tshe srog bsdud pa'i snying po gcig/khong khrag 'brel ba'i 'bru lnga la//gsod cing gnon pa'i spu gri bcu//dgod pa'i gnas mthun lho sbyor dgod//phyi rim mu khyud bzhi po la//rigs/
7a4(tsa): bzhi sbrul gyis ra bas bsnams//lnga pa thod pa'i phreng bar gnas//drug pa sreg pa'i me ri 'bar//de dag tshe srog 'chang ba yI//ru sbal lto na gnas par bri//srid gsum lho na 'a chang zhing gsod//tshe srog bdag po'i lto na yang //mi 'gyur brtan par
7a5(tsa): ye gnas ta'o//de bzhin las kyi 'khor lo bca'//'khor lo lto sbyor thod par bca'o//thod pa'i 'og ma me re e//steng ma rul sbal rgya gram e//sbyor ba kun ldan gru gsum gtsang //drag sngags dmod pa zhag bdun bzlas//bcu drug gaM ni tshes
7a6(tsa): gcig la//gnan sngags thod par 'dug pa gnan//nya stong las kyi dmod sngags bskul//sngags kyi smong ba 'di lta ste//e ka ya ma he dun snying khrag ra ma tse ye//dgra bo'i srog la hur hU~M mA rA ya rbang //de nas yang bac+doMs kyis sbyor ba thaMd gaN+DI
7a7(tsa): ba'i las 'di gsung so//'phrin las kun gyi rtsa ba yi//gsang chen gcig tu dril ba 'di//drag po'i mngon phyung sbyor ba'i bcud/klu gnyan sa bdag gdug pa dang //ma mo rgyal po btsan the rang //mdze rims drag po'i yams kyi bdag/snying gzer bdun rtsi/
7b1(tsa): [।]9jug sbyo sa nas/gra s+ho //sa mdzus sa su b+h+yo/ d+hA b+h+ya d+d+ha d+ha byu sgyu
7b2(tsa): dgod pa'i rim pa ni//dbus dang rtsa sbugs drag po la//sa dang nA ga ra snying po sdus//gnyis pa'i 'khrul mig bcu gnyis la/rtsa ba'i snying po bcu gnyis dgod//phyi yi 'khrul m-ig rtsa bdun la//snying gzeng bdun gyi sku gri dgod//'bring nag dang dang
7b3(tsa): /thod pa bca'//sgom skul dong gzhug rtsa bcas sngags//dmod pa'i thun dbab hom shing bsreg/zor thun mtha' ru mnan gzir bya//rang yang phyag rgya gsal bar sgom//de yi gnod byed 'joms shing gzir//drag sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e

7b6(tsa): ri sa gso ba'o//de yi skor ba 'di lta ste//shog bu gru bzhi cha gnyis la//thig skor rim pa gsum du bskor//dbus su gsum snying po ming /zla gam ma mo btsan rgyal po//phyi rim 'phrul mig bcu gnyis la//rtsa ba'i drag snga Ta
7b7(tsa): 'bru bcu gnyis//tha ma rtsibs chen bcu drug la ka lho dkrug sbyor ba'i spu gri gnas//rdzas kyis byugs la thod par bca'//gru gsum rtsang bcas lag bdun bral//gsal gsum phyag rgya brtan pas gnan//drag sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e ka
8a1(tsa): gdum sagags cwa sa bya sa pa sa pa dum su spang
8a2(tsa): gdug pa gsum la sogs pa gzhom pa dang //byang dgrol sred dang snying gzer gdug pa'i las//rgyud kyi snying po spobs pa med pa 'di//bkra mi shis pa'i sa la maN+Dal bya//khrag dang dug chu tsha ba'i chag chag dag//dbus su gru gsum khru gang
8a3(tsa): nag por bya//zla kham gsum gyi mtha' ma mong bas bskor//de yi phyi rim 'phrul mig bcu gnyi ste/lcags khur bar nas rdo rje rgyang pas gtams//de yi phyi rim rtsibs chen nyi shu laM//gcod ba'i mtshon man oM phyi ru bstan//de'i phyiras/
8a4(tsa): thod pa'i phreng bas bskor//tha ma me ri raM dang ldan par bya/de bzhin zhing ras rgyu la gcod pa'i 'khor lo brikabru gsum khwa khyer mdzub gang mtshon chas bskor//dbusu gdug pa'i gzugs bstan sna tshogs gzhag/gang gnod ming dang snying pos chu sbyar//zla
8a5(tsa): /kham gsum la sdong po ming dang gzugs//'khrul mig drag po dmod pa sngags kyi lus//rtsibs le sreg cing gzir ba'i spu gri dgod//gzugs bstan snying khar hom shing zur gsum gzugs//zhag lnga thun bra ba drag sngags gtum par
8a6(tsa): bzlas//zhag gsum sreg cing gzugs ming gtub la bstab//sha chen rta sha spyang khu dar ma'i sha//dug chu 'o ma so na chang dang khrag/mchod pa phul la thun gyis brab//me dpal gsad la gnad kyi rdzas rnams bsreg/gang
8a7(tsa): dmigs bdug cing phying ba gtsang mas dgab//nyin dang mtshan mo lan grangs bdun du bdug/bum pas bkrus la lha'i sku ru bskyed//drag sngags gsal la mis ma thos pa bzlas//brnag pa thon nas na mkha'i 'khor lo dang //thal/
8b1(tsa): ba ming gzugs mchod rten gnod sar gnan//ljags rjes kun garang gnod zung du brdeg gsang sngags sbyor ba drug po 'dug ste/e ka ya ma kadun dzar dag ga du thag sa nA ga dung unan kAmed byung sa sgyun
8b2(tsa): phyogs sha rbad ram/de dag sbyor ba'i mtha' sdud yin//drag sngags mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud las//thun mong gi sbyor ba'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas yang bac+doMs kyis drag po mngon du phyung ba'i khyad par gyi sbyor ba 'di gsung so//'phrin
8b3(tsa): las drag po rtsa ba 'di//rtsa ba rnam pa gnyi su gnas//phan gnod las kyi rim pa bzhin du bkod//gsang ba mchog dang lo thog gzhom pa gnyis//snying gzer dgu dang nyi shu rdzogs pa yi//gsad pa mchog gi sbyor ba 'di lta ste//zhi khrog
8b4(tsa): rje phur pa ma mo dang //bdud dang mu stegs bye brang rgyal po btsan//gnod sbyin srin po lha min du dgro sri/klu gnyan sa bdag dkor srung grul bum ging //las dang srog gi snying po bcud du bsdus nas bral//shes rab rtsol dang brtul zhugs ldan
8b5(tsa): pas spyad//blo chung rtog pas rang yang 'ching bar 'gyur//nyams dga' rang sems dben pa'i dur khrod du//dpa' brtul drag po sngags kyi dmod pa bor//de yi sbyor ba rgyas pa 'di lta ste//shog bu gru bzhi mtho gang la//thig skor rim pa bzhi
8b6(tsa): dang ldan par bskor//dbusuM gru gsum bla sems ming dang rus//phyi rim gcod pa'i 'khor lo rtsibs bcu gnyis//de la rtsa ba'i snying po me rlung sbyin 'dang gnas//de yi phyi rim mu khyud zlum po la//bsgrub pa gzhan gyis zad dang gsad
8b7(tsa): pa'i sngags ka/yang logs mkhar gyi 'khrul mig bcu gnyis la/ral gri rdo rgya gram bsnol mar gnas//ral gri bsang pa'i spu gri gnon pas mdzes//de gnyis rus sbal khra mo'i lte ba br-ika kha sbyar bcas la dug dang khrag gis byug/
9a1(tsa): @#mtshon ma'i mtshams dang zla khams gru gsum la//brtag pa'i ming rus ya mA ra ya zad brig/ sngor mar dang bcas thod pa'i nang du gsug/pho thong nang du m+bA ra ya zad bri/thod pa 'og sar brtag pa'i ming dus b+h+yo/6
9a2(tsa): bcas gru gsum kha khyer sum brtseg gzhug/tshe bdag bsgoms la yis 'khor lo bsa'//zhag bdun drag sngags dmod pa rngam tshig ldan pas bzlas//bar bar bskul gyi ni brnag pa'i yul la zang //gzir zhing btab la thod pa 'an
9a3(tsa): klu gzir gdug pa'i gnyen la gtad par bya//drag sngags ramod pa rtsa ba'i spyod ba 'di//lha srin kun gyi mthu dril drag po yin//drag bsngags kyi ramod pa 'di lta ste//oM ka he dun ya ma'i snying khrag ra ma tsa ya/dgra bo srog la myogs
9a4(tsa): mA ra ya rbad//srog staM pa ya nan/srog la thun b+h+yo/'di dag mi 'gyur gsang po'i sbyor ba yin//de nas yang bac+doMldas kyis lo thog gzhom pa'i sbyor ba 'di gsung so//dur khrod ras sam shog bu gru bzhi la//dbusu rus sbal khong stong dbyings
9a5(tsa): dang ldum bur bri//yan lag lnga dang mjug ma lto ba na//gcod pa drag po 'khor lo rtsibs bcus//'byung bar bris la rtsa ba'i drag sngags dgod//de yi phyi rim 'khrul mig bcus la//srog bzhi gnad kyi 'bru dang chu dril dbab pa dgod//
9a6(tsa): sbal pa'i lto ba'i dbu su yul gyi ming dang ser ba phob ces bri//de yi 'og du yul lha ser srung gzir phob ya nan bri//byang khog stong na gza' yi gzir sngags bri/ka/mjug mar yul lha de bzhin gzir sngags bri//lto ba ga ya su ma mo'i gzir snga
9a7(tsa): driH lto ba g.yon dus bdag klu gnyan gyi//rang gnang gzir la dbab pa'i drag sngag s+thi/mtshon cha drug dang khrag gis ngang blud la//sbal ba pho mo lho sbyar thong pa'i nang du gzhug/dgun zla ga tsu kye nye stong nyin lag phrugs ri bzlas//dpyir zla gsum ni/
9b1(tsa): zhag gsum mu dril bzlas//dbyar zla gsum kha rten pa'i'i sbyor ba bas pa/thod pa dong bung khrag dang drug drug la//nya dang stong dang nyin zhag lnga ru bzlas//tha chungs stong la tam rten bzhing bcug /skye mes sgo mthon rgya mdud kha rjen tshul khror zab du /
9b2(tsa): drag po gzir dang bzhi ka tshil gsum blun dang brnol khrag du ba'i rdzas//dug chu sngon sna 'o ma drag po thab//nag gsuM khrag dang nyungs dkar thabs kyis rdzas//lUT dang seng ldeng phur pa gzir ba'i rdzas//drag snga Ta lus la phur pa dbab gzer bzhi//gza' dang ma mo yul/
9b3(tsa): gyi g+ha btsan dang /klu gnyan sa bdag gzir la dbab pa'o//ma phebs drag po sbyor ba stobs zhan na//rgya tshi la bsnol khrag khyi khrag ri bong khrag/dug rgod dri lhuT gdab pa'i phur pa la//dang po swaT bzlog drag tu spogs par bya//des ma phebs na drag sngags
9b4(tsa): 'byung ba dkrug/rdzas kun srogs la du bas lha 'dre dkrug/rgya mdud dbus kyi rta mas chu mig skrag/dri mo yugs mo brnol mo don mngal khrag/tsha'i rgyal po sbal ba'i gzugs la brdeg/drag snga Ta spyi dril sgo bzhi saur brdeg/spogs pa'i rgyal po gsang ba
9b5(tsa): pa yin//las kyi rgyal po spogs pa mchog du gsungs//de yis ma phrebs sbyor ba'i stobs zhan na//rtse gsum chu gsum tshal gsum tshwa ba gsum//mche ba can dang 'dab chags ltos gros gsum//rta gsum gzer gsum sngags gsum sde bas
9b6(tsa): la dbag//draT po swaT kyis sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e ka ya ma he dun snying khrag ra ma tsa ya//ma hA de ba ma ma rAg+na sa nA ga pr-i phreM hri la shar phob hA la phreM/ma hA yA mA ra hU la sogs la gzi rA nAnA b+h+r-i byung hril phob/de ba yu nan gzir nan phro shar hri la phog de ba yu
10a1(tsa): @#khrob ga gr-i pra ma swa ran sa ga gzing zhums nan/br-i hung gar srog tI la ga pog/ya tsa hi dun trag sha dgra mo tA su kha ma hung nas khaM shar br-i gzir dril pob//khragar snyings su gzing la de nan gang pog/e ka he dun brams
10a2(tsa): yang ma snying khrag ra ma tsa ya/phreM ya de ba ma rag sa na ga/ra hu la/sarba u nan yaM yaM phreM phreMng shar shar hri la nan gzir phob byo//gzir zhing mthu' dbang stobs bsus la//rnam grangs lnga dang ldan pa'i las//'di dag sbyor ba daM pa ste//lo theg 'joms
10a3(tsa): lal shis pa yin//de nas yang bac+doMs kyis gnod byed thaMd gzhom pa'i las snying gzer nyi shu rtsa gcig pa'i sbyor ba 'di gsungaso//mdze rims drag po'i gdug pa dang //ma mo rgyal po mdze 'dre can//snying gzer drag po'i stobs kyis gzir ka/
10a4(tsa): 'khor lo drag po bca' ba ni//rten pa shog bu gru bzhi la//cha gnyis dur khrod che bas byug/rus sbal lto bar thig skor bzhi ka bskor la dbusu gru gsum la//zla kham sbrul gyis ra bas bsdams//de yi phyi rim 'khrul mig bzhi//thod mkha
10a5(tsa): rdo rje ra ba me//phyi rim gnam lcags 'khrul mig brgyad//rlung ri rgya gram dbus na mdzes//yang logs rtsa sbugs kyi gcig/gdug pa sreg byed yis me//'od kyi gong bu gnas la 'pho//de la gnas shing dgod pa ni//gru gsum dbus na/
10a6(tsa): sa nA ga/gang gnod ming dang bcas bri//zla gam gsum la sdong ma'i mI//lcags mkhar brgyad thod mkhar bzhi//rtsa ba'i yig 'bru bcu gnyis dgod//rtsa sbugs nyi shu rtsa gcig la//snying gzer nyi shu rtsa gcig dgod//drag sngags 'khrul 'khor
10a7(tsa): mi dpal mtshon//'khor lo mtshon chen me dpal sngags//drag po sngags dgod gdug pa'i stobs//thod men dril phreng drag po 'i mtshon//namakha' rin chen rgya mtsho las//mtshon mkha sbyor dug chu dang /sngon dmar banas dang phag mo'i stor//
10b1(tsa): du khrag bgrus la thod par gzhugs gang zugs khri kha la 'ardeg gsal gsum dang ldan gsus stong 'phrul/nya stong sar gyi la sbyang /ba chung bzhi la rab tu legs/zhag gsum shis tu gnang sna bas /de cing do ba /
10b2(tsa): ga na na//mi ldog gyad dang dregs pa bsgom//thod mkhar gteng du gdug pa'i rtsa nga //nyi shu gcig dbus mchog phur bstan//gang gasnod gzugs snang snying khar brdeg/rgyun du drag sngags brgya brgyad bsrung ka/ a shrI bi lI sbyor tshul can//snying po pa
10b3(tsa): dang shugs kyis drang //dug lnga rkam chags rtsa ba gcod//ha la nag po zhen chags 'brel//sngags kyi rgyal po kun 'joms 'di//drag la 'dril zhing sgra spangs la//sgra ni gam zhing lci bar gnas//drag po gtum chen dregs pa yi//drag
10b4(tsa): drag mtshon cha 'di lta ste//e ka ya ma ka dun dzA raM rag sha ru thum/sa nA ga snying gzer dza//sa nA ga snying gzer dug phyung rbad//sa nA ga snying gzer tsit+ta rbad//sa nA ga snying gzer thun rbad//sa nA ga snying gzer du na raM rbad/sa nA ga snying
10b5(tsa): gzer yaM ra~M phun rbad/sa nA ga snying gzer raM phun rbad/sa nA ga snying gzer srog la rbad/sa nA ga snying gzer tsit+ta phyogs rbad/sa nA ga snying gzor thun ril zad/sa nA ga snying gzer tsa kha zad/sa nA ga snying gzer nan 'drid zad/sa nA ga s+yai
10b6(tsa): gzer breng shag rbad/sa nA ga snying gzer tsid+d+ha khas rbad/sa nA ga snying gzer khrag thibs zad/sa nA ga snying gzer sbur nan rbad /sa nA ga snying gzer hi nan zad/sa nA ga snying gzer u nan zad/sa nA ga snying gzer e nan cad/sa nAH
10b7(tsa): snying gzer srog la dun dun zad/sa nA ga snying gzer srog la dun dun rbad/sa nA ga snying gzer tsi d+ha ru zhums bshag zad/rigs bzhi rgyud la zhen pa dang //ba so kha 'khor gdug pa kun//skams chags las kyi dug zhugs na//drag po
11a1(tsa): @sngags kyis 'joms shing 'byin//gdug pa la sogs 'joms par bshad//de nas yang bac+doMs kyis snying gzer dgu ba'i sbyor ba 'di gsung so//snying po rtsa ba'i man ngag bcung //gas pa chen rtsa ba dril ba'i las//drug
11a2(tsa): po mngon phyung sbyor ba'i mchog/snying gzer gdug pa thaMd 'joms//shi pad sngags kyi 'khor lo yis//bdag skyobs gzhan bsrung gdug pa 'jomaso//ma mo mdud mo rgyal po btsan//klu gnyan sa bdag the rang dri//ma lus 'joms byed no//
11a3(tsa): kyis sngags//b+hi la'i khong dus khong dregs can//b+ha ra b+ha nag po ya ma'i klong //khyad par sbyor ba 'di lta ste//dur khrod ro ras zhing ras la//mu khyud lnga ldan rus sbal ltor//dug khrag sna tshogs sbyar te bri//gru gsum 'khrul
11a4(tsa): mig thod khar bzhir//de 'og lcags mkhar baM rim brgyad//yang logs gnas pa'i rtsa sbubs dgu//phyi rim rtsibs chen bcu drug 'obs//drag po sngags kyi 'khrul 'khor bca'//de la dgod cing gnas pa ni//dbusu gdug pa'i
11a5(tsa): snying po ming //zla gam ma mo rgyal po btsan//the brang gshin rdog snying po ming //thod khar 'dren pa'i stobs kyi 'bru//phyi rim lcags mkhar dregs pa brgyad//yang logs rtsa sbubs snying gzer dgu//phyi rim mtshon cha kun 'joms dgod//
11a6(tsa): kha sbyar skyor bkur ldan dang /gsal gsum lag bdun brub cing gzir//bzlog cing 'byin pa shugs kyis mchog/gcod cing 'byin pa spu gri mtshon//sreg cing g.yeng bsdu me chu rlung //bsdud cing 'chang bsna kyom+b+ha pa'i mkhang //gzing
11a7(tsa): zhing gnon pas thog chen la/rtsa bar bcas pa mtshan la me/rtsa ba gcig la yal ga stong //'bar ba nag po zhe sdang mchog/sngan kyi rgyal po dug dang bcasaka' bar ba dug gi du byu bsdus//bdud nag rag shA yo ma'i klong //e kA po prang
11b1(tsa): thong pa'i gnas//'khrul 'kho ba dang bcas re bas zil//chos khung bsdus la stong bzlas//gsal du khyab par stobs kyi sku//rdza sa dang nye ba skyong ba'i sdengs//'dun pa mchog ldan yin pa gtsu/dbang bcas bgrod ba go mtshon mchog rigs
11b2(tsa): du stong gzung ka nya stong sbyor ba drag po rtse ma// thun mongs bsrung ba sbyor ba brgya//mngon zhugs sbyor ba drag po nyid//gsad pa'i sbyor ba spu gri dang //drag po rtsa ba bcus bsdebs//dmigs pa'i ming rus khams sbyar la//gang gnod zhen pa stobs/
11b3(tsa): la stad//mkha 'gro gzas kha dgra la 'pho drag po thog gzer sbyar ba'i mchog/gser gi dgu thog gnam lcags rba bo//ri rgyal rbab chen spu gri me//drag sngags sbyor ba gnyi su bstan//e ka he du na ya ma rag sha dun dzA raM//sa nA ga ma trag
11b4(tsa): snying gzer rba da//sa nA ga snying gzer sbur nan rbad//sarba u nan khams shog gzer thum tsit+ta predz+ra sheg/sa nA ga du ma snying gzer zad//sa nA ga kle tri snying gzer rbad /sa nA ga dug phyung snying gzer rbad/sa nA ga khaM shag snying gzer zad/sa nA ga srog tig snying gzer
11b5(tsa): rbad sa nA ga bruM ril snying gzer zad/sa nA ga snying gzer sbur nan rbad/sarba u nan khaM shag gzer ma tsit+ta breng shag snying gzer sU nAn/e ka he dun ya ma snying khrag ra ma tsa ya/dgra bo'i srog la u nan/khaM shag gzer thun tsit+ta preng shag snying gzer sbur
11b6(tsa): nanang dgra bo'i srog la thun rmugs thibs spyo//snying gzer dgu pa'i mtshon cha yis//dmigs pa gang yin mi 'tsher gcod//brda' dang snying po 'pho zhing sbyor//Ta phrad drug pos gzir zhing gnan//sbyor ba gzhan tshogs ci rigs sbyar//drag po me dpal gcod/
11b7(tsa): ba'i mtshon/brnag pa'i ci b+ha'am si ra la//rang gcod sbyor ba'i rgyal po 'dis /kha don gnyis mngon du byed pa yin//drag sngags mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud las/khyad par sbyor ba'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o///de nas yang brjos kyis gsong ba'i sbyor ba 'di gsung so//
12a1(tsa): @drag po gsang ba'i sbyor ba la//rtsa ba gnyi su shes par bya//gsang pa dang ni bsrung ba'o//gsad pa'i sbyor ba rnam gnyis//bsam gtan rang rgyud lto sbyor gnyis//de bzhin gso ba bzhi gnyis ldan//gshig dang byang grol zug pa dbyung
12a2(tsa): zhen pa bzlog na gsang laM dag ga/dug mda' khong nas dbyung ba'o//gsad pa'i sbyor ba 'di lta ste//tsit+ta 'od kyi pho brang nas//dngas ma bzhi dang sir brgyad//mdud pa bzhi brgyad su la bzhir gnas//tsit+ta phyi nang nang khrol dang //phyi'i lhu tshigs sgro
12a3(tsa): dgu rus//dbang po sngags kyi spyod yul brtag/dngas ma bzhi yi sbu bas dag la//'byung bas bskyed pa'i yi gi gnas//mdud pa bzhi yis 'brel chen la//gcod pa spu gri rno dpal gnas//si ra phra mo brgyad sbubs na//khyad par sbyor ba'i
12a4(tsa): sngags brgyad gnas/mdud po brgyad dang bcus na//khrag dang bag chags yi gi gnas//nang khrol lnga dang sgo dgu na//zor thun skyod pa'i spu gri gnas//rtsa bzhi dngas ma'i mtshams bzhin//srog bzhi lo thog 'joms pa gnas//bsku la
12a5(tsa): gyin gcod pa rang gcod de//mi 'gyur ba yi sbyor ba yin//dbugs kyi rtag spyod yul la 'phang //bsrung ba laM sogs kun grangs na//tsit+ti gnas na dngas snyings 'don//dbang po sgog gi thig ler gnas//rten la thod par snying po sdu/drag
12a6(tsa): sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e ka he dun ya ma snying khrag ra ma tsa ya//dgra bo'i srog la nI dza hur thums shag rbad//dgra bo'i srog la ma ra ya khaM shag dza b+h+rU~M zad/dgra bo'i srog raM yai hUr stam pa ya nan/las 'di mi 'gyur bstan pas na//
12a7(tsa): bsam gau tan yis 'od 'bros pas//dmin pa gdug pa'i rgyud bskul nas//dman pa'i yi ge rtsa srog cod//dngas ma sdud nas dbang po 'pho//gnan pa na mkha'i khams dang sbyar//de nas brjes kyis drag po stobs kyi sbyor ba 'di gsung so//sngags
12b1(tsa): dang 'khor lo man dag rdebs pa'i las//dung khrod rgyu la rme bas thang ba bya//dbus nas drangs la thig skor lnga ru bskor//phyogs bzhi tshangs pa'i thig bzhi rgya gr-i gdag/dbusu gru gsum 'khrul mig bzhi dang mtshams//de yi phyi rim 'khrul mig ba ni/
12b2(tsa): dang mtshams//de yi phyi rim 'khrul mig bzhi gnyis ldan//lcags mkhar bar khyams gshed bzhi'i khang pa yin//de yi phyi rim mu khyud b+h+rUM bo la//stobs dang ldan pa'i sbyor ba 'joms pa'i gnas//yang logs chu yi khang pa mu khyud zlum po la/
12b3(tsa): khyam dang khang gnas sgrub pa'i snying po rdzogs//tha ma'i 'khrul mig drag po bcus la//mtshon cha'i rgyal po rlung ri sbyin thang gnas//myos pa stobs 'brel rno dpal kha sbyor yin//de la dgod cing gnas pa'i rim pa ni//dbus dang mtshams kyi gsang/
12b4(tsa): mdzod gru gsum lngar//bla sems chags pa'i snying po ming rus bri//lcags mkhar bzhi la 'dren pa'i 'bru bzhi dgod//de yi phyi rim lcags mkhar brgyad po la//rtsa ba'i drag sngags 'bru brgyad gnas pa dgod//de yang phyi rim mu khyud
12b5(tsa): zlum po la//tha~Md gzir dang gshig pa'i spu gri dgod//yang logs khang pa mu khyud zlum po le//gang bsgrub bsnyen skad gsad pa'i sngags rnams dgod//tha ma drag po 'khrul mig nyi shu la//dgug dang thun brdeg gisang dang gnan
12b6(tsa): ba'o//drag po skabs dang sbyar ba'i spu gri drag po dgodaka zhing bcu ma lus gtan nas sreg cing 'pho//'phrul mig nyi shu rtsa gnyis mthar//drag po khyams khang mchog gi gnas//de yis thad 'joms shing 'pho//'khor lo
12b7(tsa): lto sdigs me dang rlung //spu gri mtshon chen gcod dang 'bigs//bsrung dang skyobs pa kun byed pa'o//ngar skyobs ldan pa'i 'khor lo la//dug khrag ngar blud kha sbyor bca'//ga'u dang bcas drag po'i gnas//'khrul 'khor drag blo/
13a1(tsa): gsang ba'i stobs//stobs chen tshe bdag cit+ta la//snying mo dngas ma sgra ru gnas//rtsa ba'i drag sngags skabs dang sbyar//phyi rim gang bsgrub bsnyen dang spang //gsad pa la sogs skabs kyi sbyar//'od kyi gong bu/
13a2(tsa): bsal bar bsgom//man ngag gsang ba thugs kyi sdebs//zhal gyi rmod pa bzlas pa'i tshul//e nas drangs la spu grir bsgyur//gang bsgrub bsnyen pa sbyar lo dgug/spyan drangs la sogs gzhung dang sbyar//rbad pa de bzhin stengs
13a3(tsa): du bsdeg//de bzhin drag po thugs ka la//spu gri dang bcas snga ma bzhin//'dod pa'i ming bcas mtshamasu bsdebs//rno dpal spu gri khaM sbyor yin//dmod pa ngag gi sngags rnams bsdebs//gnan la sogs gnyan sa ru//las
13a4(tsa): tshul bzhin dang 'dra bar brtag/sngags rnams gang 'dod skabs dang sbyar//man ngag ltor bsde ba drag po yin//de srung bac+doMs kyis bsrung ba thad kyi mchog tu gshig pa'i sbyor ba 'di gsungaso//drag po mngon phyung sbyor ba'i mchog
13a5(tsa): gas nga chen rtsa bar dril ba'i las//dmod pa mtshon cha 'di lta ste//dur khrod zhing ras shog bu la//mu khyud rim pa bzhi dang ldan//rlung ri las kyi 'khrul mig la//dbusuM lcags ma khar 'khrul mig bzhi//mtshams bzhi'i
13a6(tsa): rdo rje'iri ka bar ldan//phyi ris lcags mkhar 'khrul mig brgyad//zhing gi ka bas mtshams brgyad 'debs//yang logs mtshan cha rtsibs bcu gnyis//las kyi mi ri 'bar ba'i tshul//mtshon cha khang pa de dag la//
13a7(tsa): gnas shing dgod pa'i rim pa ni//dbus nas brnag pa'i snying po ming //zla khams de bzhin rdzogs par brika/lcags ma khar bzhi yi khang pa ru//srog dang las sdud 'gyed dang bdud//'bru bzhi'i sbyor ba kun gyis
13b1(tsa): mchog/khang ma khyud lcags mkhar brgyad po la//gshin rjes mo'i snying po gnyis//mkha' lding dregs pa'i snying po gnyis/sreg byed ma dang sdud byed gnyis//rus sbal ya mi'i snying po gnyis//ljags mkhar bang
13b2(tsa): rim re la dgod//mtshon cha'i khang pa bcu gnyis//dug lnga'i sbyor ba gshegs pa yi//drag po spu gri mtshon cha dgod//me la snying po spor ba dgod//'khrul 'khor mtshon ma me dpal sngags//sbyor ba'i rgyal po kun 'jom
13b3(tsa): 'di rnga me rlung las kyi mtshon cha dgod//rdzas rnams bskus la kha sbyar dril//dung mar bcug la re bas dral//rtsa nga bcas gru gsum skyer ba'i stan//zhing lpags steng nas drag pos gdab//lha bsgom sngags bzlas thun bra ba brdeg/drag
13b4(tsa): tu bskul gyi na yul la rbad//bdun nam lnga ru nya stong tsam//sbyor ba thon nas 'khrul 'khor dril//tshes gcig ngag dang bcu drug la//spobs pa med par gnan par bya//zhi khro phur pa gshin rje rnams//'khor lo rus sbal thod chen
13b5(tsa): gzhug/dkyor chen po'i 'og tu gnan//mkha' lding dregs pa klu rgyal rnams//'khor lo tshe bdag lto bar bzhugs//'brong chen thod pa ro khung gnan//ma mo bdud mo srin mo rnams//'khrul 'khor nyan thos thod par bzhug/
13b6(tsa): bka' rten byin gyin 'og tu gnan//bdud dang mu stegs mgon po rnams//'khrul 'khor g.yag ru g.yas par bzhug/ro khang sgo srung 'og tu gnan//rgyal po dge bsnyen bskor srung rnams//'khrul 'khor skyang khu'i thod par
13b7(tsa): bzhug/khyi tshang lam gyi bzhi mdor bstan//btsan dang srog dag dri ro rnams//shar du sbyang thod gong gsum gnan//klu bdud dam bdud phugs bdag rnams//bon po'i thod par lha khang gnan//the rang chagaso 'gong gshin 'dod
14a1(tsa): @#gnams//glang ru bcug la lam mngar gnan/sa bdag klu gtan mdze 'a dre rnams//'khor lo phag mo'i thod par bzhugs/bag rgod bkar brten lag tu gan//yon po rigs gnyis 'dzin pa rnams//khyi /
14a2(tsa): bcug la khyi tshang gnan//sri gnan chung thod them 'og gnan//rigs 'dzin sbyor ba bdag dbang bsdu//sa yi sbyor ba gnan pa'o//drag sngags me rlung mtshon cha'o//sarba sha tru na hur thum yaM shag nan gzir zad b+h+yo//bar snang na mkha' mchod
14a3(tsa): rten brten//'khrul 'khor me rlung kun 'joms yin//de nas bac+doMs kyis byad dgrol ba'i sbyor ba 'di gsu so//drag po byang dgrol gsum dang ldan//drag po rnams la btsan thabs dgrol ba'i las//drag po byad dgrol las kyi rim pa 'di//
14a4(tsa): drag po hom gyi 'khor lo maN+Dal sa la bya//'byung ba rten gyi 'khor lo bar snang rten la bca'//bcod pa sngags kyi 'khor lo zhing ras rgyu la bca'//maN+Dal gru bzhi khrag gis mthas pas byug/dbusu gru gsum khru gang 'dom re la//kha
14a5(tsa): khyer gsum las rtsang bcas zla gam thod pas bskor//de yi phyi rim 'khor lo rtsibs bcu gnyis//tha ma thod phreng me ris de bzhin bskor//dbus kyi gru gsum rgya snyam snying po raM//kha khyer gsum la gzugs dang me ri rtsa nga //gang gnod
14a6(tsa): grogs dang bcas pa rdzogs par bri/'khor lo drag sngags bris la phur pa dgod//gru chad bzhi la drag po'i rdzas rnams dgod//hom shing btsugs la 'thing nag dar gyis gdab//drag po byad dgrol sa'i 'khor lo yin//'byung ba stobs
14a7(tsa): kyi 'khor lo bcar ba ni//rgya shog rgyu la rme ba'i rdzas rnams bsku//thig bskor gsum ldan su khrung thum por bya//dbus rgya gri 'khrul mig bcu gnyis ldan//yang logs brgyad gnyis rtsibs dang ldan//tha ma gru gsum dbyings la gdug pa'i ra/
14b1(tsa): sa bsdam//de la dgod cing gnas pa'i rim pa ni//rgya gram dbus su gnad ming gzugs raM/rgya gr-i ra bzhir yang dang de bzhin ming //'khrul mig bcu gnyis rtsa ba drag po sngags//bcu drug rtsibs la byang dgrol sgra gri mtshon//thig gnyis
14b2(tsa): bar du me rlung a mu ka/kha sbyor bcas la bar snang mkha' la brdeg/lnga ru gzir la me sbar thun gyi gzir//myos pas bran la rdzas bsreg dmigs pa bsgyur//gtsang mas bka' la sngags kyi thun gyis gzir//drag po khyad par byad dgrol gsang
14b3(tsa): pa'o/Ta rnal ma bkrol na du ba phying ba sngags kyis brib//thun brab bskus la cha lugs brgyan cig gsal//btsan thabs drag po a yi ga'u ru//drag po rdzas sreg brag cing bdugs la bsku//phyag rgya bstan par bskyed la kun 'joms drag po brdeg/
14b4(tsa): /las kyi rgyal po byad dgrol sngags kyi rgya//drag sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e ka ya ma ka dun dza raM sad kar ya/sarba u nan gzer phyung du r-i ti tsa sUr nan srog la hur yaM sha ga/ya ma ta ra tsa sa nA ga k+ki tri sarba du raM yaM bruM shag phyung /de nas yang bac+doMs
14b5(tsa): kyis zug par byung bu'i sbyor ba 'di gsung so//dur khrod dngal 'khor lo drag po bris//thig bskor rim pa gsum du bskor//dbus su gnod byed ming dang gzugs//'khrul mig bcu gnyis drag po rtsa ba'i sngags//rtsibs chen bcu gsum spu gri drag po dgod//
14b6(tsa): ngar blung kha sbyor thong ba dong du bca'//drag snga bsdeb su gsal ba gsum//gang dmigs lus dang gnas khang bskor la zla pa//zong khrag phun thim gang dmigs thor du 'phang //mtha' ru 'khor lo smyub khang steng du bcar//tha chung mar po drag po las re bca sa// rA
14b7(tsa): sngags dang gang 'dod spu grir sbyar//sngags kyi rgyal go gsad pa 'di lta ste//e ka ya ma ka run dza sang ka ra ya/sarba dur dri skye 'dring phyung sogs la gzi nan phyung ga/zug pa ma lus 'byin pa'i sbyor ba yin//de nas yang brjes kyis zhen pa zlog pa'i sbyor ba 'di/
16a1(tsa): @#/drag tu gzing zas gdug rtsub 'byin//srog dbugs bsdud cing drag por gnon//rang dam srog nas gang dmigs 'dren//nus pa 'brel zhing sbyor ba gcod//deng bzlog stobs dang mu gri'i gnas//mtshon ma'i nus pa stobs dpag med//
16a2(tsa): gsang mdzes lnga la dmigs pa'i ming //bsdus la kha sbyar bzhi yis bdeg/dben gnas gsum du gzir zhing bskul//drag po gnan pa bar snang sbyar//drag sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e ka ya ma he rag sha dun sad ka ra ya/sarba i nan 'drid phyung
16a3(tsa): tsicitsit+ta khaM shag srog gzir skur na na rbad ya/drag po bsrung ba kun gyi mchog/sbyor ba thaMd 'dril zhing 'grub//de nas yang bac+doMs kyi las tha~Md kyi mthar rjes gcod kyi sbyin sreg 'di gsung so//drag po'i sbyor ba sregs pa'i las//gang gnod sa la
16a4(tsa): maN+Dal khrag gis byug/dbusu gru gsum kha khyer gsum dang ldan//phyi nang chags gang 'dzub gang de bzhin 'phags//'phrul mig bcu gnyis rtsibs chen nyi shu gnyis//mu khyud zlum por ban d+ha lnga gnyis gcig/tha ma me ri raM dang bcas pa'o//de
16a5(tsa): la dgod cing gnas pa'i rim pa ni//dbusu gang dmigs gzugs la khru gang ho shing gzugs//kha khyer zla kham gzugs brnyan me ri gtsang //'khrul mig bcu gnyis drag po rtsa ba'i sngags//yang logs mtshon cha spu gri sreT pa'i sngags///
16a6(tsa): mu khyud b+han d+ha dang sbal sbrul nywa yi sha/sha chen tsha ba dug gi ri lu brgya//brun gsum tshil gsum dug chu 'o ma khrag/sngo sna chang dang mar nag bdug rdzas dgod//zhing ras rgyu la gong bzhin bris la bskul//bdun nam dgu ru mod sngags zla
16a7(tsa): shing bskul//pha nyas mtshon brdeg drag po'i las la bstab//bskul dang drag sngags bcu yis bsreg rdzas bstab cing dbul//gang dmigs gdug la gtsang mas khrus dag pa//the na rjes shul du banag pa'i zer dun dbab//'khor lo thod pa se rkor
16b1(tsa): khog par gnas//phyag rgya chen po lus dbang bsrung ba'i mchog/drag sngags bsreg pa'i sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e ka he dun ya ma rag sad ka rA ya/sa nA ga du raM tri tsa du me u nan/gzer byung /srog la hu ra/tsit+ta breng shag raM/phyogs thugs/
16b2(tsa): rbad bag med sbyor ba kun 'joms yin//drag sngags yan lag du ma kun//sbyor ba gang brtsams gcig gis 'grub//sbyor ba gang brtsams na nye//yis thig le 'od dang sbyar//drag sngags mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud las//gsang ba'i sbyor ba'i len
16b3(tsa): ste lnga pa'o////de nas yang bac+doMs kyis yang gsang mchog gi sbyor ba 'di gsung so//sbyor ba'i rtsa ba'i rnam pa gnyis//gsang ba mchog dang gso ba'i las//gsad pa'i las ni bzhi gnyis ldan//de bzhin gso ba bzhi ru bkod//khyad par bcu gnyis
16b4(tsa): sbyor ba'i mchog/las gsang thabs gsang bgrod pa thob//gsad pa sngon 'gro 'di lta ste//byin chen bde ba'i gnas mchog tu//gsad pa mchog gi sbyor ba tsa ma//b+hi la'i khong 'dus khong dreg can//hU la nag po ts+tsha mU ri//be g+ha'i 'jig rten na//ya ma/
16b5(tsa): rag sha bde ba'i klong //sgyu ma'i phung po gnas sbyangs la//rin po che yi rgyal sar 'don//thugs kyi snying po gnyis med 'phang ka gzhan tshogs lhar skyed tshogs la rol//bdag dbang bde ba thob cing bgrod//de yi sbyor ba 'di lta ste//'dul khrims
16b6(tsa): nyams pa'i gos blangs la//rdo phye lcags phye zangs phye dug chu bsku//cha gnyis byas la thig gdab brtsa ma//zil gnon phyag rgyas dbang gzugs brjid//drag sngags rmod pa stong dang sbyar//tha chung bzhi yi mar ngo la//nyi shu drug nas 'khrul 'khor//
16b7(tsa): bcar//dbus nas thig bskor bzhi ru bskor//dbus kyi pho brang bde gnasu//sku yi gzugs brnyan bris la ming //zla kham gsum la bsrung ba'i lha//phyi rim bcus rtsa ba'i sngags//yang logs 'khor yug bod nad gsang/ka/bri dang phyi nang gong ma bzhin
17a1(tsa): @bla ma'i lakhrul mig bcu drug la//gsod cing gnon pa'i spu gri dgod//sring ba rus sbrul lton gnas/drug dang khrag gi bang thung la//dre ba 'am yul srungs kyi srungs long gzug rtsang bcas dong du 'jing nag dag pa mi dag+sa
17a2(tsa): ngang las yis kyi//rol pa'i phyag rgya chen por 'bo//drag tu bskul gyi na shug kyis drang //mkhara' khyab rdo rje'i sbyor ba bsdeg//rmod pa'i lus la spu gri gdags//ming rus mi sbyar khams la sbyang //rgya mdu du brtsegs pa'i lho ru gnan//yang na b+hi Sh+Tha
17a3(tsa): chen po yi//'og tu bka' yis byin bsdus la//rin po che'i lho ru gnan//bshags dang dge ba rgyud mi bcad ka mtshan bzang dus drug rgyun mi bcad//'byung ba lnga'i mdangs bsdu do la//rin po che'i rgyal sar brtan//rgyu yi snyigs ma'i
17a4(tsa): srog bsdus la//dbus kyi 'bub gnas gzugs la dpag/sa dang me dang chu dang rlung ka/na mkha'i dbyings kyi khongasu bzhug/dmigs pa'i dag pa'i tshad mas gdon//rig pa'i drag sngags 'di lta ste//e ka he du na ya ma snying khrag ma ra tsa/
17a5(tsa): ya 'gu ni srog la myogs thum ril mA ra ya rbad//srog snyam pa ya gzir nan//srog la thum ri l-i thun myogs b+h+yo//bgrod pa med pa'i phyag rgyur bstan//seng+ge khog bshig sbyor ba'i mchog/gzhan yang drag po'i sbyor ba ni//dur khrod gan
17a6(tsa): nas dpa' bo yi//zhing sogs ras dang rgyu blangs la//sbyor ba gsum dang ldan par bya//de la gcod pa'i 'khor lo bri//dbus nas thig bskor lnga ru bskor//dbus dang 'khrul 'khor lcags mkhar bzhi//mtshams bzhir zhing gi ka ba/
17a7(tsa): bzhin yang logs mig brkyang mtshams brgyad sbrul//bzhi pas 'khor yug bsrung ba'i gnas//tha ma 'khrul mig nyi shu la//bcod cing gnon pa'i yi ge gnas//dbus dang zla kham bsgrub bya'i gnas//bcus 'khrul mig rtsa ba'i sngags
17b1(tsa): bar du zad sngags gsang sngags mthar//phyi ru mgo bstan brgya brgya bskul/ka/bsnyen pa khri yi bos la gzir//zhing bcu sdig can sreg cing 'joms//'khrul mig rtsa ba nyi shu gnyis//gsang mdzod sun khung gnam lcags 'bar//rul
17b2(tsa): sbal lto na mtshon cha me//'od kyi gong bus 'khrul cing gsod//drag po sngags kyi gcod pa'o//e ka he du na ya ma tsa ya/nri srog la dun tshugs sa ma ya rbad//srog zir snyam pa ya nan/srog la baM r-i la thun b+h+yoka snga Ta kyi 'od dang 'bar gi
17b3(tsa): me gdug pas rang gshed rang la 'bebs//mtshon cha ngar blud me la bdug/khri bzhis sbyor ba dri la 'phang //tho na nas stag gi 'dzum la gnan//drag po rdo rme thabs yin//gzhan yang gsad pa drag po las//shog bu gru bzhi 'dzub/
17b4(tsa): gang la//rme ba lnga yis thar pa byaka ni rang su yi skya bcad la//thig bskor mu khyud rim pa bzhi//gru gsum ming rus zla kham lhaka/'khrul mig bcus rtsa ba'i sngags//mu khyud zlum por bor rba da gsadaka rtsibs chen bcus gcon
17b5(tsa): cing gnon/myos pa stobs dang bral ba yi//drag po stobs kyi 'khrul 'khor yin//don ldan sbyor ba sngags pa 'joms//rab chad nag po'i thod par bzhug/mun pa'i gling du gnan pa byu//drag po mtshon cha 'di lta ste//e ka/
17b6(tsa): he ma ya dun tsa ya khrag ma snying ra dgra bo'i srog mA ra ya rba da//dgra bo'i srog snyam pa nan//dgra bo'i srog la thun rbad dmugs b+h+yo//mtshon cha'i rgyal po 'khor lo can//gcod pa ki ba spu gri'i tshul//gzir stobs ldan pas bsam
17b7(tsa): dpag dka ra//'jom pa'i stag mo kha 'thabs yin//gzhan yang drag po'i sbyor ba nyid/rgya shog gru bzhi cha gnyis la//rme ba lnga dang tsha ba bsu/dbus nas su khyud bzhi ru bskor//rus sbal khri bo'i lho ru brika dbu su 'phrul mig
18a1(tsa): @bcu gnyis la//ming rus rtsa ba'i drag bsngags dgod//yang logs bcu drug spu gri dgod//kha sbyor thod pa rtsang bcas rong /ka/lha sgom sngags bzlas drag tu bskul//bdun du brtsams la thod ba bcal//mu khyud bo cad
18a2(tsa): gzhan sngags bskul//khri stong brgyu bar rang ba shed ba ba//mtha' ru srin mo'i sgom su gnan//drug sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste/e ka he ya ma dun tsa ya khrag ram snying //dgra bo'i srog tung thams mA ra ya rbad //srog staM pa ya nan//sra
18a3(tsa): la thun zad rmugs b+h+yoka drag po seng+ga'i 'chi sbyor yin//gzhan yang gsod pa drag po'i las//rigs min gzhan gyi sbyor ba gzhom//pho nyo gzir zhing slar bzlog pa//drag sngags nus pas gzhan dbang sdud//mu stegs can gyi lus srog wal
18a4(tsa): mo ral pa gsig pa yi//zhab ma sa 'khor lo rgyu byas la//sa dkar cung zi rus pa bska//dbus nas thig bskor lnga ru bskor//dbu su ming rus zla kha me lha//mu khyung gdug pa 'khrul mig sngags//'jug nas zlog la spu gri sbyar//phyi rim
18a5(tsa): rdo rgya gram la/g.yas g.yon mgo phyogs mjug ma la//'phen pa stobs kyi sngags bzhi dgod//rang sngags ming bcas bskor ba bri/ka/phyi rim rtsibs chen bcus la//gcod cing gnon pa'i spu gri dgod//dag dig glan glen thod par gzhug/
18a6(tsa): sbyor ba kun ldan bdag dbang sdud//sngags pa stobs kyi 'og tu gnan//drag sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste//ya tsa ma ra khrag snying ya ma du na he ka o//srog la dun rmugs b+h+yo//srog la kham shig b+h+yo/srog la breng shig b+h+yo/srog la yaM shig b+h+yo//
18a7(tsa): dgra bo'i srog la gzir khaM hur thum ya ma shig mA ra ya zad/srog snyam pa ya nan/pho nya rang gshid dbab pa yis//drag po las kyi sbyor ba yin//gzhan yang gsad pa'i rim pa ni//ni rang lpags shun khru gang la//sbyor ba'i rgyal po gsuM sbyor lta
18b1(tsa): mdog dang ldan par skya gcang bya//zhing gi srad bus thig gdab brtsam//dbus nas thig skor lnga ru bskor//thig mdud rgya gras 'khrul mig bzhika/phyogs bzhir 'khrul mig bcu drug gcod//mtshams gzhir gsang mdzod drag po'i gnas//
18b2(tsa): yang logs mtshon ma nyi shu bcod//'khrul mig bcus rtsa ba'i sngags//phyi rim 'khrul mig bzhi bcu gnyis//lcags mkhar 'khrul mig brgya bcu drug/gsang mdzod mtshams bzhi dbus dang lnga//dbus mtshams bsgrubs bya tshe srog
18b3(tsa): bsdud//'khrul mig bcu drug spu gri mthun//yang logs bcus rtsa ba'i sngags//'khrul mig bzhi bcu dgab pa'i sngags//gcod cing 'joms pa'i spu gri ldan//lnga pa mtha'i bzhi bcu la//thun bzhi dbang sdud gsad pa'i gnas//drag sngags
18b4(tsa): rgyal po skabs dang sbyar//kha sbyar sngags pa tsa lo'i mgor//rtsa nga bcas lha sgom dmod pa bzlas//ben nas ru bzhi 'khyil bar gnan//drag sngags glanam lcags sbyor ba yin//de nas yang bac+doMs kyis rang la dbab pa'i sbyor ba 'di gsung /
18b5(tsa): yang gsang 'phrul gyi snying po mchog/bde ba gnas kyis mchog ldan du//'dus byas la sogs dge sogs brtsams//sngon du 'gro ba'i chos de bzhin//phyag rgya chen po bstan pa yin//snying po dang bcas gsal brtags la//rdo chu bo'i bka'
18b6(tsa): mi bcag/rgyu la tsha bas kha dog dbyung //tshangs pa'i thig gnyis rgya gram gdab//ru thig gnyis te re mig skyong //thig bskor mu khyud lnga dang ldan//dbusu rtsa ba'i 'khrul mig gsum gsum drang //mtshams kyi gsang mdzod mun khang /
18b7(tsa): bzhin gsum pa las kyi rdo la//rgya gras sbyor ba bzhi dang ldan//bzhi pa mtshon cha bdun gnyis rtsibs//lnga pa rigs bzhi ra bas bskor//'khor lo dam pa kun dril ba'o//de la dgod pa'i 'bru rnams ni//dbu su gu ru phyag rgya'i sku/brjid pa'i tshul gyis kyi/
19a1(tsa): @#grangs bzhag/gzhan las spyi gtsugs gcer bu ring //zla kham ming rus gsang mdzod gzhi//zla sems 'chang ba gshed kyi mdzod/phyogs bzhi 'khrul mig bcus la/sa ge phyogs me rlung nam kha'i 'bru//mjug par/
19a2(tsa): rgya byung bdud mo dregs//gal su bdud dang gshin rje khrung //g.yon du las byed ma mo srin//'bru gsum sbyor ba bcus lus//g.yas g.yon rnal ma de bzhin bzlog/rgyu glam bzhi yi snying po la/'og phyogs sreg dbrog zad 'gug sa/sa/
19a3(tsa): mA lha dang lus srog 'byed//g.ya su mkha 'gro gzan la 'bebs//g.yon gyi rwa la gzir ba'i sngags//bod skad gsad dang de bzhin sbyar//sgrub dang thun mong go rims sbyar//rtsibs la lus sog gcod pa yi//yang gisang sU gr-i/
19a4(tsa): bcus sa log/thad sbrul gyi ra bas bskor//kha sbyar dur ma dong dang rtsang //rten bzhi khyu mchog rwa dang lnga//dang po srog dbrog bsnyen pas dgug/gnyis pas gzir zhing rang dbang bsdu//nub gsum pa la zad cing bzlog/bzhi pas rang
19a5(tsa): gshed bdud du dbab//lnga pas rtsa sogs gcad cing dbye//gcig gis gzir la rten kun bca'//khri yis klu gzir 'khyil par brtag/rkang 'dom phyogs bzhir thod mkhar gdab//dbu su khyu mchog khru dor gnan//drag sngags khri
19a6(tsa): tshor rngam par bzlas//drag sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e ka ya ma he dun snying khrag ra ma tsa ya/ya ma ta ri dun dun sarba tsit+ta breng shag ka dun rag shag/e ya ma srog la dun dun dza dza//e dza ram ya ma tsit+ta breng sha ga pra be sha ya
19a7(tsa): srog la bruM ri la ya ma tsit+ta dgra bo'i srog la dun rmugs thun b+h+yo//sarba tsit+ta ru b+h+r-i s+phur nan gzer thu tri ga nan/kha~M shag thun b+h+yo/dgra bo'i srog la breng breng tsit+ta dun rmugs yaM shag mA ra ya rbad//gnan pa mi shig staM pa'i mchog/ru bzhi rdo rje'i/
19b1(tsa): gya gr-i yin//de nas yang brjos kyis gso ba'i sbyor ba 'di gsung so//de dag sbyor de bzhi yin gcig/drag po las kyi mtha' rgya ru//mchod rten bka' la srung ba dang //gnod byed ma lus dbye ba'i phyir//drag po'i snying gzer ba cig mngags//
19b2(tsa): de dag bstan par bya ba'i phyir//Shashi phreng drag po kun 'joms mngags//bsrung ba kun gyi rgyal po ru//phyag rgya bsam gtun mchog tu gsungs//mthar du rjes gcod sbyin sreg pa'o//de dag rgyas pa 'di lta ste//dung khrongs la sogs pa la/rdzas
19b3(tsa): bzlog nag po'i bka' rten bca'//gdan khri glang po'i sbrul gyi brdar//bang rim bzhi la stag dang gzigs//dom dang seng+ges 'degs cing rngam//bum pa drag po rtsibs bcus//'khor lo mtshon cha 'brug gi 'degs//char kheg bya khyung gdug
19b4(tsa): pab za dreg pa thaMd zil gyi mnon//de la dgod cing gnas pa ni//'khor lo e lteng rdzogs pa'i 'bru//bum pa'i rtsibs la rtsa ba'i sngags//bang rim dang po gab pa yi//gsad pa gshig pa'i sngags kyis mdzes//gnyis par rgyan gyi yig
19b5(tsa): 'brus mdzes//gsum par mgo dgu'i drag sngags dgod//bzhi par dbang sdud sngags dang ldan//dmigs pa'i snying po brda' ming sbyar//khams dang bcas la de bzhin sbyar//gdan khrir thod pa mnag pa bsdu//yang gsang gzir dang snying gzir
19b6(tsa): dgod//snying po 'bru re ming rang bcas//rgya shog gro ga dug khrag gzhug/skabs dang sbyar la las kun bya//stobs chen rdegasu pa dang por gnan//bdud dang rgyal po bar du gnanaka/mi rigs gdan khri thod par gnan//snying po 'bru re mi srogs
19b7(tsa): kun na gro gi yangal khrag sug gis brika brgya brgya skabs deng sbyar ba las kun brtsam//brgya brgya mtshon mi bsnol mar brdeg/srog dang bcas lha ru brtsig/bskor zhing bsrung dang gsang pa'i las la shis//gzhan yang rdzas log dag ngas pa
20a1(tsa): @dur khrod 'phungs sa g.yul bkyes//dug chu sngol khrag gdug pa'i sha/tsha ba sme ba ldan byas la//khru gang rus sbrul lu srin 'brug/sbrul dang phag mo'i sdig pas 'degs/bus pa mtshon cha drug gnyis rtsibs//drag
20a2(tsa): po nang du e yi dbyings//'khor lo bdun brtsegs dregs pas 'degs//char kheb gong bzhin gdug pa za//dug chu thal chen sa dkar gyis//mdog dang ldan byas drag sngags dgod//bum par tshe bdag yab yum gsal//phyogs bzhir bla
20a3(tsa): 'gugs mon pa bzhi//khri 'phang 'chi bdag bdud kyi tshogs//gdan khri 'og tu brnags pa bsdu//maN+Dal dbusu zhag lnga bzlas//bskor ba rnal dang phying ke log/bsrung dang gsad pa'i skabasu bya//mchod rten snang stong yis sku//gsal bar
20a4(tsa): byas la rtag tu gzung //sbyor ba kun gyi mchog tu gsungs//sngags kyi sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e ka he dun ya ma snying khrag ra ma tsa ya//dgra bo'i srog la hur thums dza//tsa khrag snying thun ril rbad//sarba tsit+ta gzer bruM trig nan/de nas bac+doMldas kyis
20a5(tsa): gsad pa mchog gi las 'di gsungaso//snang stong gnyis med yis sku//rig pa thabs kyis grol ba'i mchog/phyag rgya chen po stobs dpag med//bde ba'i lam la bgrod pa'i phyir//gdug byed phag mo'i lto ba ru//gsad pa drag po'i mtshon cha
20a6(tsa): bca'//'khrul mig bcus yang logs rtsi bas//tha ma gdug pa'i ra bas bskor//de la dgod pa'i rim pa ni//gru gsuM ming rus zla kham lha//'phrul mig sngags dang spu gri rtsibs//dug chu tsha ba myos pa'i khrag/kha sbyar thod pa dong rtsa nga mthing //zhag
20a7(tsa): bdun gsad pa gzir ba phyed//thong pa'i ling ka 'khor lo chas/klu gziras sros 'byung ba bshag/zod mkhar dang bcas pho brang gangab//brgya rtsa brgyad du gnan sngags bzlas//e ka he dun ya ma snying khrag ra ma tsa ya dgra bo'i srog la hur long
20b1(tsa): zang breng shag phyogs zad /snum pa ya nan/thun mugs pa rim b+h+yo//gsang ba mchog gi sbyor ba gsungs//de nas yang bac+doMs kyis lo thog bsrung ba'i sbyor ba 'di gsung so//yul khams snang ba'i bcud bsrung na//seng rjeng sor brgyad zur pa la l+yAH/
20b2(tsa): zhogs brgyad la sngags mtshan tshe//rtsa la mi 'gyur bcus dgod//rtse mo lo gas gsum spu gri re//bris la zhag bdun dar dril bzlas//mtha' ru zhag gsum zlas la gdab/yul dbus phyogs bzhi ri tse la//mchod rten re yi lho ru swa//rgyal de/
20b3(tsa): phur pa rta pa la//dbyar zla 'bring po'i de bzhin sbyar//sku re byin gyis de bzhin brlab//ston zla tha chung ston la sdu/gdong bzlog rang mda' rang la 'phad ka lha gsal sngags bzlas drag la 'dril//e ka he dun snying khrag ra ma tsa ya/sar bsha
20b4(tsa): hUM pa yA nAM sha ya phaT/las rnams kun gyis mchog bsdus pa//yang gsad sbyor ba'i mchog tu gsal//gzhan yang srung ba'i mchog tu ni klu gzhan sa bdag gdug pa yi//ya nga ba'i nad dar na//rgya shog rgyu las lto sbyar bri/ka/dbus nas thig skor
20b5(tsa): yis bzhi//bdud bzhi dug rlangs bzhi 'joms skor//dbusu drag po'i bub gnas bzhi//ma mo rgyal po mdze 'dre btsan//rtse yi gsang mdzod gsum la gnas//sa dang na ga dbu su sdug phyogs bzhir rtsa ba'i 'khrul mig ni//lcags mkhar gsum
20b6(tsa): gsum rgya gr-i la//rtsa ba drag po lus dang ldan//mtshams kyi gsang mdzod mun khang bzhir//gdug pa gsum gyi gzugs bris la//lus srog dbrog pa'i 'bru dgu dgod//mu khyud gsum par gdug pa'i gzugs//tha ma phyi yi mu khyud la//'khrul/
20b7(tsa): mig rtsa sbubs bcug nas//dmigs pas bkug cing mtsham su gzir//gnyis 'joms drag sngags bcu gnas//rus sbal rtsa yi 'khor lo ni//lhon 'phel 'grib sna tshogs 'byung //spu gri mtsham su 'dod pa bsgyur//gcod pa bcig gsang la
21a1(tsa): @#la sbyar/drag po las la mngag pa yin//mkha 'gro gzas la 'pho ba'i tshul//gdug pa'i gnasu brnag pa'i gzugs//bsdus la ming rus snying po khams//gcod pa spu gri mtsham su sbyar//dgying dbu su btsang bcas brlag/
21a2(tsa): phyag rgya zil gnon gsal byas la//dmod pa nyin mtshan khri khri bzlas//'khor lo thod pa sngags kyis bskul//cha lugs bzlog la brnol khrag brde ba//kun 'joms bcug lus sogs 'brel//gdug rtsub gtsang dang zhen chags gcod//
21a3(tsa): yid kyi dbang po 'khrul pa yis//skam chags rgyun gyis srog phrog nas ka gsang mdzod mun khung gzugs la bsdud ka me rlung stobs kyis 'khrug cing 'pho//rkyen med sbyor ba'i stobs dpag med//srid pa'i 'khyams khang lho ru 'chang //mngag
21a4(tsa): gnang ma nga' yi sbyor ba yin//mgo gcig gdong lce gnyis pa'i tshul//rgyud cig dang snyigs sngags kyi dbye//gsad pa'i thod pa 'khor lo sngags//sngags kyi sbyor ba tshul gyis dbye//ming rus khams sogs sngar bzhin gnag/spu dr-i drag po gsum su
21a5(tsa): bsgyur/yang gso mchog gi snying po gcung //drag po mo pa la stobs dpag ming /skyo ze phag rjod skyeng dang 'og/rdo rje rgyal po bzlas pa bya/e ka he dun pa ma snyid khrag ram tsa la/srog la phreng shag tsu ril dun dun dza//sa nA ga srog la hU~M nga
21a6(tsa): ngo bo/buM ma~M la zhag kha nA ga t+ta hU~M ri ba sha ka/sa nA ga rgyal zang bu ri lugag/sa tsug rtsal du lid ri la shaka/sa nA gA srog la hU~M buM ril shag/s+yA nA~M ga kha~M nan gyi ril shag/sa nA ga snying gzer gyur nan bu'i lo shAg/sa nA ga du semam tho
21a7(tsa): gzur zad bur la shag sa nA gtsi shu la gzeng dan num la shag/r-i ni kr-i yang shig na gzing chu ri ba shag/snying gi 'bru gsuM 'bru drug bdun//thos pas a mu ka dang s+yam/dgu dang btsug sacu dang gsum//gzer zhing gnan pa mchog la skye ba/
21b1(tsa): snying gzer bdun la rbang gnan//baM rnag pa'i rtsa rgyud khams la brdeg/dgra bo'i srog la breng shag thu ril dun dun dza//dgra bo'i srog la br-ing dza dza bruM ril shag zad//dgra bo'i tsit+ta thu ri la shag rbad//dgra bo'i rtsa la bruM ril shag zad/dgra
21b2(tsa): bobopo'i srog la ka duM ling bruM ri la sha ga rbad//dgra po'i srog la hur thu ri la sha ga rbad//dgra bo'i srog la khaM nan bruM ril shag rbad//dgra bo'i srog la snying gzer skur nan bruM ril nan/dgra bo'i srog gzir bruM ril nan/dgra bo'i sro gzir
21b3(tsa): la yaM shig/thu ril gzir gnan/'di dag sbyor ba dam pa ste//lus can mchog gi sbyor ba yin//'od zer sngags kyi rta la bskyon//'phen pa stobs kyi dbugs kyis 'dren//'dril ba rang shugs khams dang ldan//srog gi yi ge rang las bslang
21b4(tsa): 'gug 'brel gshig pa rang nyams ldan//srod dang rtsa lam rgyad bcas yin//gang stong gnyis dang lci la myur//'brel chen bzhi dang 'byung bzhi smin//sangs rgyas khams dang bde bar ldan//rig pa stobs dang bgrod par bcas//de ma yin na
21b5(tsa): rang 'ching bgrod//sngags kyi lton dpag tu med//rtsa ba gcig la don gnyis ldan//dril dang rgyas pa gcig tu gnas//gtor dang las rang 'phreng de bzhin bsde ba//dbye ba sdu la bsdus te brdeg/che ba gsum 'dus bsam dpag dka'//u pu
21b6(tsa): na'i gnas chud bzhin//zin pa tshor bar mi byed 'joms//mkha' gro gzhan tshogs zhen chags kun//gzas kha dgra la spo zhing bsgyur//rno dpal mtshon cha thebs la gzir//rang gnad rtog pa'i rgyun las bzlog/thod pa gzir gsum mtha'
21b7(tsa): ru bca'//sbyar ba kun ldan de nyid gzer//sa sos laM du stong dang sbyor//rdo rje gsang ba'i lam du drangs//gang gnod gdug pa'i lag du gtang //gsad pa mchog gi sbyor ba yin//gdug pa gsum dang byad bsrungs dang //btsan dang rgyal po
22a1(tsa): @mdze 'dre dang /spongs nas dbye ba sngags kyis bsdig/sngags pa rgyal blon yul srungs rje//zod pa khams dang sbyar la bsdeg kha sdebs la drag sngags bcig bstan/a yin//drag sngags bcig yang gsang yin//
22a2(tsa): gzhan yang bsrung ba bcug las//rgya shog gru bzhi cha gnyis la//dbus nas mu khyud drug du bskor//dbu su gru gsum thod par bskor//gnyis par 'khrul mig bcu sa la//thod pa'i phreng bas kun la bskor//gsum par 'khrul mig/
22a3(tsa): nyi shu bya/bzhi par rtsibs chen sum cu gnyis//yang logs phyi rims thod sbrul me//dbus su gang dmigs gzugs dang mig/gnyis par rtsa ba'i drag sngags dgod//gsum par khyad par drag po yis//gzir zhing srog
22a4(tsa): rad brog myos shing gnon//drag po btsan thabs spu gri dgod//bzhi pa rtsibs chen sum cu gnyis//shi phrad gug phul drag po 'bru//yang gsang mchog gi drag sngags dgod//rus sbal lto bar rdzogs par bca'//'khor lo
22a5(tsa): rgyal po gzugs brnyan la//bdud rtsi 'o ma khrag gis byug/kha sbyor mtshon skur thod dgong gzhug/lto sbyor khri yis ngar blud la//sngags pa'i steng 'og par snang bca'//de yis thaMd srungs pa yin//shi phrad stobs kyi 'khor/
22a6(tsa): lo yis//gsad dang bsrung ba las kun 'grub//bsad pa drag po'i las brtsom na//'khor lo'i sbyor ba sngar bzhin las//dbusu brnag pa'i ming rus khams//snying po 'bru bdun 'pho zhing 'phang //spad bzhi spu gri ming rus
22a7(tsa): sbyar na nyi ma bzhi dang mtshan mo bzhir//drag sngags 'khor lo bskor zhing gcad//gnod sarA gnan la gzas kha 'pho//srog la thun zad b+h+yo zhes 'phang //drag sngags sbyor ba gnyi su sde pas//e ka he dun ya ma snying khrag ra ma/
22b1(tsa): tsa ya/sa nA ga ci ro rgyags zad khrag la skyegs te ya shag cad/sreg la rmugs kyi zad /dus byung bram shing zang /kha shag tshu ril lcags cad/tsa ti ma mya dgrags la bdag zer cung /ts+tshal cu cu zad /srog lA rgyan
22b2(tsa): rbad b+h+yo/bsrung ba'i rgyal po shi 'phrad la/bgrod pa gsum gyi rtsa ba dang ka bdun pa la sogs sbyor ba gdams//gsad pa'i skabasu gsal byas la//'khor lo thod pa'i ling ga ming //ha~M khung re thums zhabs 'og
22b3(tsa): bca'o/gsang sngags gnan gzir zor thun sde pa//mtha' ru gang gnod de nyid sba//drag sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste//dgra bo tsit+ta ro myags rbad dgra bo'i srog la myogs ti ya shag rbad //srog la rmugs ti rbad/khaM shag thum
22b4(tsa): ril myags rba da/dug phyung khri shig rbad/dbugs la thang gzer rbad/rtsa la hur sa rbad/dril dong gtor ma nyin dang mtshan//rdzogs nas gzir zhing ling gar gdab//gnan pa rbad spangs nan bzhir sbyar//snyam pa ya nan
22b5(tsa): /kha gso las kyi thun bskul nas//brab cing 'phang ba'i drag sngags brdeg/dgra bo'i srog la thun rbad b+h+yo//nA ga gu hA tsa ma ha ri/tI bA rI N+ya sarba ti/rU ba s+ya sA s+yo rI kA/su men ta'i khu ba bzhin//ha lA tsa ma ri'i stobs//'joms byed
22b6(tsa): stobs kyi 'khor yin//gzhan yang bsrung ba kun gyi mchog/de yi sbyor ba 'di lta ste//rin chen bdud rtsi dri dang chu//lha sgom sngags bzlas rtag tu bkru//yan lag bum pas bsrung ba'i mchog/zil gnon phyag rgya'i skur gsal bya//
22b7(tsa): rdzogs pa'i lto ba'i srog bsdus la//drag sngags brgya brgya rgyun du bzlas//bsam gtan rtsa ba'i bsrung ba'i mchog/dbu la na mkha'i 'khor lo'i sngags//rtsibs bzhi 'dren pa'i snying po e//thugs kar rig pa'i 'khor lo sngags//
23a1(tsa): rtsibs bzhi dbus na 'bru lnga'i mchog/lte ba 'byung ba'i 'khor lo sngags//rtsibs bzhir he dun ya dang mi//gsal bas stong gsum dbang du bsdud//'khor lo rgyun gyis bsrung ba'i mchog/drag po sngags kyi rmod pa/
23a2(tsa): ni/snying po drang dang shugs kyis drangs//rngam tshig drag po gtum po'o//e ka he dun ya ma'i snying khra ga ra ma tsa ya/stobs kyi drag sngags rtsa ba la//rno dpal mtshon cha kun 'joms sbyar//sarba tsit+ta raM myags kha thams sgrog gzir sbu na
23a3(tsa): 'drid phyung hri shag rba da//sbyor ba kun gyi mtha' sdud du//gsang chen las kyi rtsa ba 'di//'khor lo sbyin sreg lho sdebs na//gcod dang 'bigs la sogs pa'i las//bsrung dang skyob pa kun byed yin//thabs rnams ldan dang mi ldan pas//bya ba
23a4(tsa): kun 'grub brlag par 'gyur//'di dag bde dang smin pa'i las//'bigs dang gcod la sogs pa ste//bsrung dang skyobs pa kun byed pa'o//gsang chen 'phrul gyi 'khor lo 'di//gdams shing grol ba'i rim pa ni//rgyu la rdzas kyi byang ba bya//bar snang
23a5(tsa): 'khor los las kun byed//sa yis 'khor los sreg cing 'joms//thig skor gsum ldan gru gsum dbus//zlo kham dmar po 'brub nas rnag/phyi rim rdo rje rgya graM la//snying po 'khrul mig gsum drang //rtsa ba'i drag sngags kun gyis lus///
23a6(tsa): yang logs rdo rje'ir brgyad la//'khrul mig badris bcu drug la//dag zo thabs kyi sgra gri dgad//mtshams dang zla kham dbus na su//don gnyis bsdus la ming sogs bri//rdzas ldan par snang kha sbyar bcar//las kyi rgyal po gco pa yi pa
23a7(tsa): snang mtshon cha'i 'khor lo yin//phungs sa gsum gyi stegs bu la//khrag dang dug gi chag chags gdab//bam gyi srad bus lnga ru bskor//drag po 'khor lo kha khyer gsum//phyi nang chag chags dbus mtho gang //dkar dmar mthing nag ldan pa sa
23b1(tsa): rtsa nga dang thod pa de bzhin me//mthing nag ling ga hom shing dbus//phyi rim rgya glai phur pa'i ming //yang logs rtsibs la rtsa ba'i sngags//rgya gri rtse brgyad spu gri ming //gdug pa'i khrag dang dug gis bri/ka/phyi rim thod phreng me ri'i mtha'//
23b2(tsa): sreg pa sa'i 'khor lo yang //rus sbal srid pa'i lto bar bri//dug dang khrag gI rme ba byA//sha sna tshil snu ri lu lha yi grangs//nyungs kar nyungs nag skye tshe 'bras//zangs phye lcags phye rdo phye thun//pho nyas gzugs btub bstab pa bya//pra
23b3(tsa): /sngags bcu yis bsreg rdzas dbul//rgya gre rA yi 'dab brgyad du//bsreg pa'i spu gri gzugs la dgod//drag sngags sbyor ba me dang 'dra//e ka ha~M dun ya ma snying khra ga ra ma tsa ya/dgra bo'i srog la dun tshugs dza/tsit+ta hur thubs /
23b4(tsa): la baM/dgra bo'i srog la breng shag rbad/ni tri rag shA rbad/khaM raM dzA la raM/bsrung dang gsad pa'i las kun la//tshul bzhin sbyar na rno la myur//bgrod pa'i mi ldan rang la gcod//bcus rnal ma rtsa ba gcig/yan lag spu gri ming sbyar na//
23b5(tsa): las 'gyeng sbyor ba shis la bde//rig pas bde ba'i klong las phang //de ma yin na rang 'ching rgyu//drag po rjes gcod 'khor lo me//brnag pa kun gyi rgyal po yin//yang gsang mchog gi le'u ste drug pa'o////de nas 'khor gyi 'ojam piSha/
23b6(tsa): rdos bac+doMs lo gsol ba bta'o//ji snying po rang byung bas//drag po sngags kyi dmod la la//snying po don gyi ngan sngags kyis//gsad gsod dbab gcad gzir nan gyis//rtsa bar dril ba ji ltar lags//rdo rje snying pos rdzogs par mdzod//ces
23b7(tsa): zhus pas//brjes kyis drag sngags tha~Md kyi snying por dril ba//srog dmar len gyis sbyor ba 'di gsung so//de yi sbyor ba rgyas pa ni//thun mong khyad par gnyi su bya//thun mong rim pa lnga dang ldan//'bru bzhi phyi lo 'khor lo dang /kun 'thung rtsa ba'i
24a1(tsa): @'khor lo dang //gang bzugs kyi 'khor lo dang //phag mo khong yun 'khor lo dang //yung byed sdig pa'i 'khor lo dang //yan lag le thog 'joms pa dang //lnga ni thun song sbyor bar gsungs//dmar len sngags kyi snying gton sa
24a2(tsa): sbyor ba gsum du dril bsdebs dang //mtshan ma mngon byung sbyor ba ni//dmar len mar gyi kun mar 'dra//de ni stobs chen drag po yin//'bru bzhi las kyi rim pa ni//dur khrod dben pa'i bde gan su//rang sems dben pa'i mtshams gcad la//
24a3(tsa): brtul zhugs rmod pa drag po bzlas//dur khrod rgyu kha tsakra bca'//mu khyud bzhi dang ldan par bskor//lte bar gru gsum bsnol ma ra bri//gnyis pa rdo rje rgya gr-i la//e dun ka yaM 'bru bzhi dgod//gsum pa rdo rje rgya gr-i la//srog dang breng ni gro gsum
24a4(tsa): la dang dun dun bzhi pa ste//srog la zang dang oM shig drug/ rtsa zad bdun pa srog la brgyad //yang legs rgya gra rtse gsum la//phyogs dang thad dang khyer rang byung //lugs dang steng ti dang li rang shag/long dang zad rnams rwa rer dgod//srid pa rus /
24a5(tsa): lto bar bca'//kha sbyar rdzas kyi sbal bar bzhug/de yang mtshon skud phag mo'i ltor//gnyen nam mchod rten nag po laka/nyin gsum mtshan gsum gang brtsams 'grub//kham shag rtsa zad//srog la myogs rbad/khyer chung mugs ti ling shag
24a6(tsa): long zad//rtsa ba kun 'byung 'khor lo ni//gsang chen rtsa bar dril ba ste//yang gsang sbyor ba drug gnyis lnga//bde dang smin par byed pa'i sngags//ha la nag po dug 'dzin ltar//sbyor ba bcu bdun bag dang ldan//don gnyis mngon du spyod pa yi//brdeg
24a7(tsa): pa'i drag sngags 'di lta ste//bla chen bzhi dang sprul pa brgyad//yang sbrul bcus chags dang ni/gang byung gsad pa'i sngags bzhi bsdeb//rtags med kun byung den gnyis drug/de bzhin lo thog gzhom pa bzhin//chu gril zor 'phang du ba gzir//zhu nod
24b1(tsa): 'khyams pa rdzogs pas 'grub/dmar len sngags kyi mthu ma sdus nas ka las kyis 'khrul 'khor mtshon char bcas//snying po kun gyis mthu dril nas//'bru brgyad dmod pa'i lus dang sbyar ka/gsang chen dmod pa'i las bcu yis//rigs rtsol las/
24b2(tsa): dbang gnod pa'i gdon//sngags kyi brdeg pa drug dang ldan//lta 'jug phyir 'byung zhen dang bzhi//kun 'byung rtags med khong nas ldang //gang rung mtshon cha drug sdebs la//brjod pa skabs dang sbyar la spyad//rtags med kun 'byung gcig tu dril//kun gyi
24b3(tsa): rjes gcod bsreg cing gzir//rgyal po'i thig le mthu dril sngags//kun 'joms sbyor ba'i rgyal po yin//rtsa ba'i 'khor lo rgyas pa ni//gru bzhi cha nyams rus sbal lhor//yas lnga yi thig drangs la//mgo dang mjug mar thig drangs la/mgo
24b4(tsa): dang mjug mar thig rkang gdab//de bzhin lho gnyis sna drangs la//thig bzhi rgya gri legs par gdab//thig bdud 'khrul mig drug gnyis lnga//cha nyams gru bzhi legs btab ste//mu khyud rim pa gsum gsuM bskor//dbus kun gru gsum
24b5(tsa): pho brang re//byang khog dbus kyis pho brang gnyis//g.yas pa mtshon tshwa rtsibs bcu la//gcod pa'i ral gri bcu dang ldan//g.yon la rgya gro bcu dang ldan//g.yon/ral gri thun gzir 'bru bcu'i gnas//rgya gr-i de bzhin yaM yaM rngubs//bcu bcu ste/
24b6(tsa): lkog bsnol mar gcod/g.ya su rtsibs chen bcu bzhi la//gzir dang tsit+ta rtsa spur gnas//g.yon na rtsibs chen bcug la//yaM breng dun dza srog dbrog dgod//srid pa'i khyams khang bstod na gnas//de bzhin bskyed pa'i 'khrul mig bzhi//'khor
25a1(tsa): @losu khyud gsus dang ldan ga kyims chen bcu gnyis de bzhin bcu//bcwa lda rdzogs dang thabs bdun na//rma ches sprul pa yang sbrul dang /chags sngang mas pa dag sngags re/mtshan pa'i rgyal po smrangs//
25a2(tsa): rus spel khog slad lto ba'i rjod//rin po che yi 'khrul mig bzhi//rtsibs bdun rtsibs dgu de bzhin bcu//bcug dang ldan 'khrul mig la//mi 'khor 'khor 'dzug don pa la//brdeg pa'i drag sngags tshar re gnas//lo thog 'joms pa'i/
25a3(tsa): 'khor lo bzhi//yan lag bzhi la gnas pa ni ka lag g.yas 'khrul mig drag po brgyad//rtsa ba'i drag sngags kun la gnas//phyi rim bcu drug 'khrul mig la//spogs pa gzir ba'i drag sngags gnas//lag g.yon 'khrul mig sngar
25a4(tsa): bzhin las//phyi rim rtsibs dgu zor gyis sngags//rkang g.yas brgyad dang rtsibs dgu la//dgu la thabs kyi spu gro gnas//rkang g.yon rtsibs chen bco brgyad phyi//du ba'i stobs kyis dbab pa'i sngags//yan lag chu rgod drag po yin//mgo
25a5(tsa): la sreg pa'i 'khor lo ni//rim pa gsum ldan dbus rim gsum//rgya gr-i bcus rtsibs dang ldan//rgya graM rtsa sngags bcus bsregs//kun gyi mtha' ru 'di sbyar bya//kun byung las kyi 'khor lo la//gdug pa'i rab sreg dang ldan//snying po
25a6(tsa): rnga ming rus khams sbyar//las skabs sbyor ba'i rig pas gzung ka gang 'byung 'khrul 'khor lho la sbyar//gnyis gnyis kha sbyar nye ba'i rgyu//gril ba'i mtshon cha sngags blangs nas//sbyor ba mchog gi spyod yul rab//sngags la dang blud me la gdug
25a7(tsa): /kun kyang ras sbal lto bar bcal/rtags re blangs la sngags de gzungs//kun 'byung btags med snol yar brdeg/sngags kyi dbye ba 'di lta ste/a phyags la rdubs lugs sha ka dun/dgra zer srog la jil shag pa/ci b+ha bre gag zad/'a thon
25b1(tsa): srog la thun tshugs rdza tsit+ta zad/dgra ba'i srog la nr-i hung thums dza tsit+ta bring thun shag cang /dgra zom srog la thun gzing gag zad/dgra zal srog la thun gzar dza tsita phre shag zad cad/bzla zal zlog la sa ya ni dza tsid+ha gle gag / sa/
25b2(tsa): srog la nr-i dza mA rA yo zad/hab tang ha thA ting ro phyogs je br-id kri gling hi nam yal yal tsit+ta rbang /ha la ha la tsam thun gzir shag cad/he la ha la yaM oM tsa~Mt+ta breng breng dun dun dza/srog la gzing nan tsita myogs rtsal cang /yaM rngubs tsit+ta kha~M shag
25b3(tsa): myags rbad/de badz+ra dun saM nA ga ra gzir thu na pro phroM rtse ba'i sbyor ba mchog/hri la shar shar phog hu ra yaM hri la b+h+yo khro shar shar grun b+h+yo/raM yaM phreM shar hri la khaMng sha ga khaM pho ga/nA ga de ba yun na/sa tsa gzir nan shar shar hu ra yaM hri la hril pho/
25b4(tsa): yo khaM shag raM gzir thun rbo da/rtsa myags thun raM rba da/kun 'byung rtsa ba'i sbyor ba mchog/las kun sausor bsdebs pa yin//de nas yang bac+doMs kyis gab pa thugs kyi 'khor lo 'di gsungaso/sbyor ba'i rgyal po dmar len gyis//drag
25b5(tsa): po thugs kyi 'khor lo 'di//ni rang gos la rgyu blangs te//sbyor ba kun ldan be ga ta/khru ba chags pas snag tsha byaka 'khrul 'khor rim pa bzhi ru bskor//drag po dbang sdud phyag rgya'i ming //rtsa ba'i 'khor lo lcags mkhar brgyad//
25b6(tsa): yis las dang ra tsa bdud//mkha' lding dregs pa rus spel yum//rangs ma kun 'byung snying pos dgang //yang logs su khyud bsnyen zad gsang //phyi rim phyogs mtshams 'khor lo brgyad//yan lag 'khor lo bzhi gnyis ldan//mu
25b7(tsa): khyud gsum gsum drag po dbus//tha ma gdug pa'i rabs bskor//yan lag rtsa bar 'khor lo bzhi//bong ba ming gi 'bru bzhir ldan//sa khams rgya gri 'bru bcu da pa'i sngags//mu khams drug gnyis srog dbrog 'brug/rlung khams
26a1(tsa): bcu gnyis thad gzir //'debs dang drod mtshams bcu drug rtsibs//lo thog 'joms pa'i spu gri dgang //de bzhin g.yeng dang bskyed pa'i 'tshams//rtsi thogs 'bras bu sbyin pa'i gang //drong dang chu tshams bcu gsum ci ngas
26a2(tsa): gdug pa gzir ba'i 'bru yis dgang //gang sgrub 'brel la kha sbyor bcar//srid pa'i khyam khang lho na gnas//dang ldan thod pa dong du bcar//gang byed spu rmi zhag gsum brtsam//yan lag sau kha sbyar la//thon nas gdug pa'i klu la gtad//
26a3(tsa): ba'i rgyal po gdug pa'i mtshon//dmigs pa'i rtsa dang khong du 'phang //drag sngags dbye ba 'di lta ste//e myags ya rngubs mugs shag ka bdun//dgra bo'i srog la hur thums tsit+ta breng shag mA ra ya rbad//dgra po'i srog thun gzir hu ra khaM
26a4(tsa): breng dag ga s+phar nan snyam pa ya nan//sarba u nan bruM ril skur nan sha truM 'drin nan/sa nA ga thums ha la ha la yaM yaM sbyar nan hur bruM/ri thun ro myags tsit+ta ra ma khas tsa thang rag+na thibs rbad/yul 'dir se re ba e nan ka phreM/ma ts+ya hil
26a5(tsa): hil de ba gzir phro/na ga shar shar pho ba/ser ba sha truM sti pa ya nA sha ya phaT/drag po dmar len gab pa yi//brnag pa thugs kyi 'khor lo yin//gzhan yang yan lag sbyor ba ni//rtsa ba gcig tu dril ba ste//phag mo 'am
26a6(tsa): sdig pa'i lto bar bca'//dril gtor kha sbyor thod pa gsum//gang rung zung du 'brel bas gcod//snang stong gnyis med yis sku//gsal gsum don bzhi yang dag bzhi//'dun po gsum dang sdems bzhir ldan//mi g.yo rtsal med pa
26a7(tsa): slang zhugs//yul dang phrad na shugs kyipo 'char//rtag meng skyod pas 'chi ming grol/lus gsum myong bas byed med dru /snying po ngas snyigs 'byed cing myor /gcig gis ma pa chab nyin la'ang u/'bru gnyis su du dam/
26b1(tsa): ba'ang 'byed//rtogs pa ru bzlog 'khor rgyun gcod//bde lam 'jog pa rgyan ldan pa'o//rten pa'i 'khor lo 'di lta ste//dur khrod ras sam shog bu la//gsod pa'i rgyal po gdug pa 'joms//phung byed phag mo'i lto ba ru/mu
26b2(tsa): khyad rim pa lnga ru bskor//dbus dang zla khaM dbang bsdu da gsod//mun khang gsang mdzod tshe srog 'chang //phyi rim n+ya graM 'khrul mig brgyad//sna tshogs rdo rje rA brgyad ldan//rtsa ba'i 'khor lo snying po mchog/phyi rim lto sbyor mu khyud
26b3(tsa): zlum/gang sgrub gsad pa'i sngags kyis gang //me dpel 'khor lo gshed kyi 'bru//rno dpal mtshon cha lto sbyor yin//yang logs mtshon cha rtsibs bcus//dmar lam srog gi sku gri gang //rno dpal 'khor lo stobs dpag med/
26b4(tsa): gcod cing 'bigs pa dar bcas yin//thad gdug pa'i ra bas snga mas//zhe sdang gdug pas khong nas skems//mdzo yams drag po'i 'khor lo yin//gdug pa kun gyi sbyor ba mchog/phag mo mtshon cha sna tshogs spuka/klu gnyanas
26b5(tsa): bdag za yin bskul//rnol thog mdze rims dri chugs gtong //dmar len srog gi sngags kyis mdzes//'khrol 'khor rdzas dgu'i thad pas byug/khams dang snying po ming rus rnams//gsang mdzod mun khang lnga la bri//khyams dang /
26b6(tsa): mu ran gnod pa'i gdon//'khor lo kha sbyor thong bar bzhug/stengam po yi thod pa la//sbrul dang rgya gram me ri ldan//gnon cing gzir ba'i sngags kyis gang //dmar len sngags kyis dmod pa dbab//dpal brnal mas gcod
26b7(tsa): 'a la gso 'khor lo thod pa dang //drag sngags brnal bzlog nol mar brdeg/thon nas gnas kyi 'og tu swa//nya stong thong pa ling ga dang //brgya brgya mtshon ma snol mas gcod//rtags stos cher gnod kyu la gtad//rtsa rgyud bcad pa'i skye
27a1(tsa): ba ni/nyin zhag gsum dang thugs gcig bzlas//thon nas 'khor me bong ba gzing //dug shing me dang dung pas bajug 'kho la kyang ba rdzas kyis dz+wal//rdzas kyi phags malto bar mjug/sbyo
27a2(tsa): ldan klu la gtang //sngags kyi rgyal po shags kyis drangs//ma byung sbyor ba'i stobs zhan nu//'khor lo drug dang tsha ba khrag/sme ba lnga dang bsnol khrag sku//sngags rnams dug dang ming rus khrag/dgug sngags lugs dang lugs
27a3(tsa): min bris//rgyu rkyen ling ga dbus mtshams lnga//lus dang yan lag 'byung ba dkrug/lha srin gnad la bor te gsod//gdug pa snying deng kha ru gtad//drag sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e myags la rngubs mugs shag ka run/dgra
27a4(tsa): bo'i tsit+ta breng shag dza srog la hur thums rbad//thums hur la srog dza shag breng ta cing dgra bo rbad//dgra bo'i srog la breng shag tsur thums shag dza tsit+ta rbad /srog thums hur la dza shag breng tsit+ta rbad/phag mo khong yan nye ba'i rgyu//
27a5(tsa): rno dpal mtshon cha rnal ma yin//gzhan yang sbyor ba drag po'i las//sngon du 'gro ba sngar bzhin las//khyad par lha srin dkrugs pa'i las//sdigs pa chen po'i lto ba ru//mu khyud rim pa lnga dang ldan//dbus la sogs pa snga ma bzhin
27a6(tsa): bcas pa drag sngags dkrug bzlog dgod//gsong mdzod lnga la ming rus khams//zla khams gsum la srung ba'i lha//rgya gre 'bru brgyad drug po'i sngags//snying po 'bru brgyad rtsa ba dkrug/'khor yug lha srin gang sgrub sngags//
27a7(tsa): ma'i rtse mo bcus la//dmar len snga mi krugs la dgong //sbrul dang su ran gnod pa'i ming //kha sbyor zhing ras rgyal br-ika lcags kyi sdig pa'i lto bar gzhugs//mgo dgu kha dbus gcang cing ba// khrag zod dang mal log/
27b1(tsa): lag dgus len byed lag dgus gcod//zla nga dang dbang po srog rtsa sdung //rkang pa dgu yis ngung chad ko/rkang pa dgu yis gzing zhing gnon//lcags kyi sder mos tsit+ta 'byin//dug gi rmo bas myos shing glams//dug gi rlangs pas gsod/
27b2(tsa): cing bsreg/mchi ma khrag rang chu dug/rdzam mtshon cha'i lo ma yis//dug rtsub rtsa dang srog rtsa gcod//gsod pa'i sdig pa lto sbyar la//thod pa sbyar nang du bca'//mon nam rab chad nang du ga zhug/gsum ma ma phrugs gcig drag
27b3(tsa): sngags las//dug lnga skam chags yul la rba da//rgyal blon stobs can dngas snyigs 'byed//'byung ba lnga yi 'og tu gnan//stobs chen 'byung ba'i dgra la gtad //gdug pa gzir la dgra zla brko//dbang chen phyag rgya de bzhin brtag/ka lu ka/
27b4(tsa): ba bo mo byed//sna tshogs rdo rje gru gsum sprul//snying po mi nga bcas brda' gsum bsdu//drag sngags dkrug pa 'di lta ste//ya rngubyas e shag dun myags ka mugs//dgra bo'i srog la shag bred dza tsit+ta thums hur rbad /sdigs pa lto
27b5(tsa): sbyar dkrug pa'i las//kun gyi mtha' ru spags pa yin//de dag thun mongs sbyor ba'o//de nas yang bac+doMs kyis khyad par gyi sbyor ba 'di gsungaso//brda' don thabs kyis grol ba'i las//ba dang 'o ma zho dang mar//zhun mar snum gyi snying po tshul
27b6(tsa): snying po'i sbyor ba gsum dang ldan//gsad gsod bab pa'i las chen yin//gsad pa khyad par sbyor ba dag/g.yang min brtan dang grol dbang dag/stobs ldan sbyor ba'i snying po 'di//gang thun rgyu la 'khor lo bca'//rdzas dang thang pa'i sbyor ba yin///
27b7(tsa): papa' tshul drag po'i stobs kyi sa spyad/sdug byed phag mo'i lto ba ru/srid pa'i khams khang srog bsdud bca' / khyams khang lto ba rnag pa'i gzugs//spyi tshugs snying khar mtshon cha bcar//su khyung gsum dbus ming rus bri//byi rim rgya gri rtsa ba'i sngags//
28a1(tsa): @yang logs bcus spu gri'i mtshon//rus sbal lto yi phyogs mtshams las//rtsibs chen bcus rtse mo nas//gsang pa drag po'i spu grir ldan//phag mo'i lhon rtsibs bcus//rtse mo nang stan spu gris gang /ka/'khrul mig
28a2(tsa): khams khang rus gang //gnan pa'i drag sngags mtsham su brdeg/bskyod pa bcus yan lag mgo/ga don gnyis bsdus la sbyor ba brtsas//kha sbyar dang ba'i brnag pa'i rten//rgyu rten drug bcas cog bur dbab//snying khar 'debs byed sor gsum ngas/
28a3(tsa): bsgom bskul drag sngags brnol mar brdeg/srid pa gsum gyis srog bsdus la//sngags dang spu gri thems la blangs//ya bag sha'i sbyor tshul can//rtsa dang srog gzungs gcod cing bsreg/ho ba na srog 'dzin dang snyigs 'byed//srog gi thig
