28a4(tsa): le bde lam bgrod//cig shos thod par sdud cing gnon//'degs bskyod yang 'phrul ya yis las//zhag gsuM dmar len dmod pa bor//rngam tshig drag pos rang gshed bslang //lus srog phral nas srog gzir bslang //phur pa khri yis snying
28a5(tsa): gzir//phur pa byin can gzer du brdeg/gdug pa myos blams rgyal dang 'gro//gsum dang lda na rdzi yal subs//thod mkhar rten gyi rten 'brel lha//brnag pa'i rgyud la gnod cing 'joms//dbang can dregs pa pho mo 'khol//srog gi thig le
28a6(tsa): nyi du 'gyur//kye kaM las kyi rim pa yin//stong nyid ma skyes bde ba 'god//bde ba'i yis nyi du 'byar ka/thig le bde ba'i spros dang bral//kha gso mthu ru zlor de bsreg/drag sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e myags ya rngubs rmugs//shag ka dun/rtsa la myogs//
28a7(tsa): khas chang khas ci tu phyogs la rtseng shig phreng la sogs cang /srog sum pa yan /srog lathun rgyo bar byed mongs po'i gnasu /dmi min snye srung skyer 'i yen/ nas brjos kyis lo log gzho bu'i sbyong ba 'di gsung go/khang du
28b1(tsa): thuphrugs bdag nyid ga yis rig pa'i phyag rgyar gsal//tshe gnas dbang po thog 'joms//las kyi brnag pa snang snang sen//rim pa gsum ldan phag mo'i ltor//dbus yul chu gri rtsa gsum//rtsibs chen brgyad gnyis sbyor ba bzhi//kha sbyor sum
28b2(tsa): srungs la srogs rten//thod chen phag thod sbyang ki rdzas//bdun 'bum nyi khri 'bum gcig rtags//rtsa dang skyes dbus rta ma gzer//zor thab du ba spogs dang rnol/ khrag sna dgu dang rme rdzas dgu ka khrag dug zho dang seng ldeng lcags//gzugs brnyan
28b3(tsa): dkar nag klu gnas sar//mon thod nang du dar gyis dril//'bum gcig bcu par rten la bstimaka dbyar dpyid tha chungs ra ba rta//ston zla gsum la nya gsum brgyad//bzhi dang bcug nyi shu dgu//gang rung lo thog 'bras bu sdud//drag sngags sbyor ba 'di
28b4(tsa): lta ste/rnga phreM phreM shar shar hri la nA ga snying gzer rbad/raM hUM yaM shig hril b+h+yo/phreM shar hril b+h+yo/phro shar raM thun b+h+yo/nA ga snying gzer sbud/yaM shig bruM shar/drag sngags sbyor ba rgyal po yis//sman dang rtsi thog 'joms pa'o//thad zungs thub snying po
28b5(tsa): mchog/byin rlabs drag po kun 'dus gsang //bde ba nyams kyi lhun grub gsal//thaMd zil gnon sngags dang yul//snying po srog dang brdal mi sbyar//gzir zhing myos byed dbang po 'tshal//yi ge gnad kyi srog phrogs la//las dang sbyor ba rgyun
28b6(tsa): gcad snyims//rten gyi mchog la gnasu dbab//phag mo'i lto bar rgya gr-i e//nyi shu gnyis rtsibs gdug par ldan//gru gsum gang gnod gzugs dang ming //rgya gr-i de bzhin rtsa ba'i sngags//mtshams dang gsang mdzod rtsa sbubs ming //nyi shu gnyis
28b7(tsa): la spu gri swa//lho sbyar dug khrag phag thod brdag/zhagasum sngags bzlas bsregs la gnan//gang gnod ming dang snying po bsdebs//gzir la srog 'brog sbyor bo gcad //skye srog 'bru gsum gnad kyis dbrog/sgo sgo'i 'dal ming stobs kyis /
29a1(tsa): @kun gyi mtha' ru gru gsum gyis//las dang sbyor ba rang gshed gcod//e myags la rngubs mugs shag ka dun/sarba tsit+ta gzer zlum u nan/khaM shag snying gzer 'dring phyung trig nan raM shag rbad/sngags kyi yar lha spu gr-i sgra
29a2(tsa): no myur bcas te 'joms par byed//bgrod pa stobs kyi dbang med na//rang yang 'ching ba'i lasu 'gyur//thong 'joms par 'don mi zal//de nas yang badz+ras kyis dmar len las kyi kha gso kun gyi sbyor ba 'di gsung so//las rnams kun gyi mchog
29a3(tsa): bsdus pa//rtsa ba gcig tu dril ba yin//las kyi gnad dang bskyod pa'i thabs//bar mtshams sbyor ba kun dang ldan//ri nag dur khrod rme gri rnol//g.yul bkye sa chu khrag blangs la//stegs bu chag chag zor thun bcar//ri shog grog bsnol khrag mo//
29a4(tsa): rtsa ba gcig tu dril ba yin//las kyi gnad dang ba//zhing ras rus chen dug me bod//dug dang tsha ba nag pa'i rten//bcas la thod chen 'thom nag dong //drug las sbyor ba sor bcu gnyis//cog pu spyi tsugs banag pa'i brdar//skyer tsher re ba 'ji
29a5(tsa): nag sgra//gsal gsum mchog gi thugs sngags skor//rang nyid che ba'i don dang rjes//drag la 'dril ba bar mi bcad//dang po dgu lding bzhi bskor//gung srod tho rangs drag tu bskor//dgu thog lhub chen gzer 'debs tshul//rnal mas lus kyi dpa
29a6(tsa): po gshigs//bzlos ste tshe dril thun bzhir brnag/sha rus khrag chu 'joms shing skems//dkug te thun sngags drag bzhir brdeg/rtsa srog dbugs rgyun gcod cing 'pho//ma yengs gdengs gsum phye gsum brun//dgu skor thun gyi brdeg thun ldan//dbang
29a7(tsa): po rnams shes khams dang rten//'pho ba gsum gyi brda' phan yin//skor phyogs g.yas g.yon dbus mthar sbyar//mtha' ru gdon gsum gang rung brnag/gdug pa me dang mon mong kru//zong thun las gnyis bsdebs la brtsam//de yi 'pho tshul gnyis dang /
29b1(tsa): ldan ma yengs rten dang grol bas 'phang ka ra nag chad pa seng+ge kha/gang yang dogs pa med par 'joms//de nas bac+doMs kyis kun sngags drag po 'di gsung so//e myags dgra bo'i srog la baM ri ba kha thun yaM yaM rbad//ya rngub srog la hri la myags
29b2(tsa): thun shag rbad//mugs shag srog la gzer bruM raM thun rbad/ka thun srog la ma ra ya thun rbad//dus tshod nyin gnyis de bzhin mtshan//brdeg pa dang po'i las de'o//dun ka sha ga mugs dubs ya myags e/dgra bo'i srog la hu ra bruM dza/rtsa srog
29b3(tsa): dbugs la raM thun zad/de bzhin bzhi ru pra bskor brde ba//'joms pa drag po'i dbus de bzhin//e rngub tsit+ta hur thu rbang /myags thun tsit+ta breng shag zad/ka rmugs tsi t+ta rngubs shag rbad/de bzhin bzhi skor sngags kyi sgo//drug gis lhas kyi gnang
29b4(tsa): huM 'beb se//bskul bzhi skor bla sngags pa zhi//zla gcig dus bzhi rtag tu bya//kun 'joms drag po me dang 'dra//dug khrag gnyis ldan bde ba'i nyams//rten gcig shes pa dngas snyigs 'byed//dang por yul ldan dmigs pa brtags//bgrod
29b5(tsa): ba'i stobs kyis rdeng gsum ldan//dpal rtul stobs ldan mo pa tsam//rang nyams shugs ldan gang tsam smin//yis nyon mongs pade dang smin//rang 'chings thabs grol ldan dang min+ta//bdon pa gnyis 'dzin don med yal//bde ba'i rgya/
29b6(tsa): rang las shar//ma bcos bcos med bcos dang bral//e yi yis las byung ba//chos sgyu na mkha' yas drag rdzogs//ka yi yis las byung ba//las dang sbyor ba smin cing rdzogs//ya yi yis las srung ba//phul zhing spyod de srog dbugs
29b7(tsa): jo gas/lcags kyi yes las byung /grogasus las smin mthur 'ang jog yi sa gyi las las srung ba//theg skyems khrag rgyas 'phras bu rdzogs chus kyi mis yang byung bgadon gsum ma yengs me ba'i rdzog shag gi ses yang
30a1(tsa): byung bka/'bral ba gsum dang thun pa rdzogs//bdun gyi yis las byung ba//'chi bdag rang bdud sdang gsal rdzogs//'brel chen bzhi ni bgrod pa ste//yi ge sngags kyi gsang tshig nyid//che ba bzhi yi drangs don yin//btso bral/
30a2(tsa): rang nyams shugs kyis spyad//drag la 'dril zhing che ba'i stobs//yin par rtog med rang shugs ldan//de nas bac+doMldas kyis bla kun pho nya bla la mngag pa 'di gsungaso//thugs kyi sprul pa mngon byung ba//shar gyi phyogs nas bla 'gugs pa//dug gi mngon
30a3(tsa): pa se ng+ge'i mgo/dbu dgu spyan dgu zhal dgug dngas ka phyag dgu zhabs dgu tsit+ta dgu//zhabs dgus stong khams zla la rgyug/spyan dgus dgra bo bla nas 'joms//snying dgus dgu po yid la 'dzin//phyag dgus tsirta khong nas 'byin//zhal dgu kha
30a4(tsa): mngar med par gsol ka ljags dgus khrag la myos shing 'thung //dgra bo bla dang phrol la shog/ka li dung ting tsit+ta ro myags srog la bruM ri li b+h+yo//lho yi phyogs nas bla 'gugs pa//lcags kyi mon pa stag gi mgo//da'u dgu spyan dgu zhal dgu tsit+ta dgu///
30a5(tsa): zhabs dgus stong khams bla la rgyug/spyan dgus dgra bo bla nas 'joms//snying dgu dgra bo yid la 'dzin//phyag dgus tsit+ta khong nas 'byin//zhal dgus kha mngar med par gsol//ljags dgus khrag la myos shing 'thung //dgra bo bla dang phrol las shog/
30a6(tsa): ha la ha la dun ting ro myags srog la bruM ri li li b+h+yo//nub kyi phyogs nas bla 'gugs pa//bse yi mon pa dred kyi mgo/dbu dgu spyan dgu zhal dgu gdangs//phyag dgus zhangs dgu tsot+ta dgu//zhabs dgus stong khams bla la rgyug/spyan dgus
30a7(tsa): dgra bo bla nas 'joms//snying dgus dgra bo yid nas 'dzin//phyag dgus ci d+ha khong nas 'byin ka zhal dgus kha ma'ang med par gsol//ljags dgus khrag la myos shing 'thung //dgra bo bla dang phrol la shog/ra~M g+ha hu ti dun ting nge chog
30b1(tsa): dgra bo srog la tsu ri li li b+h+yo//byang gi phyogs nas zla 'gugs pa//gser gyi mon pa khung men mgo//btsu gu spyan dgu zhabs dgu gangs//phyag dgu zhabs dgus skyong khams zla la rgyug/spyar dgus dgra bo zla nus 'dzin//sgyid
30b2(tsa): /dgus dgra bo yid la 'dzin//phyag dgus tsit+ta khong nas 'byin//zhal dgus kha mngar med par gsol//ljags dgus khrag la myos shing 'thung //dgra bo'i bla dang phrol la shog/kar+m+ma b+ha ti dun ting ro myags sogs la thu ri li
30b3(tsa): li b+h+yo//drag po mtha' sdus skyor cing 'dril//pho brang thig le bde la gzhag/yon lhun grub rin po che//rtse ba gcig la yan lag 'byung //sbyor ba du ma las kyi gnang //dmar len sngags kyi mthu dril nas//'khor lo mtshon cha'i
30b4(tsa): rgyal po'o//drag sngags mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud las/dmar len snying po don gyi man ngag gi le'u ste bdun pa'o////de nas yang bac+doMldas kyis yon thob par bya ba'i rim pa 'di gsungaso//tshe sogs bdag po'i don dag ni//rnam grangs lnga
30b5(tsa): dang ldan pa'o//las dang 'bras bu nges par shad//dbye ba dag pa rnam pa lnga//rang bzhin lhun grub tshe bdag dang //yis lhun grub zil gnon dang //phyag rgya gcig pa'i tshe bdag dang //thugs las 'khrungs pa'i tshe bdag dang //las lus 'grub pa'i/
30b6(tsa): tshe bdag go//yon rin po che lta bu//kun myeng 'phel 'grib med par gnas//lhun grub tsha bdag rangs ma lnga//rgyu rkyen 'pho 'du rtsol dang bral//phyag rgya chen po lhun grub pa'o//zil gyis gnon pa'i tshe bdag ni//snang srid yis rtog las
30b7(tsa): 'angs//rtog ces 'khrul pa ru bzlog phyir//rin chen gzhal las lhun gru na gi stobs kyi gdan la de bzhin grub//rang bzhin gsal ba'i ngong nyid las//yos tshun gring pa'i su/lhun po ri rab brtan ltar brjid/sring gsum byo sa
31a1(tsa): @gzil gyis gnon//bdud mo kha 'bar gsod byed mas//myos byed khrag skyugs ma mo'i tshogs//ma lus gril gyis gnon cing 'joms//gsong byed bka' yis byin brlabs gnon//rdeg pas gnyan dngas bdag po
31a2(tsa): gnon//bya khyud klu bdud klu srin gnon//khyung mos sa bdage sa srin gnon//saing+ga rgyal blon stobs can gnon//stag gi dud 'gro gdug pa gnon//zhe sdang gdug pa'i sbrul nag gis//sngags dang dmod pa rig bzhi rlung //tsha ba'i nad dpal zil gyis
31a3(tsa): gnon//srid pa'i khyam khang rus sbal gyis//srog dbugs bkrag mdangs tshe srog 'chang //rang bzhin chos sku skye med las//skye ba'i cho 'phrul lhun grub sku//skye ba las kyi 'khrul pa yi//skum chags skye med dang du grol//dngas ma'i thig nye
31a4(tsa): bcus la sdu//snang srid bde 'gro ma lus kyi//tshe srog yi ge gnyis bsdus 'chang //phyag rgya gcig pa'i tshe bdag gis//gdan le yuM ba bya khyung dreg/rgyan gyi sngags dang gab pa'i sngags//lus srung drag pos 'joms pa'o//sprul pa'i tshe bdag
31a5(tsa): nag po ni//zil gnon thugs kyi sprul pa ste//dbu dgu gnang dgu mi nad 'joms bdud yin//theg pa dku yi don ldan nas ka 'bras bu rig lnga yis bzhi ka sprul pa sna tshogs bsam dpag dka'//las kyi tshe bdag nag po ni//'byung ba brtan bzhi rngams kyi//
31a6(tsa): 'og shed na//mi dag bcu'i las dbang las//yi rags rgyal po 'chi bdag skuka sha za ke kaM 'khor dang bcas//dbang bsdud sngags kyi las kun byed//las kyi rnam grangs 'di lta ste//rtsa babazhi dang yan lag bams//lhun grub tshe bdag nag po'i la
31a7(tsa): rtsa ba gnyis dang yan lag bcu//khyad par brgyad bcu drug yod//snying po man ngag rtsa ba gnyis//yan lag phral na ny+tsi rashuka zil gnon phyag rgya chen po las//lhas log lus kyi go cha srung //phyag rgya cig pa'i tshe bdag laka rtsa ba gsum las yan lag
31b1(tsa): Asprul pa'i tshe bdag mgo dacu'i las//skyongs bcu drug a kro ma.u /las kyi tshe bdag drug pa la las//sgyu ba'i rnam zas rgya mug cig/drug bcu cu gcig byed par gsungs//me bdag nag zi la ba 'di lta ste//nga tsas
31b2(tsa): gcig/yis lhun grub chen po'i sku//sna tshogs sbyor ba sprul pa'i sku//rol pa gsum kyi ngang du rdzogs//'bras bu'i gnas tshul 'di lta ste//chos nyid 'gyur med rang bzhin las//rang byung yis phyag rgyang 'ab//thang sdang stong yis sku//dbyas
31b3(tsa): dadu yis gnyi su med//sna tshogs nges gzungs med pa'i sku//snang stong ma 'gags bde chen nyid//'khor 'das gnyis med rdal ba'i klong //rtog sbyor 'phrin las rtsol las 'das//ting 'dzin 'du 'phro rkyen las 'das//bgrod thob rtsol mod g+hu/
31b4(tsa): gyis grub//mi zad brgyan gyi 'khor lo rdzogs//theg pa kun gyis bgrod pa'i mchog/sgyu ma lhun grub yul la rol//yis lhun grub rtog brjod 'das//rang rig rang gsal rang byung sku//sna tshogs snang ba la ngos bzung med//drag sngags mngon du phye
31b5(tsa): ba'i rgyu las//tshe bdag gis rnam grangs kyi le'u ste brgyad pa'o////gzhan yang bgrod pa'i rim pa ni//sems nyid dben pa'i bde gnas//rang nyams brtan pa'i mtshams bcad las//maN+Dal chen po bya bar brtsam//sngon du 'gro ba'i las
31b6(tsa): rdzogs nas//thugs kyi sprul pa mkha' khyab spro//gug rgyal dri yis khrus bya sa/maN+Dal dbu su brtan par bzhag/bsam gtan bum pas sum yar sbyar//dngos grub gang 'dod drug sngags gdags//gzhan tshogs gtor ma brgyad cu sbyar//skur
31b7(tsa): sgoms drag sngags sku yi sngags//rtags dang sbyar la lto sbyar bzlas//baM gyi tsau t+ta'i pho brang du//tshe bdag 'khor dang bcas pa brtag/ phung po 'byung ba rnam shes la//ya ma'i dkyor rdzogs par brtag/skabs dang sbyar la rtsom pa brtsam//
32a1(tsa): @tshogs dang sbyar mchod pa ling tas+ya//byi rgyud mchod sogs rgyun ma bcad //rang sems dashugs ldan rtags thob na//slob mtshe bdag skur bsgoms la/yol bas bcad la khrus dang sbyar//phyag rgya bkra shis gzus ma brtag/
32a2(tsa): sngags skyes rgyal rigs bram ze'i rigs//lang 'tsho mtshan ldan khrus byas lo//gso byed gzungs mas g.yon nas 'khyud//b+ha ga gsang ba'i pho brang duka/lhun grub kying rtogs par brtag/rdo rje rtsegs pa'i phyag rgya yi//gsang ba'i lha gsol
32a3(tsa): bab gdab//byin rlabs brtul zhugs 'jug pa bsam//khrus dang me tog 'jug par bsam//bde chen sku dang ba ma dkying ka gsang ba lhun grub mkha' 'jug brtul ba zhugs sngon 'gro'i rim pa bdun//bum pa sems phreng sme ba glang //rgyan ma phyag
32a4(tsa): mtshan grags po'i dbang ka gtor ma rag ta bdud rtsi brtag/lung bstan gzengs pa stong dbugs dbyung bya//gsang mtshon brtan pa mi zlog sbyin//dam tshig chos pa shang gdams pa bya//bkur sgro mumar chu gsang ba sprugs//lhar skyed bu la tshogs kyis
32a5(tsa): rol de yi rim pa 'di lta ste/slob ma gzhug phyir gsol ba gdag/lhun grub rang bzhin gnas pa'i pho brang nas//yis rgya mtshod lhun gyis grub pa'i lhaka ma lus mnyam pa'i rgyal po dgo sro ga/sleb ma mtshan ldan yumo gyi dpyod du//
32a6(tsa): zhugs drag po'i stobs kyi bzhu gsol//bde chen mnyam pa'i pho brang gsang ba ru ka rig pa'i brtul bzhugs stobs kyis bzhug stsol/slob ma mnyam pa'i don dang bgrod pa 'dod bu yis//me tog rin chen 'dod pas mdzes byas la//dkyir de
32a7(tsa): las bdag sanyam bya yi//rdo rje rgyal po dag la gsol ba gangag/ston pa'i smon ma 'arjis//lha dang slob dpon dag srol//dregs pa srabs kyis yul bstan pa//'gyur med med peng chen po grang nas//rjengs pa'i lha tshogs dgo so/
32b1(tsa): ba'i dkyir nas//mnyam pa'i rgyal po dgongastsol//dam tshigs dka' thub sdom pa'i/tshogs//rig pa'i rgyal po bdag la dgongs//gsang ba'i sgron ma bdag la dgongs//yis rol pa bdag la gsol//me tog rin chen dbyig bsdus la//
32b2(tsa): bde chen phyi yi 'jug pa tsam//nga phung po lhun grub tshe bdag brtan//bam chen mnyam pa'i dkyor du//mjug pa nang gi lha ngo bstan//ba ga gsang ba'i pho brang du//me tog dor la lha ngo bstan//gdong gi yogs dang ljon spro ba bskyed//bkra shis
32b3(tsa): mtshan ldan gsang ba'i pho brang na//gnyis med sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyar ba'i//byang chub sems nyid mi 'gyur bde ba'i mchog/pho brang lhun gyis grub par bzhugs pa dang //rme ba dang bdud rtsi sbyor ba'i sems//bkra shis mtshan ldan
32b4(tsa): ban dang brtul zhugs sbyin//mi 'gyur bstan pa'i sku dang yis bgrod//gsang ba gzungs ma spro ba skyed la blang //ho bde chen gsang ba'i pho brang du//thabs chen gsang ba'i bde pha ba la//dbu ma'i rtsa rgyud sa bon bsreg+sas/b+ha ga gsang ba'i chos
32b5(tsa): skur bskyil//rtsa lum 'od 'phros longs spyod sku//mnyam par gnas pa byang chub sku//spros bral kun khyab sprul pa'i sku//yub mnyes pas mnyes par bya//sbyor ba dag pa'i byin brlab las//rtse rgyud bye bu'i thig ler bsgril//byang chu
32b6(tsa): shugs dang rje ba bsre//bkra shes mtshan ldan brim par bskyil//btul zhugs rebye 'byams dbang bskur byA//hU~M rig pa'i stobs ldan sbyor ba'i gnas/'khrul po'i bag chagasaso sprin lta bu//yis rol pa'i sgron me gas pa//mnyam po'i
32b7(tsa): rgyal po gartul bzhugs long //e ka ya mahA ti dza hoHmtshan ldan bkra shis pho brang dang //drag po stobs kyis lha ngo bstan//bkra shis bum pas dbang bskur bya/hoHmes lhun grub bum pa yis//brtul zhugs dag pa'i dbang bcun/
33a1(tsa): sa/'khor pa nas dgas sung gsal/nga byung yis dbang thobang shog/e ka ya sa kyu byab gti de sa to//'bras cu khrag latshus yis rdzug/rdzogs par byug rgya chus gru la/rgyan ma stobs kyi
33a2(tsa): sbyin bya//glang chen stag glang zhing lpags sbru la//thod pa snying phreng keng rus rgyan//me ri dur khrod dbang bskur bas//dregs pa'i mthu stobs ldan par sho ga/e ka ya ma hu stomaka ma hA nA ga kA li tsi t+ta dz+wa la nI ho//hoH'khor ba drag byed 'kho/
33a3(tsa): lho dang //nyon mongs gcod pa'i ral gri dang //chos nyid mtshon pa'i rdo rje yis//sku gsum mnyam pa'i dbang thob shog/e ka ya ma tsakra khaD+ga badz+ra hoHbe tshon dug lnga zil gyis gnon pa dang //gdug 'joms byed pa'i btun shing dang /
33a4(tsa): log rtog skams chags 'joms pa'i sdig 'dzub kyis//rig pa stobs kyi sgo gsum dbang thob 'ashog/e ka ya tsit+ta'u ti sa ra pa ho//brtul zhugs spyod pa'i ro la mnyam par rol pa ni /hoHpad+ma rdo rje mnyam sbyar nas//pad+ma ra ga ta byang chub
33a5(tsa): sems//baM chen rme ba'i don dang sbyar//brtul zhugs rol pa'i dbang bskur bas//mi zad rgyan gyis dbang thob shog/e ka ya ma gu h+ya rag ta gu h+ya b+hi d+hi tsit+taM ho//sa ma ya rag ta tsit+ta pu ta king ni ri ti ho//dpya brtul sngags kyis byin bskyed pa'i//
33a6(tsa): pa chen ro la mnyam par ro ga//rdo rje brjid pa'i dbang thob nas//bad ched mnyam nyid thob par shog/e ka ya ma a ti pa ta si d+hi ho//longs spyod dag pa'i dbang bkur bya//'chi sed rang byung nga sa yan//bkra shis ma med ldan lhag pa'i rdzas//'phel
33a7(tsa): 'ang med pa zhung ga/legs rje sesra rdza grong ga ga/seg sas gan rgyan a rbaM ta dz+nyAn tu sa pal shes ngos padrug du chug pa 'phru po ga mangs skye la rang gas ma mi yang chu rtse//'ami med mi 'gyu
33b1(tsa): pa'i dbang thob shog/e ka ya ma A yu dz+nyA na sid+hi ho//snod kyi 'jig rten gtor gnod du//bcud kyi 'jig rten gtor ma gsal//'byung ba rnam lnga gtor ma'i rgyu//lhun grub kun 'byung gtor ma'i rgyan ka/'dzin chags med pa'i stobs kyis
33b2(tsa): rol Ta e ka ya ma ma hA ba ling ta pU tsa ho//snod med brtul zhugs zhan pa la//spel zhig smras na rdo rje khro bo dang //gtum po gnod sbyin mkha 'gro dbang chen gyis//lus dral khong gshags tsit+ta khrag tu 'thung //rdo rje 'chang chen dregs pa dra yin pas//
33b3(tsa): zur yang bcag par bya ba min//dgos pa byos shig dam tshig srog ltar srungs//gal te 'das na rdo rje dmyal bar ltung //'di ni rdo rje dmyal ba'i chu//tshul 'di bsrungs na dngos grub thob//nyams na mnar med dmyal bar ltung //rdo rje'i chu 'di khyod kyis
33b4(tsa): longs te/zadz+ra daM ka oM dam ho//'dod pa'i tshogs la rol pa'o//drag sngags sngon du phyung ba las//baM chen gsang ba'i dbang gi le'u ste dgu pa'o///gzhan yang gdug pa'i nad zhugs na//mtshan nyid rtags pas las rnams smin//de yi rim
33b5(tsa): pa 'di lta ste//rgyal rigs mgo nas rus nas skyed//rje rigs lag g.yas tsha ba skom//bram ze lag g.yon khrag chu bskyed//dmar rigs rkang g.yas zha drug yos//rdol pa rkang g.yon snag la 'jug/ba so rtsa la phol 'bras g.yo//sman dang phye
33b6(tsa): bo rlung skor 'jug/mdze rlung khrag bskyed tsha grangs 'khrug/gang gcig pu ga kha gnyis 'jug gzhog skems subs pa nid gyi rgyu/sa yams rjen shun rdza la 'jug/nang rol lnga la mdze lams bskyed//lto 'phye rtsa bzhi sbubs la 'jug/bdakan
33b7(tsa): khrag rlung khris nang bskyed//skyo du 'tshal g.yeng 'tshal dang 'thibs//sha rus g.yengs pa'i rtags lnga 'thung //klu bdud klu srin skang dang 'gribs//glang thabs grum dang po srin nad //ma mos rims dal skyo nang gtong //rgyal pos myo 'bog lam
34a1(tsa): rdzo/kha can mi gzeng nang dri dang rdzog//khe brang ze zung rgya rnams gtang /'dzin pa gtagan pani dang ldan/yid kyis dugs na khogs ldag/sa na mchog gi sngags kyis ga / bya smra ni tsa
34a2(tsa): 'dzin du shrI phrang phyag rgya nyin mtshan gdon//mthong ba'i dug ni mdze shus 'dzin//mgo dgu sngags kyi lho sbyor shes//kha blangs dugs ni sha bkras 'dzin//sbyor ba gang brtsa ma shis par 'gyur//gzhan yang gdon pa'i sbyor ba ni//sngags dang
34a3(tsa): 'khor lo sreg pa'i las//'jug pa'i rjes gcod sngags kyis gdon ka mgo la sogs pa gdug pa las ka dril gtong 'khor lo lho sbyor sdig/sbyor ba'i rgyal po gsum gyis gdon//mgo kha shi brang nyin mtshan dang //lho sde bas gtor ma'i sbyor/
34a4(tsa): bas gdon//lag g.yas zhugs la snying gzer dku/de bzhin g.yon la nyi shu cig/rkang pa g.yas la gdug pas gdon//g.yon lho kug sbyor bas gdon//bu ga dgu la bcug pas//rtsa bran zhugs la 'bru brgyad shis//dbugs
34a5(tsa): dang rdzi la bcug gtor//rjen shun zhugs na gnyis gnyis sdeg/drod la zhugs na lnga tshe sdebs//rlung rta zhugs na gnyis bor tri//rtsa lam bcug gtsor blangs yang //sa me tsa la sum sdebs dkrug/rtsa bzhi gdug pa 'byung ba'i nad//
34a6(tsa): dkrugs pa'i nas phyag rgya chen pos gdon//tshig mgo bdun la dgu bdun bsde pa//kha zas zhugs la shaM 'phrad sde paka/tsi t+ta zhugs la bcug dkrug/klu bdud klu srin bdun pa dkrug/du me 'bru dgu'i sbyor ba bsdebs//ma mo la sogs rims//
34a7(tsa): /dal gcod//tsit+ta 'bru dgu 'iri sbyor ba yis//skyo 'bog la sogs 'joms pa'i mchog/'dza yaM 'bru dgu dkrugs bsdebs pas//gzer dang dri yi sbyor ba 'joms//ro ni bcu yis 'bru bsdogs pas//blo bur nad dang mdze yams 'jos pa//
34b1(tsa): tu snying po maN+Da la//dmar len sngags kyi snying po zang //kun 'joms rtags rnams med pa'o//drag sngags mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud las//nad kyi mtshan nyid bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu pa'o///de nas yang bac+doMs kyis chos nyid gta
34b2(tsa): la phab pa 'di gsungaso//ma bcos chos sku ye brdal ni//gdod nas gnas pas lhug pa yin//bsgres rgud med pas gnyug ma yin//phyogs ris med pas kun la khyab//mtha' dbus med pas brdal ba yin//rang rgyud med pas
34b3(tsa): 'gyur ba med//mi phyed mi shig rdo rje'i sku//srid gsum khams gsum 'khor ba yang //ming tsam btags pas rgyu ma ste//rang bzhin med pas nyams pa med//ji ltar btags pa'i chos rnams kun//'gro ba'i sdug bsngal
34b4(tsa): byang chub sems//sems dang chos nyid gnyis med pas//rtag pa chen po bde ba'i sku//khyung chen na mkha' ra lding ba bzhin//chos skur sa ng+yas sngar sangs rgyas//bde ba'i yis rang la shar//sgyu ma lhun grub yul la rol//drug sngan
34b5(tsa): mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud las/chos nyid gtan la dbab pa'i le'u ste bcug pa'o////de yang 'khor gyi 'jam pa'i rdo rje des/bac+doMs la gsol ba btab pa/drag sngags rtsa ba ji ltar gnas//dka' thub sdom pa
34b6(tsa): ji ltar gsung //sbyor ba'i rgyal po ji ltar sdebs//drag po sngags dgod ji ltar lags//rdo snying pos rgyas par mdzod//ces gsol to//bac+doMs rdo rje 'chang gis//rgyal ba kun gyi dgongs pa sdus nas bkod do//rngag po sngags kyi/
34b7(tsa): rtsa ba ni//rim pa bdun dang ldan pa yin//'dun pa gsum dang lta ba bzhi//yin pa gsum dang gang sdebs gsum//gsad lugs gsuM dang gnad lugs gsum//'char lugs gsum dang 'pho ba gsum//gsal ba gsum gyis don/
35a1(tsa): chen 'gyug/glang chen grangs po stsol po zung //bde ba mu chog dang thugs rjes zung //gas me med blang zil gnod sgro ga/yi ge khyu dang mtshon ma las//lus dang yan lag bsrung gsang brdeg/rten ca na/
35a2(tsa): med sbyor ba'i tshul//na mkha' bar snang sa gzhir gnan//thugs rje chos dang yis chos//yul dang 'phrad na 'tshor ba'i tshul/lha dang 'khor lo zas pa po//lha dang sngags dang yul dang dmigs//de yis drag sngags rtsa ba 'dus
35a3(tsa): drag sngags mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud las/drag sngags rtsa ba'i le'u ste brtsis pa'o///gzhan yang dka' thub sdom pa ni ka dpa' brtul sngags kyi dmod pa la//gtsang me lo g.yeng ba min//gsang thub shes rab sbyor pa can//
35a4(tsa): dpa' brtul stobaso dang nyams ldan pa//sbyor ba'i khyad par dka' thub srung //gdug pa gsum gyi sbyor ba la//tsha gsum bcud dang rol de bzhin//dri ma che chung zil pa la//brtag pa gsum gyi sbyor bas blang //yon dka' thub
35a5(tsa): kun dang ldan//phyag rgya bstan pa'i skur gsal bya//rang lus dmigs pa gzhan mi bstan//'bum tsho drag sngags rgyun mi bcad//nya stong drag po sbyor ba brtsam//dka' thub drag po'i sbyor ba yin//bsrung ba'i dam tshig sngags kyi
35a6(tsa): srog/bcus gcig gis drang don can//kun gyis dang du blangs pa ni//rdo rje dmang gi ri mi gshig/mngon phyung dogs med dartul zhugs spyad//'khor lo srog 'thar gnan pa zor//gzir sogs dbab pa yan lag yin//dam tshig
35a7(tsa): gzungs la rtag tu 'jug/gcan zan rgyal po gsang ba min//za ma tog tu dug mi brug/rin chen lha brag sreg mi bya//gsang kyi thun chu gtung ba min//bdud rtsi sdong ngan rang mi thug/mi shis sdod du mdangs mi dbapa//sdod ngan se de'i zho mi chu
35b1(tsa): yid bzhin nor bu sreg mi byA//gnas lcags ur mo nang mi 'thag/stag gzigs rol ba'i lhag mi sdu//rdo rje gtams pakri brag mi ga shig/rdo rje ra ba'i rmang mi bral//mun pa sel ba'i sgrol mi gsang //rdo chu bo la bkar mi bcag rgyal/
35b2(tsa): bopo'i phyag rgya rtag mi dbye//rdo rje rdzong gi mkhar mi gshig/rin chen rgyal mtshan rtse mi dbog/thun mong sngags kyi dam tshig yin//lhag pa'i dam tshig rnam pa gsum//bgrod la brtul zhugs lta ba'o//drag sngags mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud wa/
35b3(tsa): dka' thugs dam tshig gi le'u ste bcu gsum pa'o///de nas yang bac+doMs kyis gab pa mngon du chung ba'i sbyor ba 'di gsung so//de yis las rim 'di lta ste//rtsa ba rnam pa gsum du gsungs//gsad dang gso dang lo thog gzhom/
35b4(tsa): gsad pa'i las ni lnga dang ldan//mtshon chab snol ma gnyen bsreg gnyis//lto ba shal log then thun dang ldan/drag po gsad pa'i skabasu sbyar//sangs rgyas thugs kyis bcud bsdus pas//gab pa mngon phyung sbyor ba'i mchog/rang seMs
35b5(tsa): dben pa'i dur khro duka/dpa' brtul sngags kyi dmod pa bor//shu gu gru bzhi mtho gang la//thig skor gsum ldan zla gAm mthong //bla sems ming rus lha rnams brika phyi rim 'khrul mig lcags mkhar bcu//rtsa ba'i snying po me/
35b6(tsa): rlung skyi 'dang gnas//de yi phyi rim 'khrul mig bcus la//ral gri rgya gr-i bsnol mar gnas//ral gri gsad pa'i spu gri la//rgya gr-i gnon pa'i spu gris mdzes//de nyid rus sbal khra bo'i lto bar bri//cha gnyis byas la dug dang
35b7(tsa): khrag gis byug/sngon mar dang bcas thod pa'i dong du brnag/pho thod dmod sngags mi thod ming rus brnag/tshe bdag bsgoms la yis 'khor lo bca'//zhag bdun drag sngags dmod pa rngam tshig ldan par bzlas//zag tu bskul
36a1(tsa): byin nag bA'i ba'i yul la mngag/drag sngags dasod pas gsang la gzir zhing thod pa bas pa/'khor mo thod pa gdug pa'i gtan la gtad/sbyor ba gzhan tshogs drag po'i skabs dang sbyar//drug po sngags kyi/
36a2(tsa): sbyong ba 'di lta ste//e ka ya ma snying khrag ra mal//dgra bo'i srog la rmugs te gshag zang //dgra bo'i srog la snyas pa ya nanun//gzhan yang gab pas gsad pa'i spyod du naM//'khor lo chu khyung rim pa gsum du sgo/grugs
36a3(tsa): maung rus zla gam bskyang ba'i lha/'khor lo rdzobs bdun su gyur gyi ru bya//mtshon rgyas gcod pa'i rtse mo khA/ming ducas spu gri bdun pa dgod//b+h+ra ma'i rtsibs chen dgu pa la//'dren pa stobs kyi gru bzhi dang //khong khrag 'brel TI
36a4(tsa): 'bru lnga dgod//mtshon cha'i bar du ming rus brika 'khor lo thed pa kha sbyar gzhug/thod pa steng ma mu khyud bzhi ru bskor//dbu su rgya gri lte ba oM dang ldan//rA la ya ma 'bru bzhi bsnol mar bri//mu khyud gnyis par rigs bzhi
36a5(tsa): klu brgyad bsnol//mu khyud gsum pa sbrul nag brgyud kyis bsdams//sbrul gyi gseb tu gnan pa'i sngags kyis gang //mu khyud tha ma me'i ri snying po ram/ka/thod pa'i kha khyer phyi ru nan gyis bskor//da pa 'og ma stu khyud ts+tsha bo
36a6(tsa): das kyu gsum rtse se ri gsum ldan//gdug po drag sngags// ming dus b+hU gA rdzogs par hU~M//su byung gnyis bya stsu ban+d+ha skye bsnol bsdum sa//sbrul gyi bya skye du song rdzas si r-I raM//thod pa'i bar du gachod pa /
36a7(tsa): lo gtsug /zhon grangs nyi shu bdun gyi che//gtsun pa'i gtan la khru di nos la ga ni//gdun pa'i ngag sdigs spyi 'di lta ste/oM ga~M oM ma ga zagar ma cho 'akra tha srog la rmugs nyi ga zang //dgra pa'i srog lham
36b1(tsa): na/gzhan yang gab pa gsad pa'i sbyor ba ni//'khor lo mu khyud rim pa gsum du bskor//rus sbal sbal gzugs gnyis lto ba stong par ni//rus sbal lto bar brtag pa'i ming rus b+hika de yis phyi ru gnod pa'i gdon rnams bri/
36b2(tsa): dug dang khrag dang tsha ba'i rdzas kyis byug/sbal ba lto sbyar phyi ru drag po sngags kyis bskor//thod pa steng mar nA ga stam nan bri//thod pa 'og mar nA ga raM yaM nri dza rbad zhes bri//'thing nag dril la rtsa bcas dong du gzhug/
36b3(tsa): ltar sngags bzlas mtha' ru ga nyan sa ra gnan//'bum phrag brgyad dang khri cha brgya bcu dbya//lu ma 'khyil ba'i dbu su nam gyi gung la gnan//lha srin kun gyi pho blang btab par bsam//drag sngags smod pa sbyor ba gsum
36b4(tsa): dang ldan//e ka ya ma snying khrag ra ma tsa//dgra bo'i srog la mA ra ya rbad//ya ma snying khra ga ra mau tsa e/dgra bo'i srog la nA ga duSh+TAn ste maM pa ya nan//dgra bo srog la gzir yaM b+h+yo//gzhan yang gnan pa drag po'i mtha' sduda?
36b5(tsa): gzhan gyi thod pa stobs can rnyed pa na//rang gi sbyor ba phyis la zha gsum thod pa gzir//khri tsho bskul gyi na rngam tshig drag tu bzlas//thod pa gdug la dmigs pa ming rus bri//gzhan gyi snying po tshangs pa'i tsa la bri//ming byang ru phas/
36b6(tsa): gyi bsnyen dang gsad pa bri//tha ma yang dgra drag pos gsang bar bri//thod pa kha khyer ram yaM baM gyis bskor//thod bar steng ma'i dbusu ma dang ni//de yi phyi ru brten bsgrub gsad pas bskor//nyin mtshan mtsham su brgya brgya drag tu bskul//tha ma yang dag drug
36b7(tsa): tu bskul//tha ma yang dag drug sngags rdzogs par bri//thod pa'i phyi nang dug dang khrag gis byug//bsgrub sngags brgya brgya drag sngags khri tsho re//nyin zhag bcu ru yul la bskul gyin nang//mi rgyud kla shad bcings la dang po'i gnasu gnan//gsang ba na
37a1(tsa): gnan la 'bru bzhi yang ka khri tsho drag tu drag po'i sngags kyis gzir//e ka ya ma snying khrag ra ma tsa/drag po'i srog la khram shing N+ya /ka/e ka ya ma ma ra/dgra bo'i srog snyam pa ya nan/thun thun ram rmugs
37a2(tsa): raM b+h+yo//drag sngags dug mda' thun gyi sbyor ba ni//gang la sbyor dang mngon gsum 'joms shing 'pho//de yang rten gyi thod pa gling mang su//thun khrag sna tshogs thod pa ming rus bcar//phye gsum thun gsum khrag sum brdeg pa'i rdzas//
37a3(tsa): skyes 'tsher bstan la thod pa dar gyis dpag//gsal ba zhag dgu smod pa dbab//brgya brgya bzlas shing bcu phrag la bstim//drag sngags bskul bas dug mda' khong du skyel//rtsa gcod khong khrag skams nas shes pa 'pho//sngags kyis
37a4(tsa): grangs las tshad kyis sbyor ba min//drag tu bskul gyi na thod khrag zor du 'bang //thod pa'i pra ngan drag po sngags kyis bzlog/lus skyod rngam tshig sgra dang bcas pas bya//drag pas bskyod cing rngam pas yul las 'pho//brag po sngags
37a5(tsa): gi sbyor ba 'di lta ste/e ka ya ma srog la dzi mun thun rbad b+h+yo//rnam drag rtsa la breng shag thun rbad b+h+yo//ra ma skying rnang hu r+ra yaM thun rbad b+h+yoka rtsa khrag khram shig thun rbad b+h+yo/gsad pa gau sum sbrel drag po sngags//gnyis sbrel mtha' gsuM
37a6(tsa): sbyor ba man//rig pas rtags la sngabs kyi sbyor ba blangs//rig pa gsum dang ldan pa lnga ru bya na/gas pa las kyi rim pa sde dang sbyar//sbyor ba gab pa'i snying po gsad pa yI//drag sngags mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud las//sbyor ba drag po'i
37a7(tsa): le'u ste bcu bzhi pa'o///gzhan yang lo thog gzhom pa'i sbyor ba 'di lta ste//dur khrod ras sa ma shog bu gru bzhi la//dbu su rus sbal lto ba stong par bri//'khor lo rtsibs chen bcus drag dang ldan//'dren pa'i 'bru bzhi 'gyu
37b1(tsa): pa'i 'bru lnga pa//dbab pa'i sngags bzhi chu dril zong thob gzing //rta mthong ba 'drag nyis gcig nga gsas//zhug lnga so lnga 'bru gsus pa la//rten gnyis 'bru gsug mi la gsus dang //rmu gnas yub ste ga gu zod sa sbyin/rgyo
37b2(tsa): gzhom phyir khrag dang dug gis gdag/dgu bdun lnga ru thu chung gsum dang sbyar/khri brgyad gang dmigs khri yi lam du gzhug dmigs pa gsum gyis gnas yul rten la sbyar/gu rgod 'khrug pa ri ru rjeb nas 'joms//drug
37b3(tsa): sngags sbyor ba bzhi ru brje ba//e ka ya ma sra ma srur ya baM//phreM phreM hri la hri la nyid 'dril phreM//hri la shar shar yaM yaM 'u ba hril b+h+yo//phro shar shar b+hi ta rag sha shar raM thun yaM shar pho ba//nA ga gzir nan dril pho ba//de ba yu nan gzir phob//
37b4(tsa): ma ma raM ga sha gzir phob//pi bi ya ma tsa shu la gzir nan hri la shar pho ba//'bras bu rtsi thog skye shing 'joms//lus dang yan lag 'dod par sbyar//drag sngags mngon du byung ba'i rgyu de las/lo thog 'joms pa'i le'u ste bco lnga pa'o//
37b5(tsa): gzhan yang bsrung ba'i sbyor ba ni//rim pa brgyad dang ldan par bya//gshig pa 'khor lo gzugs dang brda/de bzhin gdug pa gzir ba gsum//kha bcings rang gzir sbyor ba mchog/drag po sngags kyi sbyor ba khyad par gyis//gzhan gyi sbyor
37b6(tsa): ba gang shig cing gnod byed gzir ba'i las//rgya sho ga dag la gang gnod gzugs de bri//snying khar snying po tha ma sngags kyis bskor//ga'u so sor bcug la gru gsum rtsa nga bcas brnag/'thing nag dur khrod dar raM re bas btums//zhag sum
37b7(tsa): brabs la mtha' ru bka' rten gnan//gzugs la gshig pa'i sbyor ba dang po'i//dur khrod ras la snying po brda ming bri//sngags kyis bskor la dug su tsha ba bsku//la ma'i ga'u sbal ba'i lto bar bca'//zhagasum phun gzhir brgya dang
38a1(tsa): @#ru zag/thon nas du bas gdugs la se ng+ge'i khog par gnas/khardal shig pa'i sbyor ba gnyis pa'o//dur khrod ngas la thig skor gsum du bya//dbu su gru gsum meng bcas 'khrul mig dug lnga'i sngags//yang logs
38a2(tsa): rtsibs btsung gshig pa'i spu gri dgod//rus bral lto bar dug khrug tshabs bcu//kha sbyar gong bzhin thong ba ring du gzhug g+ha sgros rdzogs sngags bzlas yul la drag tu dang //drag po sngags kyi sbyor ba gcig gis bya/i ka ya ma g+has ma dza ni ngas
38a3(tsa): sarba raka hUM snyam pa ya nan 'drid zad/'khor los gshig pa'i sbyor ba gsum pa'o//gzhan yang klu gnyan sa bdag gdug pa'i nad dang na//rgya shog dag la gzugs br-i nA ga sngags kyis bskor kau/nad khrag dug khrag tsha bas zhing ngas dril//thod pa dro
38a4(tsa): du stob khrag zhag bdun brag/nye stong lha bsgoms gzir sngags mthal ru gnan/sa dang nA ga pu l-i mtha' ru thika brdal dang ming spel snying gzer sngags kyis bskor/gong bzhin skyer ba phag thong khyu mchog gnan//dung ma tshang bka' rten gnod cin
38a5(tsa): sa ru gnan//gzhan yang gdun pa gzing ba'i 'khor lo ni//dur khrod ras dang shog pu grub ni la/thig skor rims gsum dbu su sa rA ga/'a khrug mig bzhi gnyis kyi sku rtsa ba'i sngags//rtsibs brgyad sgyu gris gdug pa'i zhon chags gcod//rnam
38a6(tsa): la sogs khri gargyang bsnol khrag blag/thon nas gzing zhing mtha' ru gnan pa b+hU/e swA ya swA thai bA rA sa/sa nA ga snying gzeng sgrog le phyogs zad/sa nA gA snying gzer srur nan/gzhan yang gnad byed kha bocings pa//sbyor ba gzhan ming dz+ram
38a7(tsa): kyis bya//sarba tsit+ta gzer thu ba g+ha~M nan/gzhan yang stobs kyi bdag po nang gzor na//rgya shog gzugs byas lto bar 'khor lo bca pa/thig bskor gsum ldan dbusu yang da//'khrul mig bcul gshig pa'i spu gri dgod//mtha' ma'i dzu
38b1(tsa): rtsibs la gzer rtsa ba'i sngags//dug khrag brabs la rten gyi phur bas sdigs//rang nyid zil gnon dmigs pa gcer bung brtag/sngags bzlas dam brdar thun brab khrag gis brag/drag po thon nas zhag gsum cig na bye'i//sngags
38b2(tsa): sngags kyi sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e ka ya ma g+ha dza ni ra ya/e ya hur thum sha rdzu 'drid lang bshag sa ri rang gi lus bsrungs gzhan gyi sbyor ba dpral//mchog tu gsang ba rang gzir ming pa'i gnas//drag sngags mngon du byung ba'i rgyu
38b3(tsa): las/bsrung ba khyad par gyi le'u ste bcu drug pa'o///de nas yang bac+doMs kyis drag sngags dbang sdud kyi sbyor ba 'di gsung so//srid pa las kyi mtha' sdud du//dbang bsdud sngags kyi 'khor lo brtag/las kyi tshe bdag lte ba na/
38b4(tsa): srid pa sngags kyi 'khor lo gnas//de yi rim pa 'di lta ste//de yi//srid pa'i 'khor lo mu khyud lnga//lte ba chos kyi pho brang hUM//de yi phyi rim 'khrul mig bzhi//gsad pa drag po'i sngags kyis gang //gnyis pa 'khrul mig rtsa sbugs
38b5(tsa): brgyad //gnas pa'i ser ba'i sngags kyis gang //de yi phyir mig bcus la//yan lag sbyor ba'i drag sngags te//gshin rje ma mo bdud dang rgyal//zhi khro sri can gzir ba'i sngags//snying gzer gdug pa gzir ba'i sngags//pho mos ma ning
38b6(tsa): 'pho ba'i sngags//bcu sa las kyi skabs kyis gnas//'khrul mig bzhi brda' yis gang //kun 'byung rtsa ba'i 'khor lo yin//las kyi yan lag phral la ste//thun gyis gzugs la brdeg pa yin//gnan pa la sogs gong bzhin sbyor
38b7(tsa): a kro'i mtha' ru shis par gsungs//skabs dang sbyar la thun mongs spyad//g.yeng min bde ba'i nyams kyis brtags//je dang ldan na mchog tu sngags//sngags kyi dbye ba 'di lta ste//he ka he ru na rag sha zad //ya ma he sang rlag zad//kha rag tsasa?
39a1(tsa): rag sha zad/glu sa sU dzA ni rag+ha zad/thar kang sang ka ra ya dgra bo'i srog mA ra ya rbad /ya ma e ka ra dza 'dri ma nan/ma hA sad tsa tu ra nan pra ma sa ra nA gA thi ba ra nan/TI tri rA tsa mA ra ya rbad nan/e ka ba ka a ma ka ya sa
39a2(tsa): he sa pra ma khrag la phro phreM hI la shar drag tu phreM pro shar shar tri la b+h+yo//phreM shar shar ka ma da ri hri la phreM/sa nA ga tsit+ta ro myags pr-i pr-i/Shashar shar trag tu hri pa snying khe shag phog/e ka he ka 'dri me he kaM tri yaM he kaM b+hi sa ma sma dza ra kus ma dza ni/
39a3(tsa): dzaM rang sa de ka ra ya hUM phaT/i ka ya ma he kang 'dri ma ya rag mo reg kle ma b+h+yo//e ka ya ma he kang 'dri ma ya sarba tsi tu gzer gu big+ha nA na/e ka ya ma he sang sang kar ya dza ra~M sarba sha tru nan gzir kun b+h+yo/e ka ya ma dun b+h+yo/ka pu dza
39a4(tsa): ma sha truM sad ya kar ya thun/gzer cang b+h+yo/e ka ya ma ka dun dz+wa raM sad ka ra sha d+hu na gi zor thun rbang /da raM sha truM sad ka ra ya rbad/ya ma he sa pra ma khrag tsa tri sha truM raM thun zir zad/he sang ya ma sad ka ra ya/sa nA ga sha truM snying gzer hUMH
39a5(tsa): ya ma ka dun dz+wa raM sad ka ra ya/b+ha ti ya sha truM khaM nan thun gzir rbad/ya ma rag sha thun zad/e ka he ka ya ma nA ga snying gzer phun/tsa ba 'dri ma thi ba ra nA ga thun gzir rbad/e ka he ka nA ga sU ra nan/argya gA ru na nA ga ma ra ya nAn//
39a6(tsa): he saM thi ba ra nA ga sUr nan/stom pa ya nan/oM ka ya ma du ma thi ba ra nA ga thun gzir chad 'dri ma dud dza/he sang ya mA dum ya ma nri sarba sho na ya nan/de dag las kyi sbyor ba kun//don ldan thabs kyi rgyal pos byas//dbang srung sngags kyis/
39a7(tsa): sbyor ba kun/sku yo bsnyen ma mtha' du sbyar//drag por sbyor na rang la nyen//rig pa'i stobs kyis phyag rgyas 'phang //las rnams rtsa bar bril brda yin//drag sngags brgyad gnyis gcig gis rdzogs//drag sngags mngon du byung ba'i rgyud
39b1(tsa): nga srung rtsa ba'i le skye bcu 'dud pa la//de nas yang brjes kyis nges pa ngan 'dugs so/byur ba mi shes spyong byed pa/gnyis nadu ba'i sbyo pa sa skyes na//gas la ran mya bang lan sa gakus nga /
39b2(tsa): khyer brgyud pa'i bdud kyis zin//thabs lam gsang ba'i don dang ldan gyur na//snang srid chags pa'i spyod pas me 'bar kyang //gnyis med phyag rgyar grol ba'i 'bras bur bgrod//sgrol ba mi shes sgrol byed pa//skye shi med pa'i don dang gdeng
39b3(tsa): ldan na//khams gsum sen dus gcig srog cod kyang //chos sku bde ba chen po bgrod pa med par rtogs//spya med pa'i phrin las ma shes na//dpe la mtshon byed lus med spyod pa ni ka las kyi rnam smin dmyal ba'i btson rar tshud//rtsol ba
39b4(tsa): byas pas rtag pa'i bdud kyis zi ni//sbya du med pa'i phrin las spyod shes na//lha srin kun gyi ming dang mtshang smos nas//las la mngags nas pho nyar kun sprar kyang //blang dor med pas rol pa'i spyod par dag/bsnyen bsgrub rtsol med/
39b5(tsa): gas don ma shes na//phyi nang rtags la rten nas sgrub byed pa//mi shes srog la rtsol ba'i rang bdud rang la 'khor//ma nar med dmyal ba'i gnas la the rtsom med//bsnyen sgrub rtsol med sngags kyi don shes na//srid gsum mkha 'gro
39b6(tsa): 'dus pa'i srog mdangs ma//lha srin kun gyis srog snying dril ba'i mchog/drag po e dang yan lag sbyor ba ni ka/rdo rje'i bzlas pas yais rol par shar//gang mthong ci spyad lhun grub mtha' gnyis grol//na mkha' rin chen lhun po rgya
39b7(tsa): mtsho ltar//don gnyis smin pa yon lhun gyis grub//ma bcings grol med bgrod pa'i 'bras bur smin//snang srid 'khrul pa dug lnga'i smag rum du ka sangs rgyas bka'i drag sngags yis nyi ma shar//sngags kyis go mtshon drag sngags mi
40a1(tsa): garlung 'ang //yis rab las chos kyur gas pa//zug sngags mngos du byung ba'i rgyud las/rtsol da dang bral ba'i e se ts+tsha chung ba'o///de ngas yang rjes kyis mgo mgu ba'i sbya ba 'di gsung so//rnas grus b+h+yo/
40a2(tsa): ba bzhi ru bskor ka/rnam pa'i 'khor lo spogs pa gnyis ka log rtogs gzhom drung srung ba'o//rnal ma'i sbyor ba 'di lta ste//dur khrod gdug pa'i bde gnasaka dur khrod las la 'khor lo brika rme ba lnga dang tsha ba bskuka dbus nas thig skor ban ru
40a3(tsa): bskor//gru gsum zla kha ma lcags mkhar bzhika 'tshams bzhi rdo rje ka bas 'degs//thod pa'i rabs brgyad gnyis ldan//phyi ma'i lcags mkhar bcu drug la//mtshamasu rin chen ka ba de bzhin sa ri'i mtshams//de yi phyi rim sbrul
40a4(tsa): gdug chun pos bskor//srid pa rus sbal khra bo'i lto na gnas//de dag dgod cing gnas pa'i rim pa ni ka ra bu su brnag pa'i ming rus brika zla kham gsum la pha mes dur du song ba yi//de bzhin brnag par bya ba'i snag tshwa khrag gis bri//zla
40a5(tsa): kham gcig la bsrung ba'i lha rnam brika 'khrul mig bzhi la spu gri tshan bzhi dgod//de'i phyi rim 'khrul mig bcu drug la//drag po dbang sdud las kyi rgyal po nyid//khro bo rngam pa'i sgra dang bdud kyi snying po gnyis//gshin rje nyid dang ma mo snying po gnyis//
40a6(tsa): chags byed ma dang srin po'i snying po gnyis//shan pa btsan dang chu yi snying po gnyis//gshan pa las mkhan sgrol byed snying po gnyis//dregs pa can dang na mkha'i snying po gnyis//srog bdag pho mo gsod pa'i snying po gnyis//rang rang lcags mkha
40a7(tsa): ba'i gnas re nas ka/drag tu bskul gyis bshan pa dag la spang //'khor lo mtshon chen drag sngags med dpal drug/'khrul 'khor me rlung spu gri thig chen 'dis//lus shig brtsa gtsang khrag dang bag chags skems//shes pa brten dang dbang po
40b1(tsa): yul las 'byed//rten cig lus yang shes pa kun bsdus thim//kha sbyor 'khor lo thod pa ga pur gzhug/nyin mtshan dril gtor ming bcas spu kI sbyar//thod pa'i gseb du thun gyi spu gri dgod//ri rgyal lba ba dang gser gyi dgu thob
40b2(tsa): ltar sbrul gyi gseb tu gnan pa'i spu gri dgod//gser gyi sa bzhi ri rab brtan ltar bzlas//klu gsum nang du brnag pa'i rten rnams gzhan//'o thing nag kha gcad gsad po'i sbyor ba rtsis//brgyang ngam bcus dril gtor drag sngun
40b3(tsa): bzlas//sbyor ba thon nas thod pa gzir zhing bca'//ri rab pho brang gdug pa'i klu la ga ta da kanya stong kha bso thod brag dug bza' 'phang //drag po sngags kyi rmod pa 'di lta ste//e ka he dun ya ma rag sham dza mun tri//shaM tsang
40b4(tsa): kring shu snying //dgra bo'i srog la nri sha truM mA ra ya rbad/srog snyam pa ya nan/srog la nri shag rbad ya thun b+h+yo/me rlung sbyor ba las kyi mchog/rnal mas gsang ba'i sbyor ba yin//drag sngags mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud las/6
40b5(tsa): ba'i sbyor ba'i le'u ste bcu dgu pa'o////gzhan yang gsad pa'i sbyor ba ni/mchod sgyogs na lho dkrugs bca'//ras la thig skor bzhi ru bskor//gru gsum kham ming rus lha//'khrul mig bcu drug rtsa ba'i sngags//
40b6(tsa): yang logs bcu drug spu gri mtshon//tha ma sprul bzhir bas bskor//gcod cing gnon pa'i 'khor lo yin//blo sems 'chang ba drag po'i gnas//gdug pasreg byed las kyi me//lus sems rten 'brel 'gugs pa'i rlung //srog
40b7(tsa): dbugs 'chang ba 'brel mtshon chen dun/srog dang las sdung ze yi lcags//dbang po gnon pa ri bo sla'i//las sbyangs shes smin phyag rgya brta//gdug pa'i mtshod cha dang blung la//drag sngags s+ho sdug khri brgyan nyin/mcho ma
41a1(tsa): na mas panas bdas sa kur+dz+dzamA su ba smos/sa d+hA sa su sgyur phyu m+WA phyu sa
41a2(tsa): bgrod //drag sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste//e ka N+ye dun rag ga yA ma ts+tsha dza shu klong sbring //dgra bo'i srog la yongs thums shog zang //sbrus pa la gzir nin/smyig bye ma shig rdza mas phyogs zung b+h+yo//kunan yang las kyi sa /
41a3(tsa): ni/sprul pas lo thog gzhom pa las//dur khrod ris sam 'ashog bu la//thig bskor rim pa gsum du skor//dbusu gru gsum yul gyi ming //zla khams sbyor ba gnas pa bri//phyi rom 'khrul sog bcu drug la//rtsa ba'i drag sngags
41a4(tsa): rnal ma dgod//tha ma rtsibs chen nyi shu la//dbab pa'i spu gri drag po dgod//rus sbal lto bur sbyor ba sdu//kha sbyor thod ba zhab mas drilaka khrag sna dug snga sngon brten//sprul pa'i bsgoms kyin bskul la bzlas//sbyor ba thon nas d+ha ma gar+b+ha
41a5(tsa): drag sngagaso stong du rgyun mi bcod//ston zla gsum la dbab pa rtse ma//zor dang du ba thab dang gziraro//khra ga deng dug rdzas sngon du go/lcags phur drag sngags khri bzhir ldan//gdug pa'i rten dang gzugs la brdeg/dregaso ba kun gyi mthu dril ba'i
41a6(tsa): /sngags kyi rgyal po 'di lta ste//e ka he du na yaM maM shaM dzAM mun tri shaM raM kring shu snying //phreM phro shar shar hril thun b+h+yo//br-i phreM shar shar nA ga tur nan phreM phro shar shar hri la hril drag tu pro//nA ga dza yaM snying gzir tsit+ta nan phreM//shar shar rdzil b+h+yo//lo thog zhom
41a7(tsa): ba drag po'i las//bod pa stobs dang ldan pa'o//gzhan yang sprul pa'i tshe bdag gis//bsrung zhing gzir ba 'di lta ste//thad gcig tu dril ba'i las//shog gu gru bzhi cha gnyis lnga//'khrol 'khor rim pa gsum du bskor//sring
41b1(tsa): srid pa'i rus sbal lto bar bri//dbusu gru gsum gang gnod bri//'khrul mig bcu drug rtsa ba'i sngags//yang logs bcu bzhi spu gri rtsibs//las ni sna tshogs rtsa ba gcig zla kham me tog bsrung ba'i 'bru//bcu bzhi skod pa skabs mangs
41b2(tsa): khrag dang dug chu tsha ba sku//thod pa dong bcas sbyar ba rtsam//zhag bdun sngags bzlas drag du bskul//lha bsgoms bka' rten 'og tu gnan//sngags kyi rtsa ba gnyi su bya//bcu drug bcus rno rang shis//rig pa stobs dang brda yis sbyar
41b3(tsa): drag sngags sbyor ba 'di lta ste/e ka he dun yaM mAM ra shaM dzAM mun dri sha raM kr-ing shu/sarba du raM u nan gzer srung sbur nan srog la phyogs zad//drag sngags mngon du phyung ba'i rgyud lus/yan lag gi sbyor ba'i le'u ste/nyi shu pa'o//
41b4(tsa): de nas yang bac+doMs kyis drug sngags yongasu smin pa'i don 'di gsungaso//a ma chos sku byang chub sems//skye 'gags med pa'i ngo bor gnas//ma bcos bde chen lhun gyis grub//yis thig le dang la rdzogs//thabs kyi rgyal po rdo
41b5(tsa): yis rnga gsang ba yongasu rdzogs pa yi//drag po sngags kyi rmod pa gdams//kun tu bskyod pa gsum gyis sbyangaso//bde ba'i lam can gzhan las min//yais rigs pa snang bar sbyang//rgyud kyi rgyal po mngon phyung 'di ma 'ongs dam can srung byed
41b6(tsa): nAnunang yang dag las mi g.yel srung //'chi bdag dregs pa stobs can kun//dam tshig bka' yi 'khor lo srung //e ma'o//rig pa'i bka' rgyud 'di//bde ba'i lam can dag pa dang //ma sgribs lam la gnas sa dang //'chi bdag
41b7(tsa): tshogs kyi dge 'dun kun//rig pa'i lam la zhugs pa dang //'chi bdag dregs pa myos pa dang //'joms pa'i tshogs dang khrag skyugs tshogs//phung byed ma ngag pa gdug pa'i tshogs//'khor na gnas pa'i dam can kun//sangs rgyas
42a1(tsa): @#bka' yi rgyud 'di zung //gnyis med bgrod pa'i sa la gnas//rdo rje gtum po rdo rje'i tshogs//rje khro bo rdo khrag 'thung dang //bcud du sus sbrul rin chen phreng ba ltar//dam par sbyar la gnyen po gcig tu bsgrub//
42a2(tsa): dbang sdud phyag rgya chen po rang bzhin gzir//na mkha' rin chen rgya mtsho me dang 'dra ka dreg pa stobs chen phyag rgya brtan byas la//snying po bcus rtsa ba gcig tu bri ga/yan lag gang 'dod spu gri thams//gong rung sbyar na rin chen snying po 'bru/
42a3(tsa): bstan pa gsum ldan bde ba gcig gi ngang //snying po bcus mi 'gyur thig le la//bsrung ba'i rgyal po dmar len mtshon dang sbyar//gzir ba'i rgyal po dmar len mtshon dang sbyar//gzir ba'i snying po gang yin mtshamasu 'pho//rnam shes
42a4(tsa): 'phrog pa bcu 'bru gcig snying la bsdu//mtshon dpag dka' na skye med na mkha' 'bro ga/shes la rgyang ring sbyor ba'i tshad dang ldan//'bru brgyad rtsa ba gang dmigs spu gring ldan//'dod pa'i don bzhi snying po gcad nas dbrog/nye la gnod byed mtshon
42a5(tsa): cha rang gcod de//man ngag ldan pas bde bar spyad na rno//rangs sems stobs ldan yis rig pa'i rtsal//'ching med grol bar thar pa'i lam mchog 'bras//de med rang 'i cha nga mnar med sa las ring //thabs med spyod bral ldan
42a6(tsa): med bgrod ma thob//rtsa ba re dang yan lag brda' mi sbyor//sun 'byin grol med 'ching ba'i gnas las bgrod//blo yengs che ba'i yon bsam mi lang //grol ba'i phyag rgya yang dag 'phags lam ldan ka 'di de bde dang smin par byed pa'i
42a7(tsa): mchog kyi stsa d+ha mo mA ri/bai ra~M ba~M la~M tsA d+ha rI/kanA ga~M da ba rag ga sa/ sa ka mi bde ba'i klong //a dang kyi tshe sU b+hu ta/yis reg pa'i grong du brda la//'di dag bde ba'i lam can de//smin cing 'grod pa'i thabs mchog rgya ga/a yi
42b1(tsa): las byung ba'i//skye med rnam dag chos skur gsal//e yi yais las byung ba//drag po sreg byed nam khar gsal//sangs rgyas skyes pa'i cho 'phrulo//bde ba'i lam can snying po kun las 'das//nor bu gser ldan rin chen rgyal sa thog//
42b2(tsa): 'a yi bden pa bde ba'i ngang la bzhag/ces bac+doMs kyis gsungs pa la 'khor rnams rje su yi rang ste mngon par bstod do//'khor de dag gis bden pa mthong bar 'gyur to//drag sngags sngon du phyung ba'i rgyud las/rgyud yongasu gtad pa'i le'u
42b3(tsa): ste nyi shu gcig pa'o///dpal gshin rje'i rgyud khri drug stong pa nas/drag po rno dpal mtshon cha'i skabs bton pa'o//snying po san ngag gi rgyud rdzogs s+ho///bkra shis/mar+tha'aM/
43a1(tsa): rgya gang skad du/An+ya ma ma dzu shrI ya sAn trakA sarbA kA la k+Shu swA ta tA/yo gang du/'phags pa 'jas dpal gshing rje gshed dag pa'i rgyud/'jas dpa' gshed 'joms dran po la rgyan lam to//de
43a2(tsa): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dgyes pa chen po phul du byin te/phyogs bcu dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas thams cad tshogs nas/'jam dpal ya man ta kar dbang bskur nas/nga rgyal dregs pa can tha~Md 'dul ba'i phyir/gnas
43a3(tsa): cig tu tshogs nas/gshin rje nag po'i rgyud bka' stsal to//rgyud bka' bstsal pa'i le'u ste dang po'o///'phags pa 'jam dpal gshin rje gshed kyis skye 'gags dang bral ba'i 'di bsten to//ye nas ma
43a4(tsa): skyes gshin rje nag po'i tshogs//gnyis med 'dus pa phyogs bcu dus gsum gyi//sangs rgyas 'dus pa'i sras mchog gshin rje gshed//kun gyi yas 'dus pa'i//thugs rje chen pos 'gro ba'i don mdzad pa//'khor ba'i nyes
43a5(tsa): skyon thaMd mtshar gcod phyir//'dod chags 'dus pa'i dpal ldan gar gyi bdag/bye ba phrag brgya dpag tu med pa yang //tshad med dpag med gzhal du med/thun mongs ma yin 'jam dpal gshin rje gshed//sku gsung
43a6(tsa): thugs kyi 'phrin las rma du 'byung //zug rngu mi mnga' chos nyid rmad po che//mi dge bcu dang mtshams med tshar gcod la//chos kyi sku dang longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku//sprul pa'i sku dang gsung ni dam tshig dbyingasu sbyor//'khor ba'i don/
43a7(tsa): mdzad zug rngu mi mnga' ba'i//phyogs bcu bde bar gshegs pa kun gyi dpal//yang dag yis rang bzhin 'jas dpal yin//snying po gnyis med byang chub sems kyi don//de bzhin gshegs pa'i bu ti 'di bshad do//oM sarba ta thA ka
43b1(tsa): bus pa mnya' na sA hA//badz+rA ta thA ga ta/rang na ta thA ga ta pad+ma ta thA ga ta ka ra ma hA ma ga ta/zhes gshin rje nag po'aM tan tra las/le'u gnyis pa'o////de nas de bzhin gshegs pa zad kyi yum gyi tshogs rnams tshig tu
43b2(tsa): 'dus te/'di gsungaso//oM na mo sarba ta tha ga ta e ha te/ba ga ban ta tha ga ta pra ba ti swA hA/dar ma dar du e ha he/dza hUM baM ho hi hi hi hUM hUM hUM swA hA//zhes yum gyis tshogs thad kyis//phyogs bcu dus gsum gyi de bzhin
43b3(tsa): gshegs pa thaMd la//dgyes pa chen po'i brda' bskul te 'di skad do//e ma ho phyogs bcu dus gsum rdzogs pa'i yis 'dus pa'i 'phrin los mchog/yis 'phrin las rmad po che//sbyor ba mchog gi mchod par gzhes zhes/
43b4(tsa): gsungs pas//de nas phyogs bcu dus gsum gyi//de bzhin gshegs pa thad dbang phyug gi 'khor mya ngan 'das pa thaMd dang /bcas nas/sku che ba'i ston gyis rgyan te//sau'i btsun mo la dgyes pa chen po nyam su btang bar gyur to//
43b5(tsa): de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs las bzhengs te//'di skad ces gsungaso//de bzhin gshegs pa mchod pa'i phyir//yum chen gsang ba'i bde mnyam la//mi 'gyur don gyi mchod pa bzhes//sku
43b6(tsa): gsungs thugs kyi dbyings 'gyur cig/a la la ho//nu ma sarba ta thA ga ta/ba ro ya po te tsi ta kha ra ya mi//zhes bgyis pa chen po phul du byon to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi chos thaMng don dam par khro bor bstan te//
43b7(tsa): 'di skad do//khyad par mi bar bstan pa'i 'khro gnyer ni//yis dbyingasu rtogs pa khro bo'i pang //khro bo brgyad dang dgu dang stong rtsa gcig/stong mchong phyir ni khri klog ltar 'gyur//khwa dang spyang khu srin po su stegs dang //bdud dang log
44a1(tsa): @'dren tha~Md tshar gcad phyir//phyogs bcu kun dus khro bo chen po bcu//tshad med bzhi'i yis khyad par las//snying rje chen pos 'gro ba'i don mdzad phyir//chos nyid sgyu ma lta bu'i 'phrin las mdzad//khro bo
44a2(tsa): thugs rje chen po rna rol//chos rnams thaMd byang chub sems kyi ngang //yai sa tshan po'i yon 'byung ba 'di yino//oM b+ha gi ka ri mung dang drug/yang na rigs lnga 'jam dpal dang drugo/sbrel ba'i gshin rje dang bcu'o//yais zung
44a3(tsa): du tshim pas bcu'o//mi mnyam chos rnams skye ba'i chos can yang //'byung ba lnga dang yis dkyor brgyar//a A i I/rI rI/li lI/oM au/aM a/'khor dang ldan pa'i dkyil 'khor 'dir//de bzhin gshegs pa
44a4(tsa): 'byung ba yi//so so'i gzhugs gnas ji bzhin du//'khor lo bcu drug rtsibs stengs la//yig 'bru'i tshul du dgod bar bya//rnar 'dren pa dang bya'o//sngags dang phyag rgya rdzas rnams dang //ga'u kha sbyar de min na//kham rdzas
44a5(tsa): dang ni 'chi ba'i//rag ta gsum dang 'ag+hi ha ngo //tshon skud kha dog dmar po yis//kham rdzas kyi ni ga bu bcing //'phrin las rnam pa bzhir bya'o//byang chub sems kyi thig chen sbyar bya'o//'phrin las rnam pa gzhi dang ni//'og nas
44a6(tsa): 'byung ba'i don du shes par byaka gshin rje nag po'i tan tra las//'phags pa gshin rje gshed 'byung ba'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi sku gsung thugs btsun mo'i bdag nyid du gyur te/
44a7(tsa): sa kr-i ta ba ta na/u tsun b+ha ga sarba dang ma tsa tsar+ya e ta ba hr-i hUM/a la ya ya a bo ta/ta thA ga ta dze ti shwa ri/a pa ta hUM/badz+ra kro ti shwa ri traM dang ma ko ti sho hi hri bad+ba kr-i ti sho ri hi ha haM/zhes 'byung ste/de nas ra ba
44b1(tsa): gshegs pa tha~Md kyis//thun mong gnas 'byung byed kyi dkyor bstan te//skyed rdzogs rgyan dang cha lugs yab kyi sku bzhin bskyed bya ste//'khor lo bcu drug mu khyud rdo rje'i ra ba dang //gru chad par 'khyams gsum dang de bzhin du/spyod
44b2(tsa): mthun 'gro ba'i snying rje yum skye de//rigs kyi yab dang rigs kyi btsun mo ni//rtags dang mthar phyin de bzhin gshegs pos bya//rdzas dang sngags dang phyag rgyar bcas pa rnams//gzugs dang 'bras bu go 'phangs sbyar//kha dog
44b3(tsa): tsha zhing lus 'ongs che ba dang //rgya gad ma chad yongasu nam mkha' sgo gsal ba'i shes rab thad na mkha' 'dra//yang a ti 'odi 'byin mthar phyin pas//lung dang man ngag gnyis la mkhas par bya//gnyis med sbyor ba thad du//
44b4(tsa): sa pa skong zhing dbang du 'dus pas ni//mi dmigs gtso bo'i sngags dang phyag rgyu ni//gnas pa de ya skra bskus pa//sgrol dang mthar phyin 'phrin las gnas pa bstan//zhes 'byung ste/gshin rje nag po'i tan tra las/
44b5(tsa): 'byung byed yas dkyor bstan pa zhes bya ba te le'u bzhi pa'o////de bzhin gshegs pa thad kyi yai sa 'byung ba'i gnas bstan pa'o//gzhan gyi phyir rig pa dang //rnar dag pa dang /ji srid mtsho'i bar yis rnar dag pa dang /
44b6(tsa): gnod pa sel ba'i yis nor dag pa dang //rgol ba tshar gcod pa'i shes rab dang //mu stegs bya ka ra na sgra la mkhas pa//tshur gcod pa'i shes rab zlar dag pa dang //mi mthun pa'i phyogs mtha' dag zil gyis gnon pa'i shes
44b7(tsa): rab rjer dag pa dang brgya do//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs btsud mo'i bdag nyid yi su gyur pa dang /yis kyi las kyi ting 'dzin bdag du gyur te/'odi ska do//khro de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting 'dzin dang //
45a1(tsa): chos nyid sgyu ma la 'gro bya'i ting 'dzin dang //shes rab kyi 'dod chags sgrol ba'i ting 'dzin dang //goms pa chen po sbyor ba hA gyi ting 'dzin dang //byang chub kyi sems la gnyi su med pa'i ting 'dzin dang /
45a2(tsa): rjesu bstan pa'i ting 'dzin dang ka/thugs chen po zhe lnga sgrol ba'i ting 'dzin dang //shes rab kyi 'dod chags sgrol ba'i ting 'dzin dang //yon gyi nga rgyal sprul ba'i ting 'dzin dang //'bad pa med pa'i brtson 'grus kyi phrag
45a3(tsa): dog 'joms pa'i tinge 'dzin dang //thub pa'i bdud 'joms pa'i tinge 'dzin dang //prad nya 'gu ya/gnya' na 'gu ya/'bu ta 'gu ya/badz+ra 'gu ya/rang na 'gu ya/pad+ma 'gu ya/kar ma 'gu ya/sarba bu ta 'a pu ta 'gu
45a4(tsa): ya ho/de nas khro bo de bzhin gshegs pa tha daM rigs kyi ting 'dzin te/de ltar bstan nas de bzhin du smra so//dper mo bzhi no//'od dang sbrang 'du ba dang 'dra'o//me lce'i 'od ni 'di la yod/'od kyis sbrang ma tshigra pa de bzhin
45a5(tsa): dud skyon gyi dri mra thad dang /bdud dang mu stegs thaMd mi mthun pa'i dgra'o rnams//shes rab kyis gzir ma non pa'i//me lce dang ni sbrang ma'i tshul//tha dad pa'i mi mthun pa'o//'phags pa 'jam dpal gshin rje gshed
45a6(tsa): 'khros pas nga med sgrol bar byed//mi skye yaM sa ral gri y+r-ika/nyon mongs sdug bsngal dra ba nas//gnas nas gnas ma yin te//brgyan dang dgu ni ma yin pa//nyon mongs sen mtho ris nas//bsgral ba'i zhing bcur smos pa de
45a7(tsa): de la de bzhin du byas na//rigs dang lo zla mi mthun pas//de bzhin dag tu byar mi rung //skyon gyi rnam pa de yod pa de//de bzhin du ni shes par bya//las dang bya ba rnams la yang //nyams kyi phyag rgya rang ngo sad//de rnams/
45b1(tsa): dag par mi 'gyur te//me'i phyogs dgu go dgos la//gyi rkang dag gis byas la nAM/wA rag suT gzugs pa waM//khyad par dag ta kha lus zan//sgrub pa drug gi snying po zla//de nas sngags 'di ya de/oM
45b2(tsa): kr-i ta 'bri kr-i ta ta mA ra ha pras he he ma ra tra sa he he/de skad gsungs te//dgra dong bgegs dang mu stegs dang //bdud dang srin po ma lus rnams//gzir cing mnan pa de bzhin//de nas 'jam dpal gshin rje nag pos//zugs
45b3(tsa): mnyam pa nyid kyi dkyor nas//bdud dang srin po mu stegs dang //logs 'dren thad rang bzhin gyis//bsgral ba'i snying po 'di gsungaso//oM pra ma ra na mo pras badz+ra kyi mngon de a pra shu ku 'bras ta ma 'bras ku ma ma ta mu tri/
45b4(tsa): snying po de skad gsungs ma thag tu//rang bzhin gyis dbang du 'dud par gyur te//bdud rnams ngag ni 'phro zhing gtum//srin po dag ni 'jigs shing rngam//mu stegs log par byed pa la//de dus 'phrin las 'di gsungaso//dkyir
45b5(tsa): pad+ma 'dab brgyad laka/de'i dbus kyi lte ba la//bdud 'dul snying po 'od dgod do//a nan ta ka b+ha ma ra nan//shar gyi pad+ma la skyin po dbang du bsdus pa ci snying po dang //oM rag sha khrag nan//lho mu stegs dbang du bsdu ba'i
45b6(tsa): /snying poka tir ka nun/nub tu bgegs dbang du bsdu ba'i//oM big ma ra ya nan//byang du log 'dren dbang du bsdu ba'i snying po//oM ka ri nan/mtshams kyi pad+ma bzhi lnga//oM d+ha hUM/b+ha hU~M/ma hU~M/b+ha d+h+r-i/grwa chang bzhi la zed bya
45b7(tsa): lcang lo tsan re bri'o//mu khyud bzlum po la 'phreng ba btang //de'i phyi rim la sbrul bri//de ltar dkyir bcas pa ni//rnar ma tshe ba'i gzugs rnams kun//kham pa dag gi ga'u la//rnar mche ba'i thugs kyang bri//dog pa dmar pos
46a1(tsa): @bcings nas sra/dkyi dasus dgod bya ste//rtse gcig phyag rgya bcas nas ni//sngags 'di mang du brjod par bya//na mo dr-i khaM kha na/dang pa rba tsi Asa? te mi nan sa dang //phyag rgya 'di yis reg par bya/zhal gsus/
46a2(tsa): zil mnan byas nas nika/bgegs dang log 'dren dbang bsdus te//khro bo dang ni zhus pa rnams//dkar dang dmar dang ser ba dang //spug nag ljang nag kham nag dang /so cha lugs sdus pa'i gzugs//mgon po gzhon rje nag po yis
46a3(tsa): de dag tshogasu sprul nas ni ka bgegs dang log 'dren gzir zhing gnan//rngam pa'i zhal du 'di bstab so//oM che ge mo kha raM/hri ti che ge mo khe raM/hUM hUM hUM/phaT phaT phaT/nam/nyin zhagacig la stong rtsa cig bzla so//nyin mtshan
46a4(tsa): gnyis la khri rtsa gcig bzla so//nyin mtshan gsum la 'bum rtsa gcig bzla so//de nas rtsa ba'i phyag rgyas gsum bcas te//yang nas yang du gnan cing gzir bar bya ste//be btson gyi phyag rgya btsas nas//sngags 'di yang
46a5(tsa): gdab par bya'o//oM sti pU dz+ra yA ya ya na ba rA s+ya phu du hUM hUM hUM/phaT phaT phaT/zhes stong rtsa cig bzlaso//las rnams rdzogs dang sngags dang phyag rgya dang //brtson 'grus ma yengs yang dang yang du bya//zhes bya ste gshin rje nag po'i tan
46a6(tsa): tra las//ting 'dzin gyi dkyor bri zhing mnan pa'i le'u ste lnga po'o//ka ye shes lhun gyis grub pa'i dgyes pa chen po'i ngang le longs spyod par ston ste/'phags par rang bzhin gnas pa shes rab te/shin tu khro bor gyur nas
46a7(tsa): gsungs pa'i//nga ni dus gsum mngon du phyung //nga ni dus gsum phyis kyang byung //ra ni dus gsum da ltar byung //bstan pa thad nub pa'i tshe//lnga brgya tha ma byung ba'i tshe//bstan pa tha nub pa'i tshe//nga'i bstan pa dar bar byed/
46b1(tsa): las ngan mtsham med byed pa'i me//de tshe ba'i bstan pa zung //zas ngan lci ba za ba'i cha//de tshe nga'i bstan pa 'byung //gos ngan re ba gon pa'i dus//de mo nga'i bstan pa 'byung //bstan pa zang chub pa'i//de ma pa'i bstan btsun
46b2(tsa): ji ltar bu ni zhi 'gyur yang /nga ni tha daM zhi ba'i sku/ji lta bu ni 'khro 'gyur na//nga ni thA daM 'khro ba'i sku//thA daM nga'i rang bzhin yin//ma lus tshar gcod don 'di gsungs//'khor lo rtsi khas rgyad mu khyud zlum por bcas//sbrul gyi rgya
46b3(tsa): graM dag gis mtha' skor te//de ltar rgya gr-i bri bar byaM//'khor lo brtsi ba brgyad rdo rje rgya graM bri bar bye//de'i steng du ban d+ha ga'u kha gnyis la//cigs gshin rje 'bru drug pa'i gzugs bya//de'i dmigs pa'i rjes kyis saM dang dri za dang /
46b4(tsa): khrag sna gsum dang //thal chen rgya shug bskus pa'i khu ba dang //de rnams bsreg pas gzugs byas la//ga'u yi nang du bcug la kha sbyar nas dog pa dmar pos bcings ste//dkyor gyi thogasu bzhago//de nas lha'i sku phyag rgya

47b2(tsa): tshur nang du ni//'the bong bcug ste be con rdeg/sngags 'di yang brjod par bya//oM na mo bu d+ha d+ha ra ma sang ge bya//oM sarba dA ta thA ga ta/kro ta bi dza yA/ag dz+wa la/tA tsi ta pri ya hUM phaT/ni gang la dmigs pa grub//gshin rje gshed po'i tanaH

48b3(tsa): ba sel ba de//bdud dang mu stegs tha daM kun//byin brlabs chen pos btul na su//bdud nga srin po gdan du bA ti de//gshin rje pho nya la sogs pa//sgrol ba'i pho nyar byin gyis brlabs//bdud dang srin po'i chung ma rnams//dbang du

49a1(tsa): las khyab pa'i rtsa rang thugs rje che//gsang pa'i bdag nyid gsher rdeg med//ces gsungs pas//phyogs bcu'i bye par gshegs pa zang shis tu rnam pa'i nas nas//shAdi skang gas so//e ma byas

49a3(tsa): log 'dren tha kyis//dbang bskur gnang ba des kyang gzi byon skyed//shar phyogs dri za log 'dren zhi 'gyur zhing //oM badz+ra ku la a sho ra na ha ha/ho ho hUM hUM zhes rdo rje rigs kyi bde bar gshegs pa thaMd kyis//mgon po gshin rje nyid la dbang

50a1(tsa): zong 'phreng gi phyag rgya brtsa la la//sngags 'di nyin cig ci nus bar du bzla so//oM pa yu pra sem gyi sa ra bi tsam le se mo ma ra ya ya ti hUM phaT/de nas 'phrin las rnam pa ni/rA ru gtsang ma'i nang du/dag po yi ri
50a2(tsa): dang gzugs gsum bcug ste//sngags 'di ci mang du bzlas gdab po//oM badz+ra ka la 'bri ki te he du b+h+yo sa ma na ti ra ti che ge/mo ma ra ya phaT/de nas yang mnan pa'i las bstan pa ni//bud med kyi/dor rta'i nang du dgra bo'i gzugs bcug

50b1(tsa): zhes pa ni las kyi khonanan no//de rnams mam par byed//las dang mchog sems skyed par bya//de nas sdang bar byed pa'i dga'o la/ming rus dri ma rkang rjes la//glang mchog rab tu gas bcing krug par bya/rag ta dgu dang gra
50b2(tsa): /rnams//li la yi la khu ba dang /tsi ta ka'i 'bras bu rnams//de la dgra bo gzugs byas la//las dang brtson 'grus dgyes pa bsgrub//gsad cing gnan pa'i thabs//gro ga ras sam shing shun la//ngomigs pa gzugs ni ba
