67b3(tsa): gzer chen btab pa bzhin//bsgo mo//de nas hUM ni gshen rje'i rgyal po te//lag na gsod pa' mda' gzhu thogs/bsgral ba'i zhing bcu ma las 'joms pa las//zhes sdang gnasu 'joms pa yin//gzhu mchog yar bstan gshin rje
67b4(tsa): gshed po yin//gzhu mchog mar bstan yum mchog bsgrol byed ma//rgya 'dud byang chub sems kyi ngo bor bstan//tshangs zungs byang chub sems kyi khong lung bdud rtsi snod//gzhu rgyud ru tra bsgral ba'i rgyu ma yin//bshams ni skyes
67b5(tsa): mchog don dang ldan pas bshams//gnyen ni dgra dang bgegs kyi nyan du che//yu ba gnyis med rol pa'i mkha' dbyings nas//sprul pa ya ma ra tsa mda' zor 'phen//dgra bgegs bgrol ba'i lag cha yin//stong ni thabs dang /
67b6(tsa): shes rab ste//gnyis med sbyor ba'i phyag rgya yinaka mde ru ni kha chung bting za ba te//tsi ta'i dkyil du rtog pa yin ka ya ma ra tsi'i skyer mda' la//ma mo tshogs kyi sgo cig skyon//srin mo tshogs kyi rgyud cig dkris ka byang khub sres
67b7(tsa): kyai sring du cig bcug/srid pa gsum gyis sbyin lus byugs /'ben pa gzas pa'i dgral 'phen//ga shis rjenas po'i thugs kyis mig/khe nya rag po'i thugs mdang 'ad//gnyis med bu la li khrel ba'i/ya sa tso
68a1(tsa): @mnga' thun 'di ka rgyob cig dgra bo'i snying gi dkyil du rgyog//oM dun ting he ya ma ra ya te//pu ti baM nan ra tsa su sa ma klo ti ni nan//hUM nag mo mig rnon lta gdong ma//khyim cig 'khor ci shwa la ba/
68a2(tsa): nag mo chen mo'i mig 'bru ma/tshe gsum dran byed rgyu ma 'dren cing za//dgra bo'i dmigs 'bru bya rog ma chu ka/'bras bu lung zhig kha kha ma ka oM kha ba kra kri ma ba ya hUM phaT/ri bong gi dmigs la brde go/hUM 'phra men
68a3(tsa): khra'i mgo bo can thogs med thu ldan 'joms pa'o//gsod byed chen mo hor pa mo//chu ma cig hor mo chen mo 'joms pa mo//chu me cig dam nyams dgra la thim//dus la bab po hor mo gcang byed ma//dam nyams
68a4(tsa): ga la mda' zor ya nga //hUM 'phra men 'ug pa'i mgo can la//ltos ngan rgyal mo'i dus la ba ba//thun mong 'ug pa'i dus la bab ka/snod byed thugs kyis stong khams khya ba//'jigs pa'i kan sgra phyogs bcur brdegs
68a5(tsa): 'jigs pa'i 'u sgra 'di ri ri//sprul pa'i gshog sgra shar ra ra//'jang la phob cig 'og mchu ma nyams pa'i srog la sa ma ya//oM lu mu kha ba ha ba ha bka/ hu ba hu ba phaT/hU bde bar gshegs pa'i mda' mchog dam pa
68a6(tsa): 'di/dgra 'grong ltar 'gying ba'i snying gi dkyil du ma da' thun rgyob//sku'i phyag brgya sbrel pa las//g.yu'i mda' skra sha ra ra//lcags kyi skra 'u ru ru //dbang po lnga la mnga' thun yang /mig gis ltas pas zhe sdang
68a7(tsa): skyes//zhe sdang btsad nas bcad pa'i phyir//dmigs pa'i dkyil du mda' thun ya/gru gsum gdengs pa'i dkyil 'khor nas//'jigs byed 'bar ba'i skur snang ba'i//'dod chags tsad nas bcad pa'i phyir//gsang ba'i
68b1(tsa): dkyil du mdar thun ya/ ya ma ra tsa'i mda'i mchog dam pa 'di//nyan par byed pa rnams yin//rna ba'i dkyil du mda' thun yaka gru gsum bdengs pa'i dkyil 'khor nasaka sku ni rngam brjid chen mo las//thugs
68b2(tsa): kyi sprul pa mda' mchog dam pa yi//spyi bo'i gtsug du mda' thun ya//mda' 'di 'phrin las ngang nyid dbyingasu 'bigs//smra bar byed pa kha yin pas//kha yis smras gti mug gsal bar bya ba'i phyir//kha'i dkyin
68b3(tsa): du mda' thun ya ka lus kyis byugs pas phrag dog skyes//phra dog tsad nas gcad par bya ba'i phyir//spyi bo'i gtsug du mda' thun ya/hUM mda' ni 'phrin las dbyingasu 'bigs//sbyin ni byang chub sems kyis 'dzin
68b4(tsa): pa'i don//sgo ni sku gsung thugs kyi sgo//'asde'u ni gnyis med dang dbyingasu 'bigs//gzhu ni shes rab thab su ldan//rgyud ni yais 'od du sbreng //gsang ba thugs kyi mtha' mchog 'di//rgyob cig dgra bo'i snying la rgyab//
68b5(tsa): 'phongs cig ya mtshan la 'phongs//rgyob cig dam nyams dgra la rgyab//dang po srid pa lte bar chags//srid pa chags pa bshig pa'i phyir//lta ba'i dkyil du mdar thun ya//'dod chags drungs nas dbyung ba'i phyir//gsang /
68b6(tsa): ba'i gan su mda' thu na yaka au su na ga ga sha kha lo mu lo 'di sod//oM maM bai ru da na na ma baM/ru na mug tsa ya rbad/a la ru lu tshal rbad/au sa du dung rA ru pa nan/srad de ro mnyam dAm khrams rag ta tsi ra pha Ta/de lta
68b7(tsa): gyi thabs ma nor ba rtags myur du 'ong ba la the tsom medo//gshin rje nag po'i tan tra las//ya sa ra tsa thugs kyi mngar thun gyi le'u ste/lde drug pa'o///de nas dpal ga shin rje gshed kyis//man ngag gsang ba'i phur thun
69a1(tsa): 'di gsung so//dur khrod chen po'i gnasu//dgying 'bring nag glu gsum brtsig/dmigs pa'i ling ka bcas na suka ram rdzas yo byang chang bar dgras//lcags kyi phur ba sor brgyad pa//byin gyis brlabs te tshul
69a2(tsa): bzhin dgodaka/'phrin las cho ga 'di gsungaso//dbus phyogs gru gsum 'bar ba'i dkyir na//'jigs byed ya ma ra tsa gsheg stsol//yum chen srin mo mgo 'phyangs ma dbyings nas bzhengs//thugs kyi sprul pa khro bo mi g.yo
69a3(tsa): mgon//dbyug mtho 'bar ba lha mchog srid pa gsum gyi lha//gshin rje mchi bdag dbang po kun 'dul ba//kha dog nag po bdud phung ral gri can//srin po ma he sta gzigs gzhon nga su ka ral dri srid pa gsum po thal bar klog/srid
69a4(tsa): gsum 'dul ba'i lha la phyag 'tshal bstod//sku mdog nag po gshin rje gshed kyi lhag/ci bgyis bka' nyan ma mo 'khor gyis bskor//ma mo thod stong sdang ba'i dgra la gtong //sdigs 'dzub 'bar bas dgra bo'i srog rtsa gcod//sdang mig dgrad pas//
69a5(tsa): gshin rje'i tshogs rnams 'dul//kan sgra brdabs pas srin po bran du 'khol//srin po 'phos pas khrag mtsho khong du rngubaka skyug gru blangs pas ma me dgra la gtong //dug gsum zhal du rngubs la phyag 'tshalo//zha ba 'og gshin rje'i srin
69a6(tsa): po mang po kun//dun dun gum gum sdang ba'i dgra la gtong //gshin rje gshe du khrod te rngam pa yin//srin po pho mo don snying sngar gyis 'debs//gdug pa'i srin po 'phos te rgyug pa yis//ma mo stong gi don snying sbar gyis 'debs//gdug pa'i
69a7(tsa): /ma mo 'khros te rgyugs ya yis//sdang ba'i dgra bo don snying sngar gyis 'dabs//dmigs pa che ge mo la phur thun ya//oM ya man ta ka bsham//ar+ya a tsa ma la bsham//ma dug tshal ba zad ma dug bsham//a man gsham//
69b1(tsa): che ge mo snying tsa la mug ti rbad/tsa tsa ra rbad ma mo khrag la don te rngubs//phur pa cig sku slong khro bo mi g.yo mgon por bsgom//bdag nyid dus gsum du bdab bka chu srin gyi kha nas chang bdud kyi bu mo khram gyi bdag mor bsgom mod
69b2(tsa): de nas bskul tshig 'di brjod las yum gyis bdag po//hUM sku mdog dmar nag zangs kyis dril zhags can//ral ba sprugs pa'i sku gseb ri nas//ma mo nag mo lnga bcu rtsa bzhi dag la gtong //ljags kyi phyag zor 'bar ba 'gras
69b3(tsa): papa yis//gshin rje nag po dun dun dgra la gtong //khro mo sdigs 'dzub phyar te 'dabs pa yis//ma mo nag mo dun dun dgra la gtong ka/srin mo 'bum khri 'bum stong thal bar glog/dgyes ste gad mo gra du 'dabs pa yis//dgra
69b4(tsa): bo'i dam nyams gtan du glag par mdzod/'go 'changs ma dza dza /tsa bo tshe dug mugs/nang grol smad lnga la tsa ba ba/thum ri ri li li/badz+ra ki la ya che ge mo ma ra ya/'phrin las 'di bsgrub pa'i dusu//rmis lta su mon mo
69b5(tsa): nag mo dang /byed nag mo dang 'phrad nas bdag la bkur sti byed na kyang las grub pa yin//dang po gnas dben par 'phrin las gzhung bzhin du btsugs la//dmigs pa'i ling ka bcug nas ni//mda' 'ug gu ma cig gi sgro rtsar bug gu/
69b6(tsa): phug la//ming byang dang rnal mor shi ba'i mngal khrag dang //ra dug dang sbyar dug dang bo nga nag po dang //blugs la dmigs pa bkug te//ling ka la rdeg go//dbus phyogs kham nag gru gsum 'bar ba'i dkyor nas//'jigs byed ya ma ra/
69b7(tsa): tsa gshegasol//yum chen be ta nag mo dbyings nas bzhengs//'jigs byed sku las sprul pa'i bka' nyan pho nya'i tshogs//bka' sdod bcu'i 'phrin las dus la bab//steng gi phyogs nas las mdzad pa//gshin rje'i rgyal po thaMd
70a1(tsa): 'joms pa'i rje//su mdog 'bring nag 'jigs pa'i gzugs/tshags kyi rmu gas pas tshugs//rgya ga btsun brgyad po byur 'amed//A pa khas dag rkyen du ba/rgyag sa gcod brtul ga/skye
70a2(tsa): dgra go khrag las 'gog/oM shags tshal ba zad bu nan//rashar gyi phyogs nas 'gugs mdzad pa//gshin rje'i rgyal po 'chi bdag kun gyi rje//sku mdog dkar po 'jigs pa'i gzugs//phyag na skye 'gro'i bcis byung bsdam sa//ral ba
70a3(tsa): popo gyen du 'bar//srog chags rangs pa grangasu bgrang //dbus phyogs thaMd rtsi su 'jugs//shar nas dgra bo khrag las shog/oM dun dun rbad bu nan/shar lho mtshams nas 'gugs mdzad pa//gshin rje'i rgyal po dmigs pa ni//sku mdog/
70a4(tsa): dud kha 'jigs pa'i gzugs//phyag na mi mgo rlon pa'i phreng ba bsnams//shar lho'i dgra bo khug las shog/oM kha ra ti tshal pa rbad bu nan//lho'i phyogs nas 'gugs mdzad pa//gshin rje'i rgyal po kong rtse brag nag zur mangs gso//
70a5(tsa): phyag na srin po 'dul mdzad dgra sta bsnams kalho nas dgra bo'i khug las sho ga/oM na ma she ga ti nan/rbad bu nan/lho nub mtshams nas 'gugs mdzad ma//gshin rje'i rgyal po chos kyi glang mgo can//sku mdog 'thing nag 'jigs pa'i
70a6(tsa): gzugs//phyag na lcags kyu lcags sgrog bsnams//lho nub mtshams kyi dgra po khug la shog/oM ma bzhag ti nan bu nan/nub kyi phyogs nas 'gugs mdzad pa//gshin rje'i rgyal po gsod byed ya ba ti//sku mdog dmar nag 'jigs
70a7(tsa): pa'i gzugs//g.yas na zhing gi dbyug pa bsnams//nub nas dgra bo khrag las shog/oM khaM waM so ba bad pu nan//nub byang mtshamasu nas dgra bo 'gugs mdzad pa//gshin rje'i rgyal po lcogs pa mgo dgu'i rje/sku mdog ngas
70b1(tsa): nag 'jigs pa'i gzugs//phyag na gsod byed spu kri bsnams//nub byang mtshams nas dgra bo khug las shog/oM shi so la kha rga na zad bu nanA//byang gi phyogs nas dgra bo 'gugs mdzad pa//gshin rje'i rgyal po gsod byed
70b2(tsa): rngam pa'i sku//sku mdog ljang nag 'jigs pa'i gzugs//phyags na gsod pa'i sta ri bsnams//byang gi phyogs nas dgra bo khug las shog/oM ti tshal ba zad bun nas//byang shar mtshams nas dgra bo 'gugs mdzad pa//shin
70b3(tsa): rje'i rgyal po gsor byed rje//sku mdog ljang nag 'jigs pa'i gzugs//phyag na nyes legs khram shing bsnams//byang shar mtshams nas dgra bo khug las shog/oM tsa la khr-i ti rbad bu nan//'og gi phyogs nas dgra bo
70b4(tsa): 'gugs mdzad pa//gshin rje'i rgyal po bdud khams rje//sku mdog smug nag 'jigs pa'i gzugs//phyag ni brdung byed thob bsnams//'og nas dgra bo khug las shog/oM 'dar ru rtsubs phran bu nan//zhes phyogs 'tshams
70b5(tsa): nas bkug la brdeg/tha ma dgra bo'i phyogasu 'phangs//gshin rje nag po'i tan tra las//ya ma ra tsa gsang ba thugs kyi zor thun gyi le'u ste rje brgyad pa'o///da nas dpa'o 'dzams pa yin//'phrin las khrag thun
70b6(tsa): 'di gsungs//sngon du cho ga gzhung las byung ba bzhin du bshams ka ga yag po zan ma'i ga yag ru ru//rag ta phud dang mtshe dang nyung dkar blug/dmigs pa'i ling ka gtor mar gzhug/ga ya ga ru lag tu thogs nas ni//thun
70b7(tsa): gyi sngags brjod cing bskul 'di skad brjod//hUM dmar nag gru gsum 'bar ba'i dkyor 'dir//gsod byed la ma ra tse gsheg sol//dmar brjod 'bar bas ma mo kun gyi rje//khro gtum drag srid pa gsum gyi rje//che ba can chags
71a1(tsa): @bas ma mo 'bang su 'khol//gsod byed de'i thugs dam dgyes skul ma//ma cig srin mo mgo ma tshangs gnas nas bzhengs//dgra la khrag thun 'bebs shing nga tshur byon//dgra bo khrag thun tshugs tshugs /
71a2(tsa): gsod byed de'i zhabs kyi phyag 'og nas//srid gsum sha khrag mnga' bdag ma mo rnams//dmar lam stan gyi da tshur byon//srid pa gsum gyi khrag gi mnga' bdag mo//mchu 'jim sog la mnga' mdzad ma//g.yas na lcags
71a3(tsa): gu khri dgu stong pa rnams//g.yon na bdud kyi khram shing bsnams//'khor gyi ma mo stong na rtsi rgyad kun//thun zor phang gying ma mo thogs pa myur spyon//dpag tshad brgyad khri khrag gi dri snoms ma//sha khrag gsur la tor te khrag
71a4(tsa): 'thung me//oM mr-i suM sti subs//che ge mo la cho cho//bal hu bi bal yu/hUM bal yu snying gong chod//tsa thun nan/ma thun nan/bal thun nan/khog thun khong khrag che ge mo khong khrag la ya//ces ling ka'i snying brde go/tha ma khyi
71a5(tsa): la gtor ro//gshin rje nag po'i tan tra las//ya ma ra tsa gsang ba thugs kyi thun gyi le'u ste nyer dgu pa'o////de nas mgon po gshin rje gshed kyi//skus 'phros pa rab tu 'jigs pa yis//dam las bzlas
71a6(tsa): pa bskul ba'i phyir/'phrin las dug thun 'di gsungaso//de yang gnas dben par 'phrin las btsugs la khrag dang sol ba bses la gtor ma la spyan drang spyan dug chang gi brlang ba'i nang du dag bo'i ming byang bcug lakapho nyams
71a7(tsa): bkug la nyungs kar tshes brag po//hUM sbugs nag gru gsum 'bar ba'i dkyir nas//'jigs byed ya ma ra tsa gsheg stsol//dbang chen rdegs pa'o 'phrin las sku la rdzogs//smug nag 'khyil bas bdud dpung thal bar klogs
71b1(tsa): 'jigs byed de'i thugs dam dgyes bkul ma//bdun kyi gtso mo gshing rje yum cig sa//thugs dam dus la bab kyi gshegasol//yub gnyis med rol pa'i mkhar dbyings nas//bka' nyan phyag gnyar po ha sgreng ba'i 'khor//na byed/
71b2(tsa): bdud po bcu'i dus la bab//steng gi phyogs nas dgra bo 'gugs mdzad pa/ka/bdud kyi rgyal po khro de brang kar rje//g.yas na bdud kyi sbar spyad bsdams//g.yon na bdud kyi zhag pa bsnams//steng gi phyogs nas dgra bo khrug las shog/shar gyis
71b3(tsa): phyogs nas dgra bo khug las shog/bdud kyi rgyal po ma ha srog bdag rje//phyag na bdud kyi zhag pa bsnams//shar gyi phyogs nas dgra bo khug las shog/shar lho mtshams nas dgra bo khug las sho ga/bdud kyi rgyal po te yo ni tag rje//phyag yas
71b4(tsa): bdud kyi gsod byed spu gri bsnams//g.yon na sho da sde'u dkar nag 'grom sa//shar lho'i mtshams nas dgra bo khug las shog/lho'i phyogs nas dag po 'gug mdzad pa//bdud kyi rgyal po thong bdag thod pa rje ka phyag na bdud kyi khri baM bsnams
71b5(tsa): stag sbrul mang po pho nyer 'gyed//lho'i phyogs nas dgra bo khug la shog/lho nub mtshams nas dgu po 'gugs mdzad pa//bdud kyi rgyal po chu bdud brang dkar rje//phyag na sho rnge'u mi bgo bsdams//lho nub mtshams nas dgra bo khu ba la sho/
71b6(tsa): nub kyi phyogs nas dgra bo 'gugs mdzad pa//bdud kyi rgyal po mtshams bsdud khro bo rje//phyag na lcags kyi zhags pa bsnamaso//nub kyi phyogs nas dgra po khug la shog/nub byang mtshams nas dgra bo 'gugs mdzad pa//bdud kyi rgyal/
71b7(tsa): tsho na gal sa rje//phyag na 'dud kyi zhags pa bsdums//nub bya mams sa ras dgra go khrug la shog/byang gi phyon sa nas 'gra ga zug la shog/bdud kyi rgyal po khror bye /phyag na 'dun gyi rta dag bsdam sa ga/gsor na mi brko brjin pa y-i/
72a1(tsa): @byung gi phyogs nas dgra bo khrag la shog/bya ga ma tshams nas dgra bo 'gugs mdzad pa//bdud kyi rgyal po spyi bdud la ba re//phyag na bdud kyi rdzi shul bsdams//phyag g.yon zas kyi gnas bsdus pa/byal/
72a2(tsa): dgra bo khug la shog/'og gi phyogs nas dgra bo 'gugs mdzad pa//bdud kyi rgyal po sa to ma nag pa rje//phyag na chu srin 'bar ba'i rgyal mtshan bsdams//phyag g.yon bdud kyi zhags pa nag po bsnams//skye 'gro sems can
72a3(tsa): thad sreg par mdzad//'og gi phyogs nas dgra bo khug las shog/che ge mo a 'gu sha dza dza hUM/za byed bdud po khyed rnams kyang //dmigs pa'i dam nyams dgra bo 'di//'jig rten bar du mi bdab//'jig rten phyi mar dus bdab nas//sen
72a4(tsa): /rang bzhin skye shi 'byung //na mkha' stong pa khungs phyung nas//grub pa'i mchis//seM na skal pa chung la snyad mi gdags//se ni bskal pa chung la snyad btags//khyed la thugs rje mi mnga' yaM//ma tshogs bdud dang gshin rje rnams//dam nyams
72a5(tsa): /spying srog drongs la shog/srog chod khrag mthung rus pa 'chol//dbugs mdangs longs la yid phrog shog/sngags la 'khros pa las kyi gshin rje mi bzad kun//nyams pa'i sha la rol cig gshegastsol//sngags pa 'khros pas
72a6(tsa): long bdud po 'dra//'jig rten khams kyi bdud po ma lus kun//nyams pa'i snying la rol cing bdud phab cig/sngags pa 'phros pas srin mo mi bzad 'dra//'jig rten khams kyi srin mo mi zad kun//nyams pa'iri sha khrag srin por
72a7(tsa): zhal du rnguHsa ka/dam tshigs dus la bab bo sa ma ya//oM kyai tang byi ra ta ga ra lan ha sa ran bya pra ban du tsi ta bu ru che ge mo ma ra ya nan//'jam dbul gshin rje nag po'i tan tra las//ya ma ra tsaM thugs dug thun gyi le'u ste sum
72b1(tsa): bcu pa'o///de nas mgon po gshin rje gshed kyis//khro bo gshin rje gshed na ra rgyal ba'i man ngag bsgrub thabs 'di gsung so//dur khrod dben pa'i gan su ni//stegs bu 'dom gang brtsigs nas su//dri chen dag gi zhal du bya/
72b2(tsa): dri chu dag gi chag chag bdab//ma sa yi 'go ma yeng gtang //thig chen dag gis btab na su//dbusu dkyir gru gsum la//mu khyud zlum po 'phreng bas bskor//phyi rol 'khor lo brtsibs brgyad la//gru chad blo 'bur bsgo rnams bcas//gtsu
72b3(tsa): bzhin rdzogs par bris na su/yi dwags kyis ni gos la yang //khro bo'i gzugs sku bris na su//spyan dbye mchod dadu byin rlabs ni//lha phyag lan gsum mgo btugs la//gang 'dod pa'i gsol ba 'debs//sha chen gtor ma cha
72b4(tsa): gsum ba sha ma//tshil chen mar me kha brgyan btang //dam rdzas gzhan yang 'tshogs paro 'gram//ting 'dzin dkyir skyed pa dang //thugs dam bskul ba 'di skad do//ting 'dzin rnam gsum 'phro 'du l+la//grub nas yi ge hUM du gyur//hUM
72b5(tsa): che na mthing nag byin brlabs pas//gas la nas rdo lnga ru gyur//rdo rtse lnga'i 'od zer las//yi ge ya'i sku ru gyur//yi ge ya las sku skyed pa//khro bo chen po rnam par rgyal//ya man ta ka gshin rje gshed//dbu gsum po la phyag
72b6(tsa): drug po//g.yas ni dkar la g.yon ni dmar//dbus kyi sku dang mthun pa'i zhal//sku stong stag gzigs zil 'phro 'dra//zhal ni rdo rje mchi ba btsigs//spyan dmar grogs stong 'gyu ba 'dra//dbu skra kham zhag gyen du 'khyim//
72b7(tsa): phyag drug g.yas kyi dang po na//dung chen dmar gyis bkang ba sngas pa//thugs mi chags pa dbang du bdud//phyag drug gi san gyi bar ma ne//ba su ta 'i zhags po bsdams//ngas las 'dang ba 'ching bar mdzad/phyag drug g.yon gyi mas
73a1(tsa): @zhing gi dkrug pa sngon po bsdams//bdud dang dgra bgegs zil gyis mnon//sku yang lel bas lding can//glang chen ko rlon na bzang gsol//sngags lpags brjid pa'i banas thabs can//zhing ched ga yang bzhin
73a2(tsa): bzar gsol//thod pa skam rlon se ra kha//sbrul chen stong gi lbu tshigs brgya na//zhing chen gril ma'i ba dan skyengs na//brkyang sku ma tshul du brjid pas bzhugs//g.yon nas kho mo nye rwa gnas pa//za byed chen mo pe ta li ka/da mi gsum
73a3(tsa): /gdong ni khro nyer can//lus ni me stag 'bar ba le ka gzi chen 'das kyi rnar brgyan//gtu mo dug sbrul gos gon ma//gyen du sku rjes mthu mo che//de bzhin lag na rtse gsum thogs//gzi chen rab tu bsnyings par mdzad//zhing chung
73a4(tsa): sgri la ma'i ba dan steng su//brkyang bsku ma tshul gyi sbyor byed ma/ga yas sbrul gyi skyes bu ni//sku mdog sngo nag 'jigs pa'i gzugs//rngam pa'i zhal mdangs dbu skra rjes//zhal sgrad spyan yang 'phros pa'i tshul ka ga yas na be
73a5(tsa): tsan mtshon cha thogs /g.yon na sdigs 'dzub che chen 'bar//de ba zhin ma haM'i gdan la bzhugs//gzhan yang dur khrod chen po'i dbus//me dpung 'bar ba'i dkyil 'khor nas//'khor ni thogs med btsan thabs can//
73a6(tsa): 'khor ni ke ri brgyad kyis bskor//de'i phyi rim 'phra men brgyad kyis bskor//thar byed chen po bsgo ba brgyad kyis bskor//yi dgas paphal chen gyi dkyil 'khor 'dir//gshegs shing byon pa'i gnas mang yang //snyen cing bsgrub
73a7(tsa): pa'i lha lags kyis//yal kan can la brtse dgongs bya//rag sha'i 'phreng ba bgrang //phyag mi btsal zhing lam mi zur//sdigs 'dzub bsgreng la dbang po bdeb//seng sdeng 'khar ba rtag tu rtags//khro bo'i stobs kyi 'gro bar bya//
73b1(tsa): de ltar brtul zhugs la gnas nas//mi thun phyogs rnams 'dul bar bya//yi dgas gas ni sor brgya gA/rA Sh+ye bkra gi lcug gis ni//dug dang lan ma sbyi na la/dri dang rjes yang li bya//stegs bu'i lho mu e gag la//
73b2(tsa): drag po'i las rnams tshang bar bya//de nas yub gsang ba'i/yang snying rgyun du drangs pa'i ka bsnyen pa'i sngags ni 'di gsungaso//oM shri man dzu ya man ta ka/oM kro te log kyang bi tsa ya/ya ma ra tsa de byi kri sho ya ga sha dza dza//e ga dza
73b3(tsa): sa ma ya stom//e kA la mo ti hring hring phaT/de nas drag po las kyi thu na sngags 'di ga sung so kaya ma ra tsa mu ga ti yaM ma/ya ma ra tsa kha to ya ma/ya ma ra tsa snying to yau/ya ma ra tsa du na sdus/ya ma ra tsa su ma b+ho//ya ma na
73b4(tsa): ta ka thun b+h+yoka tring tri da ra tsa sa na/snying sha dang ka ni dza/kring kring shang ni dza/snying kha rag snying kha rag/dug khung du shan ta ra ga sha dun ni dza/b+h+yo ru lu ru lu ma ra ya phaT/de nas yang gsad pa las kyi snyen pa'i sngags 'di gsungs//
73b5(tsa): ya ma ro tsa sa du nan/ni ma ra ya/ma ra du ya ma snying /ya ma khrag/tshal pa ba ma ri li li/dam pa dum bu hUM phaT/de nas gshin rje ma mo 'khor tshogs sbro pa'i zor snga Ta 'di gsungaso/oM sa ma ya ma na dz+nyA shri mu tra ya
73b6(tsa): man ta ka/ya ma ra tsa ma ma e ka tsi ta dza dza//tra ga rag sha mu tra snying kha ra ga ma b+h+yo//rag b+h+yoka/rag mo te ke ma dza dza/ba la bi ti b+h+yo/ya ma ra tsa ma ma e ka tsa ti dza dza//trag rag sha mu tra snyi kha rag ma b+h+yo/rag mo te ke ma dza dza khe la ma hi b+h+yo//
73b7(tsa): ya ya lon dan ma la b+h+yo//snying khrag ma srog rgyo//snying khrag ma srog b+h+yoka/de nas 'jam dpal tshe bslab kyi snying po 'di gsungaso//oM a kro te ka/a yu ba la ya ma na ta ka/a u gany+dza ri na ti ba~M a ga kin rje nag po'i tan tra las//khro rnar rgyal/
74a1(tsa): pa'i sgrub pa dang //snying po sngags kyis le'u ste so cig pa'o//ka/de nas mgon po gshin rje gshed kyis//dmigs pa'i rten ri mo'i mtshan nyid 'di gsung so//lha'i dngos 'dod pa la//bsgrub pa'i rten
74a2(tsa): du bye ba ni//rin chen shel las bzhengs gsol dang //dam can bsgrub pa'i bskal bskal pa las ka ral la ri mo bris pa'i ka mtshan nyid nges par gsungs pa'i//gser dang dngul dang mkhar ba bzangs//rA gan in dra ni lad
74a3(tsa): 'ching bu shel dang rin chen dang /rgyal srid 'dod pa gser la bya//longs spyod 'dod pa rdul la bya//nad rnams zhi bas mkhar las bya/zangs kyis thaMd dbang du bsdu//'dod pa dag kyang rab tu ster//dgra rnams gshig phyir rag gan ste//
74a4(tsa): mchog dang 'dod pa gter ba ni//in 'dra ni la dag la bya//nor ltar glid bu dgra yang bsgragaso//dri med shel las byas pa ni//zhi dang rgyas pa'i mchog rnam ster//rin chen sa 'og dngos grub gter ka/de'i mtshan nyid rnams bstan pa ni//gser
74a5(tsa): sor ni brgyad pa ste//de bzhin rdul sor bcu'i tsha dkamakhar ba sor ni bcu dru go/zangs dang ra gan 'di gsungaso//bcu drug in tra ni la'o//thing bu sor ni bcus pa//rin chen sor ni brgyad pa la//mchod gnas 'dod dgu kun gter ste//
74a6(tsa): spyir ni shing la byas pa ste//rin tshon kun gyi mchod 'dod na//tsan dan shel las byas pa te//sor ni nyi shu'i tsha du byan/bde ba phun sum tshogs ldan bsgrub//a kro sha'i snying po la/sor lnga'i tsha du byas nas ni//tha duM kun la dbang med
74a7(tsa): bshad//de bzhin ta la'i shing dag la//sor drug tsha du byas nas ni//a men rlung ya khugs par byedaka/na ga ge sar shing dag la//sor bdun tsha du byas nas ni//nang 'jigs dgra rnams rmongs par byed//mi'i thod pa dag la ni//sor ni
74b1(tsa): kyim chung sum/ring po mi sogs ca 'du gyug/mA khyong drug la m+b+ha sa k+Sh+ma/sna na gsu dus b+h+yas /m+b+ha ga phrul zhig rgyas sa gyu//dgos pa yo nananas rmub pa bye /myos surgya 'dod pa rlab pag+sa
74b2(tsa): ras la ni/mkhas pas khru gang tshang dag lnga//mar gnyis kyi sku dang dkyil//s+thir kyis legs par bri ba bya//tshon gyi kha dog kun mong ba pa/mi ra khrag gi bri yang rung ka dazu sa rgyag ni co brgyad ngas gang drug rgyagan bcu gnyis pa
74b3(tsa): dbu gsum phyag ni drug pa ste//de la zhal cig phywa gnyis rnams//rang nyid gang 'dod gzhung bzhin bri/ka/byis pa la me tog dmar po dang //mchod pa dang ni cher mchod laka/sgrub pa'i rten du bzhag par bya//gzhan gyis mthong na dngos grub yal/
74b4(tsa): 'jam dpal gshin rje nag po'i nyan dza las/ri mo mtshan nyid las nges par gtan pa'i le'u skye so gnyis pa'o///de nas mgon po gshin rje gshed la//phyogs bcu dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas thad kyis//thugs kyis kyi
74b5(tsa): 'am sku bdag nyid yin te//dus gsum gyi bstan pa la gnod pa'i dus dang //mu stegs dang srin po dang //gshin rje dang yi dwags dang //sha za dang ni ma mo dang //dgra bgegs la sogs pa tha~Md 'dul zhing tshar gcad pa'i gnyen por ming
74b6(tsa): dbang bskur te//gnang ba mdzad nas skrags pa mdzad de//lnga brgya dus kyi snyigs ma la//bstan pa gnyen po srung bar bskoso//de nas gshin rje dang ma mo 'khor tshogs thaMd kyis cig tu stod de phyag 'tshalo//'di skad ces zer ro//hUM mi gnas
74b7(tsa): mi dmigs chos sku dag pa la//bdud 'dul khro mcho gis sku sprul pa//thugs rje gzi ldan gdug pa thabs kyis 'dul//khro rgyal gshin rje bshed la phyag 'tshalo//mnyam med thugs mnga' gtum chen 'bar ba'i sku//dus gsum za byed bskal
75a1(tsa): @pa me ltar 'bar//ma rungs 'joms mdzad khro bo tshogs kyi gtso//thar byed gzhonu khyod la phyag 'tshalo//lhag pa'i yais spyan gsum rna raM dag/thugs rje spyan ldan khro bo rab 'jigs mdzad//ma rungs 'joms
75a2(tsa): mdzad kho bo'i dgra bo'i bshed//mnyam nyid spyan la phyag 'tshal bstod//khro mchog yid bzhin thabs kyi sku mngar ba//dpag bsam shing ltar re ba skong mdzad pa//mchod pa sdig sbyangs byas nas bder thob pa/gtum chen mi bzang
75a3(tsa): rtsub so'i dgrar ldan pa//khyod kyi mtshan nyid thogs pa tsam gyis bgegs chen phung //'bar ba'i sku mthong gsung sgra thos pa yis//dgra bgegs rlag par byed la phyag 'tshalo//khro bo'i rgyal po khro chen gshin rje'i//'jigs
75a4(tsa): pa'i sku la sngags lpags na bza gsol//mthu mchog bstan phyir sbrul nag sku la bcings//'dzegs byed mtha' yas sgrol la phyag 'tshalo//'od phung chug mo skal pa'i me ltar 'bar//yai sa 'od 'phro gti mug 'joms
75a5(tsa): ba'i sku//khro bo thod 'phreng dbu brgyan can//'dod khams dbang du bsdud la phyag 'tshalo//yais mkha' gro sngon mo'i mdog du 'gyur//sku mdog sngo nag dbyer gyi sprin lta bu//dus gsum mun pa 'joms par dam bcas pa//dri med
75a6(tsa): mche bo dkar po gtsigs//ma rungs sdings pa gtum po skye du byas shing //thabs kyi zhags pas 'gro ba ma lus 'dren//mtshungs med thugs rje can la phyag 'tshalo//'di dang pha rol gnyis ka bder mtshal nas//lha mchog khyod la
75a7(tsa): rten nas bzhan mi bzhugs//yun rings ltar yang khyod la gsol 'debs na//ri rab gser gyi ngos la bya gnas pa//bya'i rang bzhin bor nas gser du gnang //thaMd khyod kyi sku ni mos skyed pas//sau'i rang bzhin bor nas khror gsal//
75b1(tsa): maM phyed dang pas khyod la phyag 'tshalo//yid bzhin gter chen mchod sdong 'bar 'dra yang //khyod kyi sku mthong rim gro bgyis 'tshal naka bdag cag 'khor dang 'bangasu gtogs pa las ka/sman pa'i phyogs rnams phun gsum tshogs
75b2(tsa): par gsol ka khro bo'i rgyal po dri med yis sku//rab tu mi zad khro'i gzir gnang bas//chos nyid mnyam pa'i ngang las mi g.yo ba'i//thugs rjes tshul gyis 'gro la phyag 'tshalo//nang gi chos nyid dam par tshur sbyong zhing //pha rol
75b3(tsa): dam rdzas bsres par bgyi ste//mi gtsang ma ngos 'di 'phags pa mi bgyis kyang //bdag gi lus 'di 'phags pa yongs 'bul na//gnyis med don gyis mchod pa zhe stsol//bdag cag rigs drug 'khor du rtogs pa rnams ka lus
75b4(tsa): ngag yid gsum dang bas phyag 'tshal na//dad pa'i sems kyis 'phags mchog mi gtong ngo //'gro la phan phyir khro rgyal khyod mos na//mi mthun phyogs rnams brdul du rlag du gsol ka zhes kun gyis stong la phyag 'tshalo//gshin rje
75b5(tsa): nag po'i tan tra las//'khor gyis stong pa'i le'u ste so gsum pa'o////de nas gshin rje dang ma mo dang //mkha' gro ma thaMd 'gro ba cig smras pa//bdag cag dus gsum tha~Md du//mgon po gshin rje gshed dka' dang dam las mi 'da' zhing //bka'
75b6(tsa): ci gsol ba bzhin du bgyi 'o//gal te dam bcas pa bzhin ma bgyis na//mgon po nyid kyis bdag cag rnams mngon gsum du bsgral te//snying 'gas par gyur ces de bca so//de nas 'khor zad kyis kyang //de bzhin du dam bcas nas//sau rang gi gnas
75b7(tsa): 'dongo //'jam dpal gshin rje'i rgyud las dbu zhabs don sdus pa dang //dam bcas pa'i le'u ste so bzhi pa'o//ka'ajam dpal gshin rje gshed nag gi rgyud/rdzog s+ho///sarba mang+ga laM//
76a1(tsa): rgya gang ska du/ya ma kr-ita gu h+ya sha ra ta kha thun nA ma tan tra/bod skad du/gshin rje nag po gsang de kho na nyid kyi rgyud kha thun+da zhes bya ba/bac+doMs 'jas dpal gshed po grub la phyag 'tshalo//'di skad
76a2(tsa): bshad pa'i dus gcig na//bac+doMs 'jam dpal ya mAn ta ka nyid chos sku'i ngang las gzugs sku zhal gsum phyag drug par bzhengs te/'phrin las rnam bzhi lhun gyis grub pa'i gzhal ya sa khang phyi nang yongasu gsal
76a3(tsa): ba'i pho brang yas las grub pa'i 'og min gyi gnas na/yum po ta li deng gnyi su med par bzhugaso//de'i dus de'i tshe na 'dus pa'i 'khor yang yis dang las las grub pa dang thabs cig pa las/dregs pa chen po'i
76a4(tsa): ti nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugaso//de'i ngang las bac+doMs kyis sku gsung thugs las rdo'i tshig 'di gsungaso//kye ma ho//'khor 'das tha 'khor lo'i dang //'khor lo thad 'jam dpal don//de nyid rig pa byang chub
76a5(tsa): sems/bde ba chen por sangs rgyaso//oM a ho//zhes bka' stsal to//'jam dpal kha thun gyi rgyud gleng bzhi'i lo'u ste dang pa'o////de nas bac+doMs 'jam dpal chen po des//srid pa'i gdug pa 'dul ba zhes
76a6(tsa): bya b.ri ti nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugaso//de'i dus de'i tshe na gsod byed ya ma rA dza zhes bya ba//ri rab kyi 'og chu'i dkyir gyi steng du na gnas pa la sogs pa'i bdud dang //gshin rje dang srin po dang /ma mo khrag
76a7(tsa): 'thung dang /lha ma srin rnams shin tu gdug pa che bar gyur to//de rnams kyis srid pa gsum gyis sen zad za zhing /gsod pa dang rngam par gyur pa las//bcom ldan 'das ya mAn ta kas/yum be ta li dang gnyi
76b1(tsa): su med pa'i sbyor ba mdza deka/sras ya mA rA tsu sprul nas gsod byed ya ma ra dza'i lcags mkhar bcu bdun gzir ba las/de rnams kyi shin tu dngangs te sogs dang bral ba snyam du gyur nas//'jigs pa'i sems kyis
76b2(tsa): babastod pa ni/gtum chen dreg pa chen po tha 'joms pa'i lha//dbang byed bdud 'dul dur khrod chas kyis brgyan//khro rngam gdugs pas srid gsum thal bar rlog/sgrol byed sku mchog ma mo 'bangasu 'khol//gtum chen
76b3(tsa): rab 'bar gang zhin rje 'joms mdzad lha//'jigs byed yaM ma rA dza'i sku la phyag 'tshal bstod//ces smras te/ya ma ra dza du du man ta ka ra nu pre tA dza ya e mu kaM snying la guM rbad dza/ni dza shag pa baM ril snying gong
76b4(tsa): ma ra na nag po yA mu srog la phra su ga bi ril rbang/ma ra ni rbad rbad/bdag gi smod pa rig par bcas//dpa' bos bzhesu 'bangasu mchi//zhes dbus kyi gsod byed chen pos smraso//de nas slas mchog ya
76b5(tsa): ma rA dz+nyA sa bka' stsal pa ka nga la bstor pa phul ba yis//phan par gyur pa ma yin gyis//dA yi lha la bstod pa dang //'khor bcas khyod kyis srog snying phul//zhes gsung so//de'i dusu lha gang yinA snying ste//nam/
76b6(tsa): mkha'i dbus na ya ra bltas pa las//srid pa gsum gyi rtse mo na sngar bas gzi ma dngas dang ldan pa'i lha ya mAn ta ka mthong ste ka shin tu bred pali dbang gis bstod pa ni/nyi ma 'bum pas lhag pa'i 'od stong phyoT
76b7(tsa): bcur 'gyed//gdug byed mtshon cha mang pos skal pa'i me ltar 'bar ke/rab brjid gshin rje 'joms la phyag 'tshalo//zhes smras te/dbus kyi yi rags dang gshin rje rnams kyis rang gi snying po phul ba nika ya ma~M
77a1(tsa): dza sa to me ya/ya me to ru na yo ta ya/ya ta yo nai ra gak+She ya/yAk+Sha ya tsu ni ra mA ya hUM bkra zhes bu'o//de nas sh+yA za dmar po yub gyis sgras ga oM nal ma rnal te gsod la rnga ma g.yogs la me
77a2(tsa): dbris la chus saM bru~M d+his thum b+his thum b+hi sa/lcags kyi sku cig mas dgra bo'i snying byung cig/de'i tshe ma mo dang khrag 'thung rnams kyis rang gi smod pa phul te smras pa//ma ma e ka dza dza ti dza dza/trag rag sha mu tra snying ma b+h+yo b+h+yo//e
77a3(tsa): ka dza ti mi tsa ro la baM ri la rbad tra ga ra ga sha thum ri la rba da/rak+Shau te kum dza dza kha la ma hi b+h+yo b+h+yo//re ma ti snying la baM ri la rbad/thun muT snying la rngub sa rbad/ya ya lon cin d+ha na ma la b+h+yo dza dza/snying kha rag srog la b+h+yo/ma mAM tsit+ta
77a4(tsa): nurgud thum//snying la tshal pa me da baM ri la rbad/ces smraso//de'i dus su gza' ra hu las smras pa/ra hu la tshal ba me pa sha mi ki kha ta hUM nan laM sti pa ya nan/zhes smraso//de nas yang khri bdag bcus dang lha ma srin sde brgyad kyis smras
77a5(tsa): oM ya ma rA dza satwa me ya b+h+yo//ru lu ru lu baM ril rbad nan/oM dub nur du ba rlur che ge mo tshal pa nan tshal pa nan/oM snying gshogs me kha lo mu lo tsa thun nan/oM sa ti ru ru grong krar tsa nan/a mu kaM mA ra ya nan/oM akro d+hi/
77a6(tsa): many+dzu shrI ya mAn ta ka kA la ru pa ga shaSha ya nan sa du baM rbad ru lu hUM dza/ru la tsa ba ya/ni ti sha ru nI nan/a mu kaM sA ra ya nan/ni mA ra ya/ni ma ra du/ya ma snying ya ma khrag tshal ba baM ri la/dam+b+ha duM bu hUM phaT/badz+ra tsI dum ling tsa
77a7(tsa): mu yau ma mu ya ba snying khrag nyug tshil zad/ya ma rA dza sha traM mu tshil hi gug/ling dul ling rbad ling /a mu kaM tshom ling swA hA/bdag cag bstan pa bsrung bar gyis/zhes smraso//de'i tshe bac+doMs kyis dam tshig gi snying po bsla
77b1(tsa): pa ni/oM hrIHkhI bi kri tA na na sa ma ya hUM hUM phaT swA ha/zhes bka' stsal te ram chu blud nas mnar bsgrags te/bstan pa bsrung bar dam bcas te zas bskal du bstan pa bshig pa'i dgra bo'i srog dbugs byin te/gtor ma dang me rdzas so/
77b2(tsa): nas bka' las mi 'da' bar bka' bsgo ba ni//oM ya mAn ta ka hri ste mauny+dza shrI sa ma ya u ni u ye dzA//zhes bka' stsal to//ma mo khrag 'thung zad kyis snying po'i mgo yon gyi sngags kyis gnan pa dang //gshin rje'i mgo mnan
77b3(tsa): pa 'bru bzhi pa bka' stsal pa/oM saHya many+dzuHthI ya mAn ta kA ya ma rA dza/zhes bka' stsal to//ya mAn+ta ka zhes pa ni/gshin rje'i mgo ma nan par bka' stsal to//de ltar thaMd btu la te/bstan pa bsrung bar dam btsas nas 'jig
77b4(tsa): rten pa bde ba la bkod nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing du gyur to//'jam dpal kha thun gyi rgyud las//dregs pa btul ba'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o///de nas bac+doMs kyis dges pa'i ti nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhug so//de nas
77b5(tsa): thugs las sprul pa'i sras ya ma rA dzas gsol pa//e ma ho//se naM kun la gnod byed pa//btul nas dam tshig bkod pa ni//kye ma shin tu ngo mtshar to//de nas rje su bsgrub pa ni//gsang sngags ji lta'i rim pa dang //de dag
77b6(tsa): btul ba'i rim pa rnams//las la bkol na ji ltar bkol//las kyi kha tshar ji ltar lags//bac+doMs kyis bsha du gsol//de nas bac+doMs kyis bka' stsal pa//kye ma nyon cig 'dus pa'i 'khor/btsun 'jam dpal bsgrub 'o/
77b7(tsa): pasas rna khyor dam tshogs ldan pa yis ka dben pa chen po'i gnas nyi duka phrin las bzhi yi dkyil 'khor la//lha yi phyag mtshan be tshon dgod//bu don du gnyer nas 'grub//dang po bla ma mnyes par bya//de nas phyi nang gis
78a1(tsa): pa'i dbang thob nas dam tshig ngag dang ldan/bdag nyid yub bskyed byas la//spyan drangs mchod nas dngos grung byung //yid bzhin nor bu'i bsnyen pa rdzas//oM a kro te ka ya mAn taka ta nA ma ra r+d+ha lA hU~M pas/
78a2(tsa): 'bum phrag bcu ru bzlas byas na//dpa' bo 'jig rten gsum las rgyal//zhes gsung so//'jam dpal kha thun gyi rgyud las bsnyen pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas gzhan yang bshad par bya//dur khrod dag gis gnas
78a3(tsa): 'dug la//bdag nyid yub 'phro 'du lpa//mdun du sras mchog bskyed par bya//kha ni lho bltas 'brub khung du//ling kha bcug la thun gyis brab//rgyun du ston snying po drang //oM sa ma ya many+dzu shrI mu tra ya mAn ta ka pany+tsa d+he ro ni hUM//
78a4(tsa): de nas sngar gyis yon la//ma mo khrag thun bong rbad gsang//dril ba'i sngags kyis bsgrub par bya//gsod byed gtor ma rgyun du gtang //zhes gsungaso//'jam dpal kha thun gyi rgyud las sgrub pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o//
78a5(tsa): sa gzhan yang bshad bya bkalas la sbyar bar 'dod pa ni//thun dang 'khor lo gang rung bya//gza' skar ngan pa dag la gtad//zla ba mar ngo dag la bya//ro tshas ras la mi khrag dang //sbyar rtsi ldum bus snag tsha bya//skad sna bzhi yis
78a6(tsa): gsod byed sti ma//bya rog 'ug pa'i sgro rtsa yos//bcus rtsibs chen bri bar bya//'khor lo'i las sngags 'di btags bskor//e mu kaM pra ma ya hUM phaT/a mu kam rak+ta tsa ra ya hUM phaT/a mu gaM mu ra tsa ya hUM phaT/a mu kaM mA ra
78a7(tsa): ya hUM phaT/phyi nang gsang ba'i rim pas bzlas//de mar thag chad nas rgyun gtor gtang //khri las mi mang mi nyung bya'o//yang na de bas thun la bya'o//thod pa nag po yin nges la//'gram pa g.yas g.yon lhag pa dang //mchogs
78b1(tsa): sa gzhi ru sngags bris la//dmar nag dril pa'i thun gyis brag/mtshan mo gsod byed drug sngags bsdus//bdag nyid sku ni brgyan cig bskyed//ya sa rA dza rgya chab gdon//khri bdag bskul zheng gshed bdun zad ku ya sa rA dza bskul
78b2(tsa): byas la//seng ldeng phur pa snying la gdab//oM ya man ta ka k+ShaM Ar+ya a tsa la k+ShaM/oM ma dug rtsa rtsa tshal ba tshal ba rbad rbad/ma dug kaM ya mak+ShaM/a mu kaM srog rtsa la mugs ting rbang /rtsa rtsa ra rtsa rbang /ma mo khrag la don ting muH
78b3(tsa): srin po'i rus la chom chom tsha la//gshin rje'i khr-i la lhan lhan tho ba//dgra po khr-i la khr-i ri li li NaM dza rba dka rnye kre d+ha de na p+la de b+ha ba ta che ge mo mA ra ya rbad/ces pas phu ra pa gdab par byaka de nas de nyid yum bskul la//ma mo khrag/
78b4(tsa): 'thung snying po dang //khrag 'thun 'di yis dbab par bya//mgo 'phyangs ma dza dze/dun mugs zla tsa la dza dza/rtsa bo che la dun du na mugs//nang khrol smad lnga la rtsa la bam//dun bam dun ri li li rbad/khrag dang du gnyis bsres la brde/
78b5(tsa): de nas gsod byed bsku la byas la//drag sngags bdag gis sngar ltar brdeg/de yi yum dang ma tshogs bskulaka khrag thun dag ni brdeg par bya//dgra bo khrag thun mugs//oM mu ya mugs ti su bas//che ge mo la mo cho/kha la hu/
78b6(tsa): bal hu spu snying gong rtsa thun nan//bal thun nan/che ge mo nan/khrag thun khong khrag/a mu kaM khong khrag la ye/nad rnams sor btang ba dang //ban de la sogs gsad pa ni/ka/man ngag dam pas bya ba yin//thun drug dril ba'i man
78b7(tsa): ngag la/mda' yis gzar bar bya ba ste//'ug pa ma yi mda' zer ni/zla ba zad khar 'phang zhing bskul /dug gis myos byed rak+tas 'jig shungs kar mtshon la khram shing brab ka dang la phur pa gdab par bya//sngags pa nang 'gras
79a1(tsa): @rtags 'gor naka ro langs rak+sha nag po bya//yang na thugs kyi bsrung bzlog bya//las rnams grub na dur khrod du//drang srong sngags dang smod pas mnan//gtor ma gtang la 'phrin las gzhol//zhes gsung so//
79a2(tsa): 'jam dpal kha thun gyi rgyud las//khyad par las la 'akol te bsad pa'i le'u ste lnga pa'o///de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//las kyi kha tshar rim pa ni ka rma lhor bkra shis 'dod pa ni//tshe yi man tra 'di ltar bya//oM
79a3(tsa): a kro te ka ya mAn ta ka kA la rU pa A yu ba la sid+d+hi b+huM dza/zhes pas tshe yi bsgrub pa bya//tshe yi dngos grub chen po 'thob//gzhan yang ser ba dbab 'dod nA/bkra shis 'dong ba ni/dud kar phro ma shu ti phrom//yug ti smin trag du tsar sha
79a4(tsa): lta hri la phob//nyungs dkar dag la sngags te 'phang /gzhan yang ser ba bcad pa ni//oM kI la rU pa tsa ya bi dang sang sho sha ya mo ha ya snyam pa ya hUM phaT/ti shum shu sha ma li ti di bi rau hi ha ya ru ru ti swA hA//phe kaM na ya phaT/skye 'gram nyus
79a5(tsa): kar dag la sngags//gang 'od phyogasu btang byas nas/'chud par 'gyur te the tshom med//snying dang 'dra te shin tu zab//gzhan yang bltas ngan 'bebs 'dod na//ya ma ni ra kya kyeng trab 'ur phog ces gsungaso//'jam dpal kha thun gyi rgyud las//las
79a6(tsa): kha tshar gyi le'u ste drug pa'o///de nas bac+doMldas la 'khor gyis bstod pa ni//e ma ho//yab cig 'jam dpal gshin rje gshed//dbu drug phyag kyang bcu gnyis te//zhams kyang rnam pa drug tu bgrang //gdug pa'i mtshon ma sna tshogs
79a7(tsa): bsnams//dung rjod 'bang ba'i can dang ldan/ma mo gshin rje kun gyi bdag lha mchog nyid la dad pas bstod//ces bstod par gyur to//'das dpal khathun gyi rgyung las//'khor gyis bstod pa'i lo'u ste/bdun pa'i///de nas parjos kyi/
79b1(tsa): bka' stsal pa//e ma ho nga'i rgyud 'di ni//rgyud kyi snying po de kho na nyid ces bya ba ste//'on tang na sangs rgyas kyis bstan pa la dgra dar ba'i dus su//sangs rgyas che ba'i yon du bstan pa yin pas shin tu brnag cing gzung bar bya'o//zhes gsungaso//
79b2(tsa): 'jam dpal kha thun gyi rgyud las/rgyud yongasu gzung ba'i le'u ste/brgyad pa'o////gshen rje nag po gsang ba de kho na nyid kyis rgyud ces bya ba rdzogs s+ho///rgyud 'di 'jam dpal gyis 'og min du gsungs nas//rgyal po
79b3(tsa): dza zhos bya ba bsod nams bsags pa de la babs//des bkra shis bde ldan gyi dga' rab rdo rje la//des 'jam dpal shes gnyen la//des pa su d+hA ra la//des yang dbang gter la//bka' re re las spel du mi rung ngo //rgya rgya rgya//
80a1(tsa): rgya gar skad du/ya ma na kriSh+Na ku he sha ta ta kha thun nA ma gu t+ya tan tra/bod skad du/gshin rje nag po kha thun gsang ba de kho na nyid phyi ma'i rgyud ces bya ba//bac+doMs 'jam dpal gshin rje gshed po khros pa
80a2(tsa): nyid la phyag 'tshalo//'di skad bstan pa'i dus gcig na//bac+doMs gshin rje gshed po nyid//rang bzhin gyis gnas na bzhugs pa las//thugs mchog ya ma rA dzas gsol pa//kye kye btsun 'jam dpal khros pa nyid//
80a3(tsa): khyad par thun gyi 'phrin las ma yin pa'i//drag po'i 'khor lo bskor ba gang //bdag la rim par bshad du gsol//zhes gsol pas//'jam dpal khro bos bka' stsal pa//spyir na 'jam dpal nag po yang //'khor
80a4(tsa): lo bskor thabs bsam mi khyab//phyi nang nang dang gsang ba dang //de la phye bas dgu ru 'gyur//dbye ba dgug pa bskrad kha dang //ru bzhi rdo rgya gram dang ka dug gsum a ra la gtad pa dang /khasam gyis mi khyab gsungs kyu/
80a5(tsa): rgyud chen zla gas la sogs pa'i ka 'dod pa yin te 'dir ma gsungs//de ltar mang du bskor gyur na/sngags pa dag la rkyen yang 'byung //ro langs bskor ba la shad gyur kyang //bskor na rang nyid rlag pa yin//de yang 'dir ma smos
80a6(tsa): ba'i ngagal te 'khor lo bskor gyur na//'dir ni bskor thabs rnam pa drug phyi dang nang dang gsang ba las//phyi yi phyi ltar bskor ba dang //byi yoM gas ltar bskor ba dang //nang gi byi dang nang gi no//gsang ba'i phyi rang gsang ba'i ya~M/gsang
80a7(tsa): par bskor ba dag dang drug/de dag phyi ni dgug pa la//de dag gsang ba gsad pa'o//de dag phyi ni g.yon skor yin//nang gi ga ya suM bskor ba'o//de ltar 'khor lo bskor thabs drug/bskor bar shis pa phyi yi phyi//mi bskor ba ni rnam pa spa
80b1(tsa): bsnyen pa rdzogs pa'i rna myor ba 'am//bsod nams ldan pa'i rgyal po 'am//de ltar bu yis bskor ba la sa ka bskor na rang la gnod par 'gyur//zhes gsung so//'jam dpal kha thun phyi ma'i rgyud lasaka/'khor lo dbye ba skyon yon
80b2(tsa): bsdu zabs bstan pa'i le'u ste dang pa'o///de nas gzhan yang bka' stsal pa/phyi yi phyi ltar bskor ba ni//ya ma ra dza sa do me ya//che ge mo zhig pra ma ya hUM phaT/nyi ma dang po'o//ya me do run yo da ya che ge mo cig mur
80b3(tsa): tsa ya hUM phaT/nyi ma gnyis pa'o//ya da yo ni ra ya k+She ya che ge mo zhig rak+ta tsa ra ya hUM phaT/nyi ma gsum pa'o//ya k+She ya tsa ni ra ma ya che ge mo zhig mA ra ya hUM phaT/nyi ma bzhi ba'o//de nas phyi yi gsang ba ltar bskor ba ni//ya
80b4(tsa): ma ra ni tsa ya k+She ya che ge mo pra ma ya hUM phaT/nyi ma dang po'o//yak+She ya ra nai yo ta ya che ge mo mu ra tsa ya hUM phaT//nyi me gnyis pa'o//ya ta yo na ru tro me ya/che ge mo rak+ta tsa ra ya hUM phaT/nyi ma gsum pa'o//ya ma do dza mA ra ya
80b5(tsa): che ge mo mA ra ye hUM phaT/nyi ma bzhi pa'o//zhes gsungaso//'jam dpal kha thun phyi ma'i rgyud las/phyi yi phyi dang phyi'i gsang ba ltar bskor ba'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o///de nas yang nang gi phyi ltar bskor ba ni//ya
80b6(tsa): ma me ta k+She/che ge mo pra ma ya hUM phaT/nyi ma dang po'o//ra tsa sa to che ge mo mu ra tsa ya hUM phaTo/ny+dzi ma gnyis pa'o//ru nau yo na/che ge mo rak+ta tsa ra ya hUM phaT/nyi ma gsum pa'o//ra ya tsa ni che ge mo mA ra ya hUM phaT/nyi ma bzhi pa'o//
80b7(tsa): nang gi gsang ltar bskor ba ni//ya ma k+She ta me/che ge mo pra ma ya hUM phaT/nyi ma dang po'o//ra ni tsa ya/che ge mo mur tsa ya hUM phaT/nyi sra gnyis pa'o//ra ni yo na/che ge mo rak+Sha tsa ra ya hUM phaT nyi ma gsum pa'o//ru to satsa/
81a1(tsa): che ge mo ma ra ya hUM phaT nyi ma bzhi pa'o//zhes gsungaso//'jam dpal kha thun phyi ma'i rgyud las//nang gi phyi dang nang gi gsang ba ltar bskor ba'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas
81a2(tsa): gzhan yang bshad bya ba//gsang pa'i phyi ltar bskor ba ni//ya ma ra dza sa to me ya//ma ra dza sa to me ya ma//ra dza sa to me ya ma/sa to me ya ma ra ya/me yaM ma ra tsa sa to//che ge mo pra ma ya hUM phaT/nyi ma dang po'o//
81a3(tsa): ya me do ru na yo da/me to ru na yo ta ya/to ru na yo ta ya me/ru na yo ta ya me tokan yo ta ya ma to ru/yo ta ya ma to ru na/ta ya ma to ru/yo ta ya ma to ru na yo//che ge mo mu ra tsa ya hUM phaT/nyi ma gnyis pa'o//ya da yo/
81a4(tsa): ni ra ya k+She ya/da yo ni ra ya k+She ya/yo ni ra ya k+She ya da/ni ra yaM k+She ya da yo//ra ya k+She yang yo ni/ya k+She ya ta yo na ri/k+Shi ya da yo ni ra ya/che ge mo rak+ta/tsa ra ya hUM phaT/nyi ma gsum pa'o//ya k+She ya tsa ni ra ma/k+She
81a5(tsa): ya tsa ni re ma ya/ya tsa ni ra ma ya k+She/tsa ni ra ma ya k+She ya/ni ra ma ya k+She ya tsa/re ma ya k+She ya tsa ni/ma ya k+She ya tsa ni ra/che ge mo rak+ta/mA ra ya hUM phaT/nyi ma bzhi pa'o//gsang ba'i gsang ba ltar bskor ba nI//k+She
81a6(tsa): ya tsa ni ra ma ya zhes pasaM la sogs snga ma de//ga ya su bskor ba gsang ba gas pa//gong mas sbyar la shes par bya//zhes gsungaso//'jam dpal kha thun phyi ma'i rgyud las//'khor lo gsang ba'i phyir dang //gsang ba'i
81a7(tsa): gsang ltar bskor ba'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////de nas gzhan yang bka' stsal pa//de ltar 'khor lo bskor ba yang //dang po bsnyen pa rdzogs par bya//de nas mdun rogs bud brten//de na sum bcu rtsa gnyis pa//
81b1(tsa): rmis kham yang na brgya bdun theg/de bzlas kyi drug ngas la/'kha la rtsibs ni brgyas pa//myur gyi rdzum pa dus khrugs//pha rags sgon+d+has dpe nga gA'i sa/bris la bden pa zung bya 'o//smyod las g+ha sh+ma b+he ts+yai dun/2
81b2(tsa): por bskor//mtshan mo dril l+ya phun gyis brag//lci brgyad la gnyis pa bskor//nye dgu la gsum pa bskur//gnam spyod nyi ma bzhi la bskor//tshes gcig thun gyi dgongs ka ni//dur khrod dag tu gnan pa bya//lan gsum bar du spyon
81b3(tsa): shing bya//de la d+ma snod yang ngo la/sa ma yaM dang sum cu rtsa gnyis brig/zla smad 'ikhor lo skor la gnan//phyi yi phyi nang gsang ba zab//nang dang gsang ba bskor byar dag pa/bsnyen rdzogs grub pa thob nas bsko ba/des ni gang yang
81b4(tsa): rlag par 'gyur//zhes bka' stsal pa dang //sras mchog ya ma ra dzas gsol pa//bac+doMs rgyud 'di ji ltar bdag gis bzung bar gyi ces gsol pA//bac+doMldas kyis bka' stsal pa//rgyud 'di ni gshin rje nag po kha thun de kho na
81b5(tsa): nyid rgyud phyi ma zhes bya bar gzung zhing //sangs rgyas kyi bstan pa bsrung ba'i phyir cang zhing sba bar bya//bsgrub par bya'o zhes gsung so//gshen rje nag po kha thun de kho na nyid kyi rgyud phyi ma las bsgrub pa'i man ngag dang //rgyud gzung zhing bcang
81b6(tsa): ba'i le'u ste lnga pa'o///gshin rje nag po kha thun gyi rgyud de kho na nyid phyi ma zhes bya ba rdzogas+ho////gu ru ba su d+ha ras gnubs yis rin po me la//b+hA sa yi'i dgon par gnang ba'o//rgya rgya gye//IM//bdag 'dra sangs rgyas
81b7(tsa): ye shes kyis ka gu ru ba su d+hA ra la/zhus te dar la legs par pha'o//su la ma byin bla ma'i bka'//yang dben logs la sbas pa yin//las 'phro can dang phrad nas kyang //dam la gnas pas spyod par shog//sarba mang+ga laM//
82a1(tsa): rgya gar skad du/shrI a yu da ra kA li ka mi sa sa ta sarba a d+hi tan+t+ra ma hA/khod skad du/dpa' tshe bdag dag go'i 'phrin las kyi dgongs pa zang rdzogs pa'i rgyud chen po//rjes dpal cheme bdag rag ze'i phrin las thang rdzogs pa
82a2(tsa): phyag 'tshalo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/bac+doMs chos thaMd lhun gyis rdzogs pa'i rang bzhin rdo yongasu bkod pa'i snying po 'og min gyi gnas mkha' dbyings rnar dag pa'i klong rang bzhin lhun gyis grub pa'i gzhal yas khang na//
82a3(tsa): 'khor rdo snying po nyid kyi rang bzhin dang dus gsum cig pa'i sangs rgyas rnams dang /ma skyes pa'i lam la 'dun pa'i rigs pa can rnams dang /sa la gnas pa'i byang chub se'i rnams dang //khro bo'i tshogs dregs pa can rnams dang /
82a4(tsa): 'thoms pa bdud kyi tshogs dang //khrag skyugs ma mo'i tshogs tshogs dang //phung byed/mngag pa'i tshogs dang //gdug pa gsum po'i tshogs dang thabs cig tu bzhugaso//de nas bac+doMs la 'khor rnams kyis yongasu 'dun par gyur to//bac+doMs
82a5(tsa): dpal rdo 'chang chen po des 'khor rnams yongasu 'dus par gzigs nas//chos thaMd kun tu mi g.yo ba zhes bya ba'i snang bas khyab par mdzad//de nas 'khor thad sems 'drog par gyur to//de nas bac+doMldas kyis sems 'drog par gzis
82a6(tsa): nas 'khor rnams la nyid kyi rang bzhin 'di bstan to//chos rnams ma skyes ye nas gnyisu med//rgyu dang rkyen med rang byung gnas//de las kun 'byung rang 'byung thabs bsam yas//'gro ba ma lus pa'i rang bzhin las ka ma g.yo
82a7(tsa): g.yo med cir yar gsal//snying po nyid la g.yo ba ci yang med//ces gsung so//de nas 'khor rnams kyis rang gi de nyid so ma yin par gyur to//de nas rdo snying po des//bac+doMs rdo 'chang chen po la 'di skad ces gsol to//kun
82b1(tsa): gyi rang bzhin nyid lags par//gsang sngags ngo bo de kho na//dus gsum sangs rgyas nyid las seng/nyid pas che ba rgyas par mdzad//ces gsol to//de nas bac+doMs rje 'amang chen pos des//mi rtog pa rang byung gis dgongs pa chen po la 'od
82b2(tsa): pa phrag sa ma ya phyogs bcur bkye'o//'od zer des zhing khams thad du snang bas khyab par mdzad nas//sangs rgyas thaMd kyis dgongs pa rgyud de slar bsdu so//sangs rgyas thaMd kyi rang bzhin//chos thad kyi rtsa ba//rdo 'chang chen po nyi du mdzad nas
82b3(tsa): sbyor ba'i don gnyis gsungs par gyur to//de nas bac+doMs kyis lhun gyis grub pa'i gnas la gnas pa deg/lus dbang sems yul 'dzin chags grol//kun bzhi nyid las bye brag gsumaka rtsa ba gcig las lnga gnyis brgyad//bco brgyad bzhi dang ldan
82b4(tsa): ba'i lha//tshe sogs kun dbang gtso dang 'khor//gnyi su med pa'i rang bzhin du//bston pas 'bras bu lhun grub bshad//mdab brgyad dngas ma bzhi dbus na//ston pa rang bzhin de 'khor rdzogs//lhag pa'i sku la yis lnga//rtsa lam 'khor lo rim
82b5(tsa): rgyud gnas//lhun grub mchod pa rang gnas thim//rig pa'i yis rang las 'char//ston pas 'bras bu gnyis grub bshad//yu~M gyi b+ha ga gsang ba'i gnas//rnam bzhi pho brang lhun gyis grub//sgo 'khyams b+ha ga sna rtse la//ston lhun grub rin po/
82b6(tsa): rdo gsang ba'i las sbyangs nas//ston pa'i gtso 'khor bstan pa'i dus//mnyam rdzogs gcig tu lhan cig skyes//bde ba'i lces rgyun lam bcad//smyon pas gsang ba'i sgo nas gcad //de nas bac+doMs la ji snying pos gsol pa//kye tshe rdo 'chang
82b7(tsa): ba khro bo'i rgyal//rnam dag chos sku'i rang bzhin las//'gro ba 'dul phyir chos sku bstan//'khor gyos dregs pa pho mor bsten//dkyil 'khor yongs rdzogs par mdzad/ces gsung so//de nas bac+doMs kyis khro bo 'byung ba'i dgongs pa/
83a1(tsa): ma la zhugs pas//'jig rten gsum po rab tu g.yos te//ldeg rab tu ldeg cing kun tu ldeg/'ur 'ur rab tu lurur kun tu 'u'o//ches ches rab tu ches kun tu rag tu che so//mtha' mtho na dadus dus /dus/
83a2(tsa): mtho na mtha' dma' bar gyur to//'jig rten gsum po 'jig la khang nas//'khor thad rgyal te lus sa la brdegs par gyur to//de nas bac+doMs kyis las thad yongs rdzogs par gyur pa'i phyir nga ro chen pos sdig so//he
83a3(tsa): hUr thums dza zhes bsgrags pas ka 'jig rten gsum dang bcas pa'i srog phrog par gyur to//der phyogs bcu'i bde bar gshegs pa zad kyang //'khor der skad cig ma cig la lhags nas srog gi snying po thad gdongs gsol du phul lo//
83a4(tsa): bcom ldan 'das kyis rngam pa'i sgra bton pas srog dang las thaMd 'dus par gyur to//A A A e ka zhes rab tu brjod do//de nas bac+doMs rdo rje 'chang chen pos//'khor rnams la gzigs te/lhun gyis grub pa'i dbyer
83a5(tsa): 'di gsungaso//rang bzhin mi gnas mnyam nyid las//lhun grub yis gzhal yas khang //rin chen dur khrod brtan par gnas//phyi nang gnyis med rdal khyab gsal//gru bzhi sgo bzhi rta babs mdzes//rtse mo gnam lcags 'dar/
83a6(tsa): bcas mtho'//lha brgyad klu brgyad zhing dang ro//dra ba dra phyid zhing dang sbrul//glang chen 'brug dang seng+ge stag/rus sbal chu srin gnam lcags thog/zhing dang zhing chung thod bu khyung //kha khyer lha mo mchod sogs mdzesaka e yi pho
83a7(tsa): drang zod 'khor ba'i//lcags khar bcu drug stobs bcus//khyams dang gru chad sgo khyud ldan/rgyal chen sa yi pho brong na//klu 'bum mnyan khris bdag stong //phyogs skyong ma ho nyi zla'i gdan//gzhan yang zhing skyong nyis
83b1(tsa): dngas nA pa~M lA gnan rgyus g+hu myu/'bring nag dag chen dag+sa k+Sha 'dzags /d+ma gsum byug rgyu zhabs pazhir spyad //g.yas pa klu dkyang gtan dzA g+sa yos/ sa byung dgA daglegs dun skyu rlo 'khor stso la hU~M dza na
83b2(tsa): bebe bcon btun shing bsdigs 'dzub g.yon//glang chen la sogs dur khrod chad ka rigs bzhi rgyan dang rin po che ka dbu la khrag 'thung lnga dang sbrul//klu brgyad mgo bcang do shal snying ka/gnod sbyin mgo bcing de bzhin dbu//bdud mo
83b3(tsa): 'thing nag thod sbru la mkhyur//ya ma dregs pa rus sba la lhor//byA khyung spyi bo phrag so khyung //stag dang seng+ge sogs nu ma//zhe sdang gdug pa sta zur gnyis//khru chung phyi nang par khyams gang ka tsit+ta hUM zhes rtse ba'i sngags
83b4(tsa): thod mkhar bla kun mon pa bzhi ka/'o bas na bdud pho bdud mo dang //mon po rigs bzhi pho nyi'i tshul//lcags mkhar sha za ke kaM tshogs//gru chang sde brgyad dregs pa bcu//khyams la 'chi bdag bdud kyi tshogs//mon pa mon
83b5(tsa): mo rus sbal sprul//mdun na mgo dgu yuM ba dang //srin mo lcags thags bla rkun dgu/lhun gyis grub pa'i phyag rgyar gsal//yongasu rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor du//gnyis med rig pa'i rang bzhin du//ston pas btsa'
83b6(tsa): bzhi rdzogs par bshad//de nas yang bac+doMs la//rdo rje snying pos gsol pa//sprul paM las kyis sbyor ba la//rnam grangs du dang ldan par byed//dgos pa'i ci rdog chen po ci phyir 'brel//brnag pa'i mtha' ru gang gis bsdus//dgos
83b7(tsa): pas nges par bstan pas gang //gsang sngags ngo bo gang la bzhag/snga ma'i don 'di dgongs par gsungs//zhes gsol to//de nas bac+doMs rdo rje 'chang chen pos//dri ba lnga yi yon 'di gsung so//dang po mdo yi rtsa la gsum//
84a1(tsa): @phyi dang nab dang gsang ba'o//phyi la rtsa ba yan lag gnyis//rtsa ba gzir dang rang gshed dpag/yan lag 'khor lo lo thog gzhom//rdegs dgu gnyis pa'i lasu bshad//nang la rtsa ba yan lag gnyis
84a2(tsa): rtsa ba bzhi dang yan lag drug rgyu dang 'khor lo tsit+ta gsang //spogs dang dmar len rtsa bar 'brel//lo thog gzhom dang srog dbrog gzer//phyag rgyas srung dang ltas ngan na do//spyi dril lnga gnyis ldan par bshadaka gsang bskor drag
84a3(tsa): sngags mdo rtsa bzhi//dbang bsdus gab don drag po dgu/rtsa ba chen por mdor 'brel ba'o//dbang bsdud brgyad gnyis rtsartsa bzhi//bcu gnyis thun mong sbyor bar bshad//gab don rtsa ba dang ni yan lag go//rtsa ba bzhi gnyis gsad
84a4(tsa): dang gzir//yan lag thun mong khyad par dang //rtsa la 'gran drag pos 'joms pa'o//thun mong sbyor ba drug gnyis ldan//khyad par rang bshad rgyu dang ldan//kun gyis rjes gcod bzhi ru gsungs//rtsa la 'dren bcu gsum de bzhin lha
84a5(tsa): de bzhin lnga bcu gcig tu bshad//drag po mngon phyung sbyor ba lnga yis rdzogs//thun mongs sbyor ba de bzhin lnga dang ldan//khyad par las kyi rim pa de bzhin bzhi//gsang ba'i sbyor ba lnga gnyis yin//yang gsang sbyor ba rnam gnyi/
84a6(tsa): ldan na sbyor ba drug gnyis thabs dang ldan//mchog gi sbyor ba bcuka snying po don gyi sbyor ba gsum du gnas//thun mong sbyor ba drug po lnga//khyad par gsum dang gsang mchog gsum//bcu dang cig las sum bcur 'gyur/
84a7(tsa): de bzhin drag po'i las la drug bcu rtsa dkur bshad//dgos pa'i don chen gnyis dang 'brel ba bzhika mtha' ru bsreg dang 'khor lo mnan pas bsdu//dgongs pa'i don gsum de bzhin gsum//bgrod pa gsum gsum gsal
84b1(tsa): / sa zlu ga [ऽ] sa ga sus susu gsun mya sma
84b2(tsa): zug du rgyu / pas nas /6 rgyur gyu la// sgru ga ng+gA snyed/ nyid m+sa ga dI

84b4(tsa): de nas sa kyis 'khor de drug las chos 'da'i byung ba'i gni 'di gas so//snang skyod 'khor 'das bzlas gas rgya /rdzu byed du
84b5(tsa): na du k+sha// sh+tha da mig sa /rang byung gag gang nga bag med//rgyu med da byung //'ad pa'i rgyun rgyun dag nga //don tsu zla dus/
84b6(tsa): blo nyid//rgyu 'brus ro cig mnyam nyid dang //kun gsal 'bras bu don cig phyir//phyi nang chos kun+da rgyu rkyen bral//skam chags sdug bsngal stong bdag med//sdug bsngal nyid na yi shis te//'gro ba bu grol ye sangs
84b7(tsa): bden gnyis mi rtog bdag med//rtsol sgrub bya byed 'ching ba'i nad//rtsa ba kun 'dus don cig la//sgro ma sgyu byi rtsal med brgyan//chos kun skye med snying po rgyal med pas//namakha'i dpe bzhin stong bdag med//skye med na ma
84b8(tsa): rtsol sems bral//don dam chos nyid ma skyes pas//dpe med na mkha' lta bur bshad//rtsa ba gcig pas zod mkhyen//ngo bo med phyir cing sdong srid//ma skyes pas na thad bzung //sangs rgyas sku gsung yis thugs/na/
85a1(tsa): lus ngag bag chags la//sdang srid chos sku rnams kun gyi gzhi//snying po byang chub sems las byung //sangs rgyas zhing dang pho brang gnas//kun yin gang min rang rig nyid//yis thig le cig
85a2(tsa): gsal//srid pa gsum du snang ba yang ka lam lnga rgyud lnga gnas lnga 'khor//la sogs sdug bsngal mnyam la sogs//sdug bsngal rtsal med mnyam par gnas//thabs kyi phyag rgyas rang grol ba'o//chos sku skye 'ag gce
85a3(tsa): med bu las//drag sngags yas rol par shes//dpal tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las chos nyid don gtan la dbab pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas 'khor gyis dam pa rdo rje snying pos bac+doMs rdo 'chang chen
85a4(tsa): po la gsol pa/rgyud ces bya ba ji ltar lags ka 'khor zhes bgyi ba ji ltar dbye//tshe srog bdag po'i don gang lags//sbyor ba'i rim pas las gang byed//gsang sngags ngo gang gis rtags//ces gsol to//de nas bac+doMs
85a5(tsa): kyis dri ba lnga'i don rgyas par bsha do//rgyud ces bya ba gsum yin te//gzhi dang lam dang 'bras bu'i rgyud/lha spyod 'khor lo dbang dam tshig/lam pa'i rim pa sbyor ba'i tshad//ngo bo 'brel bcas rgyu dang rkyen//phyi dang nang dang gas
85a6(tsa): ba ste//rang bzhin gnas pa'i mngon byed pa'i//thubs kyi rgyud ni gsum dang ldan//de mtshon mngon pas rgyud ces bya//'khor lo zhesu bstan pa yang //rten 'brel bsam gtan gzugs shArnyan sngags//sgeg byed gnan pa yas
85a7(tsa): rig de yi rim pa rgyas pa na//snang srid phyi nang 'khor ba yi//chos rten 'brel bcu gnyis tshul gyis ni//rten 'brel srid pa'i 'khor lor bshad//rang lus brtan pa'i sku nyid gsal//spyi bo mgrin pa snying lte ba//gsang ba rjes
85b1(tsa): 'jug rten byag na/se ru sum bcu gnyis bcu drug/brgyad dbus pa sa sa drug bcu tshul bzhi /bzhi bcu phyag dang ldan par gnas//yon drug sngags du mas mdzes//hUM dang au dang e dang a/raM yaM dbus na rkyed dang sdu //srog
85b2(tsa): sir dri dang rgyu//de skrod phyag rgya chos skur gsal/kha sam gtan gnas pa'i 'khor lor bshad//gzugs bstan 'khor lo dur khrod kyi ka ro ras zhing lpags rgya shog la//rtse ba lnga dang rus pas sku//gang dmigs rten gyi 'khor lo//
85b3(tsa): bca'//kha sbyar rus sbal phag mo'i lhor//bcas la gzugs snyan 'khor lor bshad//gcod pa sngags kyi 'khor lo ni//mtshon cha'i 'khor lo me dang 'dra//srog gzugs rtsa gcod bag chags sgeg/g.yon skor 'og nas gtso/
85b4(tsa): pa ni//chu yi 'khor lo rlung dang 'dra//skyol zhing rim dal bla ga nas sdud//dbus kyi rta skyon rlung gi rkyen ka rkyen dang 'phrad nas ste ba 'khor//lte ba'i rkyen gyi rtsibs dag gcod//gcod pa sngags kyi 'khor lor bshad ka bsreg pa me
85b5(tsa): yi 'khor lo dag/sa chu dug gi rten 'brel gyis//sbyor ba kun srog tshe dpal bskyed//zho rgyas dbang drag lhun gyis grub//mchog tu me yi 'khor lo bshad//mnan pa 'khor lo las kyi mthar/kha sbyor pho mo'i thod pa la//rim pa
85b6(tsa): gsum bri steng dang 'og/rgya gram gru gsum sbrul dang mo//ri rab rus sbal mchod rten gyi//gnan sngags ming rus snying po khams//thod par snying po'i bcud bsdud la//gnang dgu mi nad 'joms dgu yin//gnan pa thod pa'i 'khor lor
85b7(tsa): gshad//ye shes rig pa'i 'khor lo yin//tha~Md lhun gyi grub pa yi//rol pa ma 'gags cir yang gsal//don gnyis mngon du spyod par byed//rang 'byung 'khor lo rang rig pas//yis nyi ma'i 'khor lor bshad//de phyir 'khor lo zhes
86a1(tsa): bshad//tshe srog bdag pos gang gsungs pa//snang srid 'khor 'das sdang stong gi/ston srog dang sbyor ba'i las//yon dbang po che bkug kun//'bru gnyis dag la bsdus nas 'chang /skye med rnam pa/
86a2(tsa): dbyingasu thi ma ka las dang sbyor ba spen zhing rgyas//de phyir tshe srog bdag por bshad//sbyor ba'i rim pas brgya brgyad bcung //che ba yon tan dum byed/gang tsam 'bras bu sbyin zhing rgyas//de byed sbyor ba'i lalu ba shang //gsung/
86a3(tsa): sngags ngo bo gnyis med las//'khor ba sgyu ma lhun gyis grub//gzhi rtsa med cing thog mtha' med//'das pa'i yis lhun gyis grub//gang thod cing snang byang chub sems//sems phyir ma gsal rang rig pa//bde ba chen por don
86a4(tsa): la thebs//de phyir gsang sngags ngo bor bshad//dpal tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las/ston bris pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas yang bac+doMs kyis rgyud kyi don gtan la dbab pa 'di gsung so//de yang rnam pa
86a5(tsa): bdun du dbab//gsum pa'i spyod pa'i khyad par ni//bya ba med par kun rdzogs pas//rtsol bral gdeng gsum ldan pa yi//blang dor re dogs med par spyod//sbyor sgrol bya ba gang byas kyang //sems phyir gang yang byas snang med///
86a6(tsa): las kyi sbyor ba du ma yang //gang ltar byas kyang 'bras bu 'byung //lam gnyis lnga yis sa skrod kyang //sems dpa'i lasu gol ba med//chags sdang lnga yi lam spyad kyang //'jig rten sa la gzhi tsa med//rang bzhin lhun grub
86a7(tsa): sbyospyong ba'i mchog/dmyal sogs tsha grang kun spyad kyang //'khor ba nyid na yis spyod//lha yi sku dang cha lugs dag/'dod dgu lhug par ci spyad kyang //snang stong gnyis med yis sku//gang ltar bltas pa de ltar snang //rtog sgrib
86b1(tsa): rgyung yan yangs kyi med/gtug gsus dran pa gyu //ma sa nyid na zhes byung /sa ga lugs das pa nga/tshul bu lalas byung/rgyan kya smrang thos tshul zhes sa pad+ma nas byug rlung
86b2(tsa): spyod par bshad //dpal tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las/ka bya ba med pa'i spyod pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////de nas brjes kyis brtul zhugs bgrod pa'i dbang 'di gsung so//slob ma dbang gzhan thob pa la//bskyod pa mthar phyin rdzog/
86b3(tsa): po'i dbang ka ngo bo sems kyi chos nyid la//rnam grangs gnyis dang rdzas kyi dbang //rig pa brtul zhugs rang dbang rdzogs//dper na rgyal po'i phru gu la//rgyal sar ma bton sa mi thob//dbang 'thob rgyal srid mda' mnyes tshul//bdag/
86b4(tsa): dbang mtshon pa'i rim pa ni//sems nyid dben pa'i bde gnasu ka rang nyams brtan pa'i mtshams gcad naka maN+Dal chen po'i bya ba tsam//sngon du 'gro ba'i las rdzogs nas//dur khrod brdal ba'i rgyu blangs laka thugs kyi sprul pa mnya
86b5(tsa): khyab sgro//gug rgyal dri yis khrus byas laka maN+Dal dbu su brtan par bzhag/bsam gtan bum pas rten sbyangs la//lus la gshin rje'i dkyor rtag/tsit+ta 'od kyi pho brang du//tshe srog bdag po'i dkyor brtag/bstan pa spyod phyir
86b6(tsa): tshogs gtor la//bya ba'i rim pa bsgrub pa tsam//brtul zhugs rim pa'i rten du bzhag/mtshan ldan bkra shis bzung ma sngam//rgyal rigs rje rigs sngags skyes ma//mkha' khyab rdo rje mdangs ma reg lus dang yan+da lag dbang
86b7(tsa): bo 'jam//skye rings phra 'jam tshigs mi zul//dpral ba'i dbyes me sprin dgus ring //mig bre dkyil nag ut+pal mdog/shes rab spyod pa rna khyor ldan//lus la lha yi dkyil 'khor brtag/tsid+ha rdzogs pa'i dpyir brtag/
87a1(tsa): @b+ha ga gsang ba'i pho brang du/lhun gyis grub pa'i dgying brtag/bde ba chen po'i brten du bzung //rdo rje gsang ba'i chu rgyun gyis//dkying lnga dang dkyil 'khor kru/gsang ba lhun gyis grub pa'o//dkying lha la gsol pa/
87a2(tsa): gdab//byin rlabs brtul zhugs 'jug pa brtsam//yol bas bar bcad me tog ldan//sdig pa bshags la sdom pa dbog/dam tshig bsgrags la me tog dor//dkyer lha yi rnams pa bstan//lce la bdud rtsi lung bstan bzhag/bu de rgyan
87a3(tsa): zhing phyag rgyar bsgom//tshogs la rol la lha ngo bstan//rdzas dang dkyor byin gyis brlab//slob ma gzhug phyir gsol gdab//lhun grub rang bzhin gnas pa'i pho brang nas//yais rgya mtsho lhun gyis grub pa'i lha//ma lus mnyam pa'i rgyal po
87a4(tsa): dgongastsol//slob ma 'dul ba'i dkyil 'khor du//brtul zhugs drag po'i stobs kyis bzhugasol//bde chen mnyam pa'i pho brang gsang ba ru ka rig pa'i brtul zhugs stobs kyis bzhugastsol//slob mas me tog thogs la brda' phyag
87a5(tsa): btsal la gsol ba gtab po//ston pa'i sbrel ma bcom ldan 'das//lha dang slob dpon dgongastsol//dregs pa stobs kyi tshul bstan pa//'gyur med bde chen pho brang nas//dregs pa'i lha tshogs dgongasu la//b+ha ga gsang ba'i pho brang
87a6(tsa): nas/mnyam pa'i rgyal po dgong stsol//dam tshigs dka' thub sdom pa'i mchog/rig pa'i rgyal po bdag la gsol//rig pa'i rgyal po bdag la gsol//gsang ba'i yis bdag la gsol//yais rol pa bdag la gsol//stog
87a7(tsa): rin chen dmigs bsdus la//bde chen phyi yis 'jug pa tsam//phung po lha yi dkyil 'khor bstan//baM chen thugs kyi dkyir bstan//'jug pa nang gi lha ngo bstan//gzungs ma lus kyi dkying du//gsang ba phyi yi lha ngo bstan//tsit+ta/
87b1(tsa): nang gi lha ngo bstan//b+ha ga gsang ba'i pho brang du ka gdong g.yogs dang ldan spro ba skyed/bkra shis mtshan ldan gsang ba'i pho brang du//gnyis med sangs rgyas nyid kyang mnyam sbyor ba'i//byang chub sems nyid mi 'gyur bde ba'i mchog/
87b2(tsa): pho brang lhun gyis grub par bzhugs pa dang //rme ba lnga dang bdud rtsir sbyor ba'i sems//bkra shis mtshan ldan bde ba'i bzhugs sbyin//mi 'gyur bstan pa'i sku dang yais bgrod//de nas gsang ba'i gzungs ma skye ba skyed par bya'o//
87b3(tsa): bde chen gsang ba'i pho brang du//thabs chen gsang ba'i bde phab nas//dbu ma'i rtsa rgyud sa bon srog/b+ha ga'i gnasu chos sku bskyol//rtsa lam 'od 'phrod longs spyod sku//mnyam par gnas pa byang chub sku//spros bral kun khyab byang chu
87b4(tsa): sku bstan pa thob pa rdo rje'i sku//yuM ba mnyes pas mnyes par bya'o//de nas gsang ba sbyor ba'i brdas//yai sa nyi zla'i thig l-i'i bde bas gang bar byas la//sbyor ba dag pa'i byin brlabs las//rtsa rgyud bde phab theg ler bskyil/
87b5(tsa): byang chub shugs dang sme ba bsre//bkra shis mtshan ldan bum par bskyil//brtul zhugs rab gnas dbang bskur bya//ho rigs pa'i stobs ldan sbyor ba'i gnas//'khrul pa'i bag chags sprin ltar pu/yis rol pa'i sgrol ma gsal
87b6(tsa): mnyam pa'i shugs kyi brtul zhugs long //e ka ye ma pra tits+tsha ho//me tog dor la mgo la gdams//mutshan ldan bkra shis pho brang dang //drag po stobs kyis lha ngo bstan//bkra shis bum pas dbang bskur bya//hi yaMs lhun
87b7(tsa): gra bum pa yI/brtul zhugs dag pas dbang bskur bas//'khor ba rnam dag chos skur gsal//rang byung yis dbang thob shog/e ka ya ma gud+ya//b+ha ga d+ho d+hi tsit+ta ho//'bras bu khrag 'thung yis ldang /rdzogs pa'i phyag rgya rkun
88a1(tsa): @#/grub pa'o//de nas dung khrod kyas gyi dbang sbyin pa ni//glang men sdug gags zhing lpags sprul//thod pa snying phreng keng sa can//ma ri du rgod nga zung zag/dregs pa na byams dang ldan pa cag ga lag
88a2(tsa): sta ma hA nA ga kA pa li/tsit+tA dza la ni ri ho//de nas phyag mtshan sbyin pa 'am//ho 'khor ba dral byed 'khor lo dang //nyon mongs gcod pa'i ral gri dang //chos nyid mtshon pa'i rdo rje yis//sku gsum thugs kyi dbang thob shog/e ka/
88a3(tsa): ya ma tsag kre kha ta kha ta badz+ra ho//be con dug lnga yis lnga ru sgrol//gtun shing gdug pa gnasu 'joms//sdigs 'dzug dug gsum sku gsum dang rdzogs//rigs pa stobs kyi sgo gsum dbang thob shog/e ka ya ma b+hi ha hu/
88a4(tsa): sti sa rA pa ho//mnyam pa'i brtul zhugs sbyin pa ni//ho rdo rje pad+ma mnyam sbyar nas//pad+ma rA ga byung chub ses s+ya//baM chen skye bya don dang sbyar//brtul zhugs rol pa'i dbang bkur bas//myi zad chun gyi dbang thob shog+sa/e kA ya mahA/
88a5(tsa): h+ya rag+ta/b+ho d+hi tsit+ta ho//maM sa rak+ta tsit+ta ho//s+phu ta keng ni ti ho//dpa' brtul sngags kyi byin bskyed pa ni//ba~M chen rol mnyam pa ri ro la/rdo rje brjid pa'i dbang thob nas//bde chen mnyam nyid thob par shog/e ka ya a tI pa ta sid+d+hi ho//
88a6(tsa): sa spyong dag kyang dbang bsku bya//ma ma rang chung las sa yin//bkra gsu mnan rdzas rnams dang //'phel 'tshwab med pa'i yang bku bas//rigs lnga seMs m+b+ha y-i dbyung zlab shog+sa/e ka ya sa sarba ba rgyas /bdUn d+hA/
88a7(tsa): tshod pa gis dngos grub kyi ro//mi 'gyur ban po'i rgyal nas//rang rtags ma yis zeng bsrung bas/'am med drag pa'i ba tho ba shed/i ka ya ma sa sre dz+nyAn ma rkyed/rjod kyis 'jig rten gtang sdod/'phu gyi 'dzis rten no
88b1(tsa): mang gsal//'byung ba rnam gtor ma'i rgyu//lhun grub kun 'byung gtor ma'i rgyan//'dzin chags med pa'i stobs kyis rol//e ka ya ma ma hA ba li ta ho//dam tshig sngags la mna' chu blung bar bya'o//snod med brtul du/
88b2(tsa): zhan pa la/ka/spel zhing sgrags na rdo rje khro bo dang //gtum po gnod sbyin rdo rje dbang chen gyis//lus dral mgo bshags tsit+ta khrag tu 'thung //e ka ya ma kA ya bAk+ka tsit+ta rag ta kha hi ho//khyod kyi rdo rje 'chang nga yin pas//bka' yi
88b3(tsa): zur yang bcag par bya ba men//sgo ba byos la dam tshigs srog ltar bsrungs//gal te 'da' na rdo rje dmyal bar lhung //e ka ya ma du na rag taM ho//'di ni rdo rje dmyal ba'i chuka /tshul 'di bsrungs na dngos grub thob//nyams na tsit+ta
88b4(tsa): ga shakas te 'phro//rdo rje chu 'di khyod kyis long //badz+ra ti kro d+ha e/dam ho//'dod pa lnga yi tshogs la rol//rgyud gsum lhug par man ngag bstan//rdo rje 'dzin pa'i gral thob yin//brtul zhugs gsang ba'i 'grod pa gsum
88b5(tsa): thob ni mi ldog la gnas//brtul zhugs gsad pa'i dbang du ba shad//dpal tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las//brtul zhugs dbang gyi le'u ste lnga'o///de nas yang bac+doMs kyis dam tshig gzungs kyi don 'di gsung so//
88b6(tsa): de dag rgyas po 'di lta ste/dka' thub dam tshig sdom pa'i mdo//chos rnams ma lus byang chub sems//'khor ba lhun grub dang du 'dus//de mtshon dam tshig srog ltar bsrung //dam tshig rnam pa gnyis yin te//thun mongs
88b7(tsa): khyad par rim par bsrungs//thun mongs rtsa ba yan lag las ka/sku gsung thugs dang bco lnga yis//bsdus te bsrungs mtshams shes pa'o//khyad par kho na dka' thub dang //bsrung ba bcu gnyis gcig tu gsungs//gsang ba'i lam ni
89a1(tsa): @#rtogs bral ma mi bzung //drag sngags thun tshams khri bar gtam mi bya//'jig rten pa dang dman la phyag mi 'tshal//khro bo chas dgu gar dasu gsal bar brtag/mtshan ldan gzungs pa thang dor med par sbyang //rdo rje paH/
89a2(tsa): rdzingam gshig sngon du spyad//rdo rje re dogs som nyi med par gzir zhing gzhom//tsha ba gsum dang rul sum myos pas bcad//'khor lo gzir mtha' sbyin sreg gnan pa bzor//lus kyi dbang po gzhan gyis mthong mi bya//shes rab spyong/
89a3(tsa): bla ma'i zur mi bcag/khyad par dag las kho na'i dka' thub bshad//sprul pa'i khyad par dam tshig bsrung bar bsrungs//rdo rlung gi ri mi gshig/mngon 'byung brtul zhugs dog med spyad//gcan zan rgyal po gsad mi bya//za ma traH/
89a4(tsa): dug mi blugs//rin chen lhag phrag sreg mi bya//gangs kyi zhun chu gtung ba min//bdud rtsi snod ngan nang mi blugs//me shigs snod du mdangs mi dbab//snod ngan seng+ge'i zho mi blugs//yid bzhin nor bu sreg mi bya//bya khyung gshogs
89a5(tsa): pa bcag pa min//gnas lcags ur mo nang mi 'thab//stags gzigs drol ba'i lhag mi gtung //rdo rje gtams pa brag mi gshig/rdo rje ra ba'i dmang mi dral//mun pa sel ba'i sgrol mi gsad//rdo rje chu bo'i bka' mi bcag/rgyal pos
89a6(tsa): phyag rgya rtag mi dbye//rdo rje rdzong gi brag mi bshig/rin chen rgyal mtshan rtse mo dbog/lhag pa'i dam tshig bsrung bar bshad//brtul zhugs lam las cig car bgrod//'bras bu bgrod thob lhun gyis grub//las dang sbyor ba kun dang
89a7(tsa): ldan na mthu stobs byin rlabs dashugs kyis 'byung //dam can rgya mtsho der gnas 'grub//de ma yin na mdze dang g.yed//dus mo ni tshe zad mnar med 'pho//legs nyes bgrong med gnyi bar bye//bsrung las 'bang zhing gnyi su med//rang
89b1(tsa): bzhin gnas dang shugs kyis rdzogs//lta ba'i dam tshig chen por bshad//dpal tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las//dka' thub dam tshig gi le'u ste/drug pa'o///de nas 'khor gyi nang nas rdo snying pos bstan las langs nas//
89b2(tsa): bac+doMs la gsol pa//rgyal ba kun gyi rang bzhin dngos//gsang sngags ngo bo de nyid lags//'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten pa'i//dregs pa mthu rtsal ldan pa kun//bag bzhi nye ba ci yi phyir//lhun grub phyag rgya zil gnon sku//gnyen po gang
89b3(tsa): gis 'dul ba legs//drag po'i 'dul bya gang lags pa//'khor la rgyas par bsha du gsol//zhes gsol bas//bac+doMs kyis nyi ma gsum pa'i don 'di gsungaso//bskal pa rdzogs ldan bya ba lags//rigs las gol ba'i dregs pa lnga
89b4(tsa): nag po gsod byed thoms pa'i gtso//srin mo kha 'dar khrag skyug gtso//rtsub 'khor sro gcod yag sha'i gtso//zhe sdang gdug pa sbrul mgo ri gtso//dregs pa bcwo brgyad bgegs rigs stong phrag brgyad bcu'i gtso//de yi bdag po phud byed chen/
89b5(tsa): kA li A yu 'chang gis dregs//'byung ba bzhi yi rngams 'og nas//lus chen yan lag rags la rngams//sgra dang kha spu a ga tshom rtsu ba//stod smad srin po shun gyi gos//mgo dang snying phreng sbrul gyis brgyan ke/mtshon ma me rlung ha
89b6(tsa): la'i dpal/thog dang se ra ba bu yug 'bebs//sgyu ma glog snying thor khrag 'thung //nag po sder chen mi lpags gon//tha~Md rlon 'bru mar 'chang khrag 'thung //bran mau g.yog mo phra men ma//rigs ngan mo rnams 'khor du byas//tshags pa
89b7(tsa): me rlung rab tu 'khol//srog chags kun gsad mngal du skyes//rnam bzhi 'khor la gnod cing 'tshe//myur du gsod cing sha rmon pa//ded dpon bzhi ni 'khor la spong //de lta bu li kA li lngag sangs rgyas bstan pa bshig la nye//'gro ba ma lus
90a1(tsa): @#phungs khang //de dag 'dus bying sangs rgyas kyu//phyag rgya zil gnon 'jigs par ston//de yang ji lta bu zhe na//lha yi dbang phyug ded dpon rgyal ga ru srid ma mo rigs kyi bdag por grags bcig/gsang sngags rig su de/
