100b4(tsa): brgya khri ni stong 'khor ba'i gzhal yas nas//lcags mkhar sgo med bcu drug 'bar ba'i nab shes nas//'obs chen bcu gnyis 'bar ba'i dkyil 'khor nas//thod mkhar 'bar ba'i dbus phyogs nas//pho brang waT mkhar
100b5(tsa): spu gris gtams pa nas//klu 'bum gnyan khri sa bdag stong gi gang naka lto 'phye phyogs skyong ma he rdzis pa'i khri//thabs dang shes rab nyid zla'i gdan stengs nas//srod bdud 'jam dpal tshe bdag nag po bzhengs//rngam pa'i tshul
100b6(tsa): gyis bla rkun dgra la khye//tshe bdag las kyi sprul pa mngon phyung ba//shar gyi phyogs nas bla 'gugs pa//dug gi non pa eng+ge'i mgo/dbu dgu spyan dgu zhal gdangs//phyag dgu zhabs dgu tsi t+ta dgu//zhabs dgus stong khams bla la rgyug/spyan dgu/
100b7(tsa): drag po ltas ngan 'dzin/snying dgus dgra zo yod la 'dzin//phyag dgus ci d+ha khong nas 'byin//zhal dgus kha mngang med pa gsol/ldag sto dgus khrag la myos shing thung ngan po zla di grol los shog/ka la dun te tsi d+ha ro lcags srin yo /
101a1(tsa): @la thun ri li li b+h+yo//lho yi phyogs nas bla 'gugs pa//lcags kyi mon pa stag gi mgo/dbu dgu baspyan dgu zhal dgu gdangs//phyag dgu zhabs dgu tsi d+ha dgu//zhabs dgu stong khams bla ga rgyu//spyan dgus dgra bo ltas
101a2(tsa): ngan 'dzin//snying dgus dgra bo yid la 'dzin//phyag dgus tsit+ta khong nas 'byin//zhal dgus kha mngar med par gsol//ljags dgus khrag la myos shing 'thung //dgra bo'i bla dang grol la 'ashog/ha la ha dun ti de tsit+ta ro myags srog la thum
101a3(tsa): ri li b+h+yo//nub kyi phyogs nas bla 'gugs pa//bse yaM mon pa dred gyi mgo/dbu dgu phyag dgu zhal dgu gdangs//ljags dgus khrag la myos shing 'thung //phyag dgus zhabs dgu tsit+ta dgu/zhabs dgus stong khams zla la rgyug/
101a4(tsa): spyan dgus dgra bos ltas ngan 'dzin//snying dgus dgra bo yid la 'dzin//phyag dgus tsit+ta khong nas 'byin//zhal dgus kha mngar med par gsol//ljags dgus khrag la myos shing 'thung //dgra bo'i bla dang phrol las shog/ra ga ho ti du na tig
101a5(tsa): ro myogs srog la thum ri li li b+h+yo//byang gi phyogs nas bla 'gugs//gser gyi mon pa khyung chen mgo/dbu dgu spyan dgu zhal dgu gdangs//phyag dgu zhabs dgu tsit+ta dgu/zhabs dgus stong khams dgra la rgyud//spyan dgus dgra bo lta
101a6(tsa): na 'dzin//snying dgus dgu bo yid la 'dzin//phyag dgus tsit+ta khong nas 'byin//zhal dgus kha mngar med par gsol//ljags dgus khrag la myos shing 'thung //dag po'i blo dang phrol las shog/karma b+ha ting du ni ting ro myogs sogs la brum
101a7(tsa): ri l-i li b+h+yo//dgu bskor mthun dbab sgrub pa bya//srog bdud tshe bdag nag po ni//sku yang che la dregs shing rngams//sku ni na mkha'i khams dang mnyam//thugs ni stong gsum chang par lta//zhal gdangs hom khungs gdengs pa 'dra//ljags ni
101b1(tsa): ho ma zor rgyad pa 'dra//tshems ni hom shing btsugs pa 'dra//mche ba gtsang phur gtams pa 'dra//khyod ni srog 'gugs bdag po yin//zhal gdangs glu gsum pas gdangs shig/sdang dag gnod bgegs kha raM khA hi//yuM chen gsod byed
101b2(tsa): rgyal mo na/ma thing nag 'jigs tshul rnga ma stabs 'gying //nad gtong rgyal mo zhal gdangs shig/dgra bgegs sta ba so kha raM khA hi//rus sbal//gsod byed ya ma rA dza ni//smug nage 'jigs pa zhal gdangs shig/sdang dgra gnod bsheg
101b3(tsa): kha raM khA hi//dregs pa phyag na rdo rje nikastobs chen drag po zhal gdangs shig/sdang dgu gnod bgegs khaM raM khA hi//rus sbal rgyal po mi zan ni ka tshe srog 'khyams pa zhal gdangs shig/gsang dgra gnod bgegs kha raM khA hi//bya khyud rgyal
101b4(tsa): po ka ru na//klu 'dul mkha' lding zhal gdangs shing //sdang dgra gnod bgegs kha raM khA hi//sgrol byed tshe bdag mgo dgu pa//sprul pa chen po zhal gdangs shig/sdang dgra gnod bgegs kha r-i khA hi//bdud mo khrag gi ral pa can//nad /
101b5(tsa): ngang srin mo zhal gdangs shig/sdang dgra gnod bgegs kha raM khA hi//sum bcu rtsa gsum gnas kyi yangs stengs na//bdud ye med 'khrul gyi rgyal po ba zhugs ka 'chi bdag rgyal po zhal gdangs bshig/sdang dgra gnod
101b6(tsa): bgegs kha raM khA hi//rgya mtsho chen po'i gting ru ma nas kaMg shin rje bye ba dgu khri bzhugs//las kyi mtha' sdud zhal gdangs shig/sdang dgra gnod bgegs kha raM khA hi//de nas bac+doMs kyis dregs pa'i tshogs la bstod pa//mchod
101b7(tsa): pab stabs pa 'di gsung so//chos kyi dbyings kyi pho brang nas//drag po oM yi gzhal yas nas//klu 'bum gnyan khris bdag stong gi gad ni//lto 'phye phyogs skyong ma he brdzis pa'i khri//thabs dang shes rab nyi zla'i gdan stengs nas
102a1(tsa): dug lnga brkam chags 'joms pa'i lha//srog bdud tshe bdag nag po ni//gtum chen dus mtha' bskal pa'i me bar//sku mdog nag po bdud chen bskyil ba 'dra//sku yang rngam chen dregs pa'i sku//
102a2(tsa): dbu gsum phyag drug zhabs bzhi bgrang //g.yas dkar klu brgyad zhal du gsol//g.yon dmar mnyan khri klu 'bum gsol//dbus kyi 'thing nag rngam pa'i yis//sku dgur gyur pa'i las ngan dang //sa bdag klu srin brgyad kyi//
102a3(tsa): dbang po lnga dang tsit+ta gsol//phyag drug mtshon cha 'bar ba yis//zhe sdang klu gnyen sa bdag dang //dgra bgegs mgo lus tshal bar shog/sku la dur khrod rgyan chas dang //klu chen brgyad kyi mgo bcad nas//do shal snying gi phreng bas
102a4(tsa): brgya na//dbu la bdud dang srin po dang //btsan dang mu stegs rgyal po dang //mgo thod pa'i phreng bas brgyan rgya la rigs gnyen gyi grong khyer brgyan//rje rigs mgo dku tshangs bskur brgyan//bram ze'i rigs kyi phyag gdub
102a5(tsa): brgyan//dam pa rigs zhabs gdub lha tshig brgyan//rigs ngan rdol ba zhabs 'og gting /ba so rigs drug khri tsang phur gtams//spyan nas dug gi tsha tsha 'phro//dbu ska smar ba spu las//gdug rtsu ba dmar nag me tshon 'bar//
102a6(tsa): dam nyams dgra bo sdig can dang //gdug pa'i klu gnyan sa bdag sreg/gang la bya ba khyod shes kyis//dmigs pa gang yin mngon du sgrol//lus srog dbang po rten dang phrol//'byung ba rnam shes bkrag mdangs bsdus//zhe sdang /
102a7(tsa): sreg pa'i sbyor ba chom//sreg rdzas mchod pa 'di bzhes la//khrag dang bag chags bla mtsho skoms//gdug tshug me lha'i zhal du gsol//sdong pa'i dgra dang gnod pa'i bgegs//mchod par 'bu lo kha raM khA hi//g.yon gyi phyogs nas yum//
102b1(tsa): gcig sa//bdu ma gsang byed rgyal mo ni//sku mdag mthing nag brjid pa las//dngu la sbrul rag ral pas ga sa//ram bzang wa re khA/gser dang sa tig rgyun 'byung /rdom pa'i rA med pa mas//dbyu byan
102b2(tsa): sku tshigs brgyad//zhal nas mi ro t+tA ro 'phrul te/spyan nas khrag 'dzag Tu lu lu//shangs nas rlung dag tshub mar 'khyil//g.yas pas dgra bo'i snying phyung ma gyur//g.yon pa ljang lor snyed khrig s+t+wa~M hA//drag tu bskul zhing sdud tsa/
102b3(tsa): ma mo'i gi gang dgra la 'bebs//mo gdon 'bum ste gzir zhi 'joms//gang la bya ba khyod shes kyis//dmigs pa gang yin mngon du sgrol//lus srog man chad yo la 'dre//tshe bdag thugs kyi dgying nas//thugs kyi sprul pa mngon
102b4(tsa): phyung na//gsod byed ya ma rA dzi na//sku mdog skugs nas 'jigs tshul can//rngam zhing dreg pa'i dgra bgegs 'dul//rna ba'i grong khyer sbrul gyis brgyan//tshangs pa'i mgo gcad dong kal byas//bdud dang mu stegs rgyal po yis//
102b5(tsa): tsi t+ta phreng bas brgyan du byas//g.yas pa 'khor lo rtsibs pa rnams//nyams pa kun gyis mgo lus bshog/g.yon pas tsit+ta bton nas bsnams//dgra bo'i don snying sbar gyis 'debs//rngam zhing dregs pa'i cha byad kyis//sde brgyad
102b6(tsa): kun gyi sbyor ba 'joms//bskul zhing drag tu rbad tsa na//sa bdag klu gnyan gzir zhing mnan//klu nad mdze shu ka ru mchod//gang la bya ba khyod shes kyis//dmigs pa gang yin mngon du bsgrol//ya ma thugs kyi dkyil 'khor na//thu
102b7(tsa): kyis sprul pa mngon phyung ba//dregs pa phyag na rdo rje yi//sku mdog ljang nag brjid pa ni//phrin las sna tshogs mdzad pa'i rtags//sku yi 'gying bag brjid pa ni//gdug pa tha~Md 'dul ba'i rtags//zhal dang mche ba gzigs pa ni//
103a1(tsa): /khams gsum dbang du sud pa 'i rtags//mcheb dgu khri gtamal pa yi//dgra ma ma srog rtsa gcong ba'i rtags//klu brgyad gdan du 'di ba nA~M//klu gnyanas bdag gzing ba'i rtags//gnyan waM klu 'gyu
103a2(tsa): gsol ba nI//klu nang mdze shu ka ru gcod pa'i rtags//rigs bzhi sbrul gyis sku laM brgyan pa ni//klu dang mdze shu dag la btong ba'i rtags//rin po oM yi sku la brgyan pa ni//'dod gu re ba yid bzhin sbreng ba'i rtags//phyag las rdo rje
103a3(tsa): dgu bsnams pa ni//ngas nyams mgo lus gshog pa'i rtags//g.yon pa tsit+ta phreng ba bsnams pa ni//dgra bo'i don snying khong nas 'byin pa'i rtags//glang la bya ba khyod shes kyis//dmigs pa gang yin mngon du bsgrol//dregs pa chen po'i
103a4(tsa): thugs kyi dkyor nas//thugs kyi sprul pa mngon phyung ba//rus sbal rngam pa mi zan ni//chags sdang lnga yi rtsa ba gcod//'byung ba lnga yi rtsa ba la bskyems//lus srogs gnyis kyis rtsa mdangs 'brel//skye ba med pa'i khams la sdud///
103a5(tsa): dngas pa khrag gi snying po 'chang //thugs kyi gsad mdzod 'bar ba ru ka bkram dngas bcud kyis snying po 'chang //dkon mchog dbu 'phang smod pa dang //rgyal ba'i bkar la bcog pa dang //sdang ba'i dgra dang gnod pa'i bgegs//sku dgrar 'os
103a6(tsa): pa ma lus kunaka gshed kyi gsang mdzod par ba chug/rtag tu 'khyam pa'i gnasu khrid//rtsa ba'i sngags kyis rdeg cing gzir//dmod pa drag por mtshon ma mchod//yan lag lnga dang gsang ba las//mchod rten drug gi dgra ba gegs gnon
103a7(tsa): dgra bgegs gdun pa las ngan gdug pa rnams//spu gri gnas la shor re//tshe bdag nag po'i spyi gtsug na//gser gyi bya khyung ka ru na//rgya khyung rgyal po dam tshig can//bya khyung rgyal po mthu bo che//'khor lo 'bar ba'i gshog rtsa
103b1(tsa): nyams pa'i mgo lus tshal bar gshog/ral gri 'bar ba'i gshog pa can//dgra bo'i srog rtsa rtsad nas gcod//s+thu grI 'bar ba'i the gshog gis//dgra bo nyams pa'i srog lus grol ka/yis 'bar ba'i gshog zer gyis//nyon mongs gdu/
103b2(tsa): rtsub rtsa nas sreg/spu bran spu gri dpal men gyis//nyams pa kun gyis srog rtsi chod//spyad gnyis nyi zla khyil po yis//zhe sdang klu mtsho gting nas snyoms//gnas ljags 'bar ba'i sder mo yis//klu gnyan sa bdag drangs kyin gsol//
103b3(tsa): gnam lcags 'bar ba'i rdo rje mchus//sa bdag chu srin klu bdud gsol//khyung gi gshog pa brdabs pa yis//gdug pa'i gnas khang dmangs nas gshig/khyung chen rngam pa'i nga ro yis//klu gnyan sa bdag zil gyis gnon//gang la bya ba khyod /
103b4(tsa): //dmigs pa gang yin mngon du sgol//yang sbrul dug gi khyung chen gnyis//mtshon ma' bar ba'i gshog pa yis//gdug pa 'dul ba'i las ngo long //bsreg rdzas mchod pha 'di zhes la//klu nad mdze ku ka ru chod//stag dang seng+ge rngams pa yis
103b5(tsa): nyams pa'i sha khrag ro la rol//zhe sdang dug sbrul nag po yis//kha nas me 'bar dug lnga sreg/dgra bo'i khong du dug mdar stsol//dgra bo'i mkhar ni rlangs nas ba shig/dmigs pa gang yin mngon du sgrol//shar gyi phyogs nas bla 'gun
103b6(tsa): ba dug gi skyen gang ba serge khaM ma go/men me blo kun rngas pa'i gdong //snang dgra gnod pa gegs mchong ba bzhes//lho mi phyogs nas bla dgugs pa//ljags kyi mon pa stug gi sago/son pa rag po gsuM yi ral ba ca//snang dgra gats+tsha
103b7(tsa): /bgegs mching bar gzheg/khub kyi phyogs nas zla 'gugs pa/gas yi mon pa dred kyi man/man ma me srog lal ba cad//sdang dgra gnang bgegs mchong bar ba zhes/byang gi phyogs nas zla 'khrugs pa//gser gyi son pa khrung me ma go//
104a1(tsa): @son so zla kun snying la gzer 'debs ma//sngang glu gnang ba gegs mchod bar bzhes//he las khyab pa shed gyis bdud//bdud po mkhu 'gro ral cig sa//so 'dab 'khrugs pa lha me bdud//bdud po khrag 'jo /
104a2(tsa): byed ma//nag mo 'dzub med nub kyi bdud//bdud mo rdo rje mche gtsigs ma//ral pa spyi bcings byang gi bdud//bdud mo las kyi khro gnyer ma//rgya mtsho chen po'i gting rum nas//dug mtsho 'khyil ba'i nang shes nas//gshin rje bye
104a3(tsa): pa rigu khri bzhugs//chibasu chu glang sngon po chibs//sku la lcags kyi zhun khrag gsol//thod pa zhing chung sbrul gyis brgyan//g.yas na ral gril kha mangs bsnams//dam nyams srog rtsa dmar du gcod //phyag na lcags kyi ri rab
104a4(tsa): bsnams//dgra bgegs las ngan gzer zhing sreg/leg pa dgu khris tshogs kyang 'gyed//yang sbrul bye ba las la bgyed//de phyir bye ba dgu khris bya//las kyi mtha' sdud khyod yin gyis//sdang ba'i dgra dang gnod pa'i bgegs/
104a5(tsa): gdug pa'i klu gnyan sa bdog rnams//gtad to las kyi mtha' bsdus shig/dgra bgegs las ngan mchod par ba zhes//'o ma dug chu chang dang khrag/me la dbul zhing drag tu bzlas//drag po'i gnasu thun brabs la//thal
104a6(tsa): thun zor thod pa dbab//mnan pa gdug pa'i klu la btang //kha rlangs dug la mu zi//brun gsum tsha yi lus bdug/yid kyi dug la bya phag tshol//gu gul rta bong mu zis bdug/mthong ba'i dug la zla ba'i khra//bya dang nya yi 'khris
104a7(tsa): pas pa dug/reg pa'i dug la rdzas rigs kun//bsregs la bdug te phying bas dag pa//drag sngags bzlas shing yul la zad/yung kar brubs la bskor ba bya//bye ba sum brgya dza la gdungs//gug rgyal su yis gas sa gdus
104b1(tsa): dug dang khrag gi dar b+hud la/rkang dang sha ro bems la brdeg/thun brdeg zhal gyi ma mo pa dpab//gdug pa gsus pa'i dug ro gdon//bskyang ba kun gyi mchog tu bshad//dpal tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las//bsrung gA byang
104b2(tsa): par bstan pa'i le'u ste/bco brgyad pa'o////de nas yang bac+doMs kyis srog mar len gyi sbyor ba rang gshed rang la dbab pa'i sbyor ba 'di gsung so//dug gsum mchog gsum bde gan su//rang nyi mas brtan pa'i mtshams
104b3(tsa): gcad la//yai sa rig pa'i bde klong nas//mkha' lnga khyab pa'i 'khor bsdus la//gzung 'dzin chos nyid dbyingasu bsgral//stobs chen gzhan sogs gzan la dbab//rang rgyud yi ge gshed mar blang //bdud dang phan byed rang btsal
104b4(tsa): ngar du bde ba'i thig le dang las shar//shugs kyis rin chen 'phel 'grib med//ga gsum rkyen gnyis cha mnyam la//mu khyud rim pa bzhi ru bskor//dbus kyi pho brang rgya chen sar//spyi gtsug cog pu dkyil dkrung du//
104b5(tsa): dmigs pa nag po'i snying po bri//phyi rim rgya gram rtsa ba'i sngags//yang logs mu khyud g.yeng dang skyom//rang bzhin gang grub gsad pa'i sngags//phyi nang mgo bstan ming rus par//lto sbyor rang bshad gzan la dbab//mtha' mtshon
104b6(tsa): cha'i rtsi bas bcu bzhi//rtse mo nang bstan gsad pa'i sngags//spu gri rang bcas gsum gnyis 'pho//skabs dang sbyor ba rtsa ba brtsams//mtshon ma kha sbyor dug chu khrag/tsha ba gsum dang ldan byas la//ling ga phag mo'i lho/
104b7(tsa): bar bca'//gru gsum rtsegs rtsa nga bca su//thod pa rus sbal nang bcas ma nan//zhag sum drag sngags rngam tshig zlas//drag tu bskul la bla mas mngag/dog bcu dang bcas mtshan yang brjod//dbang gzugs dam gdar gdung ba
105a1(tsa): brlag/then nas mchog phur khri yis/gang ba//'khor lo thod pa chos kyis dril//na mkha' bar snangs la mnan//gsum gsum drag dang gsum du dbye//rang yang che ba'i nga rgyal bskyed//mgu ru seng+ge'i kho
105a2(tsa): gshig na//sgyu ma sku yi phung po la//thugs kyi myu gu bde ba'i mchog/rin po che yi rgyal sar gdon//thod pa seng+ge'i khog par mnan//slob ma ra la gzhu sdi gsog na//tsog pu'i snying khar sdig pa gzhug/dam gdar mda' chu bur
105a3(tsa): pas bsdigs//thod pa stag gis 'dzum la man na//dam 'dres yi dam sngags gcig na//seng+ge ma che sbyar gtser bu khrid//klad par khrag gis sbal ba bzhugs//me khrag nang khrag rkyen blangs bsku//thod pa gzigs gi thig ler man/
105a4(tsa): sngags pa nag po spyi yis spun//spyi gtsug snying khar bse sbal brika/bcug la rgod dza la'i khrag/thod pa mun pa'i gling du ma nan//bon po 'am rig pa 'dzin pa la//skyi la sdig pa klad par sbal//snying la phag rgod gsang bar sprul//
105a5(tsa): bcug la lus nad khrag sku//thod pa srin mo'i sgom su mnan//drag po gnam lcags nang 'gras na//rtog med mnyam nyid rdzogs pa'i sku//bde chen khyab gsal rtog med las//phyag rgya gzi brjid stobs dpag med//lus l-i yis
105a6(tsa): 'khor lo btsa'//kun gyi thugs khar sUr+ya yi//tsakra yis stong 'phros gsal//gzhan gyi rgyan gcig bris la gzir//rang gshed phyag bya reng la bskor//drag sngags rnam dkrug bsnol mar brdeg/thod pa 'brong gi thod mar mnan//de yis
105a7(tsa): thad 'joms par gsungs//de nas yang bcas kyis sku gsungs thugs rdo rje la//e myags la rngubs lugs shag ka dun//ga ru ni dza hur thums dza//srog la yam shag tra~M myags zad//dgra bo'i srog la mA ra ya nI yA//breng shag rngung
105b1(tsa): yaM zad//dgra tso li tsit+tA d+hu thum dza//srog la mA rA ya zad//dgra zo la tsi p+ta yo bshag ni hru thums dza/kha sgrog sun zad/dgra bo'i srog la du ma ni tsi d+hu dza//srog mA ra ya rbad//dgra zo li tsit+ta yaM hu brums dza/sgrog phreng shag sa ya \u0f7fsa
105b2(tsa): spu gri hidaka so so'i sgo na brdeg pa'o//de nas bac+doMs kyis//spyir dril la bdeg pa'i sbyor ba dmaH'di gsungaso//khyad par dmar len drag po yi//rang bshed dril la dbab pa'i las//brda don thabs kyi se grol ba'i mchog kha dang 'o ma zho dang
105b3(tsa): mar tu zhun mar snum gyis snying po bcud//snying po'i sbyor ba gsad pa mchog/g.yeng min rten par grol dbang bcas//stobs ldan sbyor ba'i snying chung 'di//gang mthun rgyal gzugs brgyan bya//dpa' gtul drag po'i stobs kyis sbyang //gdug byed
105b4(tsa): phag mo'i lto ba ru ka/srid pa'i khyams khang drod bsdud bcar//khyams khang lto na nag pa'i gzugs//spyi gtsug snying khar mtshon cha bcar//mu khyud bzhi dgas mi rus bri//yang logs bcus spu gri mtshon//rtsa ba spu gri'i bar na khyams//chu srin
105b5(tsa): khang pa mu khyud zlum//lto sbyor nang la phas kyi phyi//mu khyud bar du ming rus khams//bskul zhing lus la bzar kha dbab//rus sAl lto ba'i phyogs mtshams las//rtsibs chen bcus rtse mo ning //gsad pa phag mo'i spu gris gang //'khrul mi//
105b6(tsa): bar khyams ming rus gang /don gnyis bsdus la sbyor brtsams//kha sbyor dug khrag tsha ba dang //rme ba dang bcas thod pa dong //rgyu rten ling ga'i bca' ming //rus khams nag pa rten du bzhag/zhal gyi mthun dbab rten la sti sa//zhag sum khri bzhi sgra
105b7(tsa): spangs bya//drag sngags 'od zer ra zhugs 'bri//na po gsod byed po nya bskul//kud gyi yis 'od 'phro las//rang rgyud rtsa pa'i'ikhor lo las//snying gi rtsa bzhi dngas ma na//rtsa ba'i 'bru brgyad nal sa'i gnas/ma bran rgya nga yul bzhin/na po
106a1(tsa): gyis 'phrul bas spyas/dam ma can ma se sa/chos nyid rtsal e dang grags/sanyam pa nyid kyi bags gas 'tshams//gnyis lang 'a thaMng ma pas spyong /seMm sa mam sa su//dranas sh+tsa na sa r+b+ba /
106a2(tsa): rtad pa'i yis rang sdod 'joms//khrag dang tsha bcu lu bskyed//rang rgyung ser dngas sa rung //bag cags 'phel 'grib 'phen dang phral//ka da sa gnas rgya 'ang skyed//bya ba'i le da bdu 'ams//las ni mags
106a3(tsa): ma 'degs//de bskul de 'od de mtshon chas//de gcod de nyid sreg cing bskyems//de las de byung rtsa mdud bskul//mdud pa bzhi pa la mtshon cha'i 'bru//bzhi gnyis gsum gnyis snol mor gnas//chos nyid mdud pa srog la ni//
106a4(tsa): de bzhin rang rgyud hU ra thums dza//yon mdud pa byang khrag snying thugs nas//yais rim dang shag dang rbad//rang 'dod me dang mtshon char 'char//de yis de bcud bag chags sreg/rtse bran brgyad dang sul bzhi la//'od zer 'phro zhing
106a5(tsa): thi ma phog pas//nad dang mtshon chas rang sngags bskul//rang bshed bdud kyi mtshon char 'bebs//yis klong bu thig ler 'du/bag chags khrag 'khol me mar gsod//kun 'dus dkar nag mtsham su thim//hu thums dza yis ni
106a6(tsa): phogs nas//gzugs brnyan grogs kyi 'khor lor 'phro//srid pa'i khyams khang rten la sdud//de yis rang gshed rang la 'bebs//tshe dril drag po'i dug mda' yin//de nas bac+doMldas kyis nyid kyi sku gsungs thugs rdo lnga gcig tu dril
106b1(tsa): 'i drag sngags bsnol la bton to//e lugs ya rngubyas lugs shag kA d+hAH//dgra bo'i srog la hur thams de//rtsa khrag snying thams ri la raM shag cad//gA po'i cing phreng shag nga srog la d+hu thums rgyung //de dag sbyong bu dan
106b2(tsa): yin snang snang ma yin dril las brdeg/mnan pa dug pa'i klu la gtad//de yaM las de ma smin na//srid pa sngags kyi 'byung ba dkrug/mnan pa gnyen dang dkyir 'og/'khor lo drug shing bsnol du gzhug/srog rdums ri l-i ni dz+r-i/
106b3(tsa): snying baM la thun shagA rbad//dgra bo'i srog breng thums cit+ta shag hU ra dza la rbad//las kyi rgyal po mchod chen yin//de nas yang bac+doMs kyis nyid kyi sku gsung thugs rdo rje las gzir zhing mnan pa'i sngags 'di bton to//dgra bo srog
106b4(tsa): la hur thums dzi tsit+ta gzir gan na//srog snyam pa ya nan//dgra bo'i srog la thum ri li li b+h+yo//'di des las gsum rdzogs par byed//rang gshed dril ba'i sbyor bar bshad//dpal tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las//
106b5(tsa): rang gshed rang la dbab pa'i le'u ste/bcu dgu pa'o///de nas yang bac+doMs kyis srog len gsang ba'i dug mda' khong du bskyal ba'i sbyor ba 'di gsungaso//las rnams kun gyi mchog bsdus pa//rtsa ba gcig tu dril ba
106b6(tsa): yin/las kyi gnad dang skyod pa'i thabs//bar mtshams sbyor ba kun dang ldan//ri nags dur khrod sne dri snol//g.yul bkyes lu dgu rlangs la//stegs bu hom khung thod pa 'thol//khrag sna dug sna ling kha ming //bcas la maH/
106b7(tsa): kha dred mos rab pa//rdo rje lcogs bye zangs phye dang //thal chen bsnol khrag yung kar thudaka/gsal gsum kun sngags dgu sgor brdeg/thun pa nab sha za mtshon mar gas la//'od kyi lum bsdus phrin la this//khrag khol dug
107a1(tsa): @#rlangs khongs zhing 'chol//zor bskyod bsrung ba'i cha go ma dral//lus dang sha rus de bzhin bral//tsi d+ha sgo phye sgo dgur 'jug/de nas brjos kyis lo thog gzhom pa'i sbyor ba 'di gsungaso//las kyi rim pa gsum yi /
107a2(tsa): rnam pa bzlog dang lto dkrug gsum//dang pos gsum sngo sna gsum//tsha ba gsum dang lha la lnga//gzugs brnyan sor bzhi sngags kyis dril//mon thod na bran thun gsum bstim//khri tsho brgyad du dril dbab//dmigs pa'i yul dang srung ba sa
107a3(tsa): des ma phebs na sbyor ba bzlog/dmigs pa'i yul nas gzugs blangs la//tsha ba'i rgyal po de bzhin sku//bskus la gzugs brnyan par du gzhugs//bran gsum 'bru gsum mon thod bstim//dug shing sa dgod brgyad kyis bdug/dmiT
107a4(tsa): pa'i rten la gzir bar bya//des ma 'phebs na sbyor gzhan na//dug dang bsnol khrag btsal ba gnyis//bag zan bsred la sor bzhi'i tshad//gnyis byas bar du gson ma gzhug/lcags phur khri bzhi dril zhing gzir//mtha' ru yul dbus//
107a5(tsa): chu mig dkrug/rdzas dgod brgyan gyis dud pas bdug/gsal bar khyad par zil gnon brtag/chu rgod khyags pa ji ltar gtibs//dam pa'i sbyor ba drag sngags sde pa//de nas bac+doMs kyis nyid kyi sku gsung thugs rdo rje las drag snga Ta/
107a6(tsa): 'di ba ton to//e myags ya rngubs mugs sha ga ka dun//phro sha ra nA ga hur thum hril b+h+yo//b+h+yo hri la yam hu ra ram shar shar phro thun//drag po'i snying gzer hri la yaM phu hur thums dza/shar b+h+yo drag tu phog gu h+ya tsit+ta so ma dzA hA/las
107a7(tsa): gyis mchog chen nag por gsungs//de nas brjes kyis brnag pa mngon du 'pho ba'i'adi gsung so//gsang ba'i sbyor ba gsang bcud gas sbas mchog/rang la ring chas gab bzhon gas //sbyong ba rim pa lnga do rdzas/ca ba'i rgyas kyi
107b1(tsa): rtsal gsum/kha se bya ga dang bsre zho ma khris//ma bsrungs zhe sdang lus la bsduka bdun khri'i mthal ru yul dang sprad//na mkha' bar snangs la gsum//mal dang lam dang ma thebs la//bskul zhing spang bas sbyor ba 'du/b+h+yo
107b2(tsa): baM lo'i dus na smin//bgreng dang rka bzhi lus la sku ka gar bsgyur gsar pa'i sha la sbyar//gu h+ya tsit+ta so ma ho//de bzhin gnyen po stobs dpag med//ga bur skyer chu kha ru tsha//zil pa shugs drangs rdo rje'i chu//yung ka ra kha 'da' drug bsroT
107b3(tsa): laT sngags kyi rgyal po khri gsum stim//lus la bskus te 'thungs pas 'joms//de nas yang bac+doMs kyis nyid kyi sku gsungs thugs rdo rje las drag sngags 'di ston to//hu ra yaM tsa ma ri hUM/tsit+ta khas ma ra rbad//e baM raM ha la tsa ma ri byu
107b4(tsa): hur b+hi dz+wa la raM//de nas brngos kyis sbyor ba smin pa'i don 'di gsung so//bsrung dang gsang ba me tog ge zhom//bstan pa che la gnyen phyir mngags//slu ba'i bag chags gnas sbyangs nas//sku gsungs thugs kyi bde ba phog/dablugs kyi
107b5(tsa): rta skyon rkyen byas nas//srog bdud rang bshed rgyud las slong //sangs rgyas dgongs don yongasu dag/gsang lam sgo dgu'i rten sregs nas//dang gnyis shes pa 'bru gnyis bsdud//bde ba'i yis thig la mnyam//dngas ma'i mkha'
107b6(tsa): dbyings bde lam bsgod//sku lnga yai sa lde ru smin//kun tu bzang po'i bde chen gnas//phung po sgyu ma'i gnas sbyangs nas//mchod dang dpe byad longs spyod skur//lhun grub yis skur rdzogs//bag chags las dbang 'gro/
107b7(tsa): ba drug/'bru sdebs rten sbyangs tsa na'i 'bru//tsi t+ta dkar nag sdig sbal gyen//mun thab lcags sto 'khrol zhing 'u/'bru gsum gnad kyis 'phrog byed cing //so rno'i sdor bzhin po zung nas 'phrog/rang dbang med par byed
108a1(tsa): la bsdug phod pa'i ying ga khams khang phreng //gas mdzod gnasu 'chang zhing 'breg/sgrogs pa med par las rnams 'joms//drag sngags dmong pa zor la skyon//dgu skor slong la yang gsang bsdeg//de yis/
108a2(tsa): po thob bkod pa smin//sreg med srog dang gcad du med//srog med skyes bu'i lo theg tsas//log par rtog pacas nyid las//bla zhing zab pa med pa yin//srog dang 'dzin pa 'khrag pa spyi//bde ba'i gnas las ring bar 'gyur/
108a3(tsa): dus gsum sangs rgyas thugs rjes kyang //byang chub sems la spyad du med//sems de ma skyes stong bdag med//stong pa nyid ni 'byung gnas sa//rten dang rten med rang grol ba//'dod pa chen pos ye sangs rgyas//zhes sdang rna
108a4(tsa): ngag mngon sangasu rgyas//phrag dog dbang rdzogs der sangs rgyas//gti mug thig le spros med rgyas//nga rgyal mngon pa'i bde chen rgyas//sku laM lam lnga spang bya lnga//mgo zlum drang rgyas sed dang //bya ba spyod pa rna myo grags//
108a5(tsa): sku lnga bde ba'i ye sa lnga//bskyed dang rdzogs dang chen po'i ngang //bde chen sangs rgyas lug pa brgyus//rang sngags dang bcas rten la 'dzi//'gyur med pa'i sbyor ba yin//de nas bcas kyis nyid kyi sku gsung thugs rdo rje las
108a6(tsa): thu ni sngags drag po 'di ston to//e myags la rngubs lugs shag ka du na//dgra bo'i srog la thun b+h+yo//nIr rje mo raM shig tsit+ta rbang //dgra bo'i srog la hur ya ma thum re li lI b+h+yo//drug po sngags kyi sbyor ba'i mchog/dug mnga' khor
108a7(tsa): du stsal bar bsha //dpa'i tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud drug po las//sngags kyi skyon rdzogs pa'i le'u suM nyi shu ba'o///de nas yang bac+doMs kyis bsas gtan rang rgyud kyi 'khor lo gsung so//nyams dga' dben pa'i bdeg
108b1(tsa): bde ba'i gnas la bde ba'i mchog/bde ba chen po'i don nyid lnga//bde ba'i yis rang gsal brtag/yi ge sgra bcas smin par bzung //sgra nyid don da ma snyam par grags//yi ge re 'khor lo bdag nyid rdzogs//brda rngon spong ba'i nangs
108b2(tsa): ngag yid//sku nyid phyag rgya gtan gsal la//spyi bo sus bcu rtsa gnyis mdzes//bde ba chen po sa nyid dbus//oM dang gab pa'i rtsa ba bcu//shIg pa lnga dang ya yis gang //chos kyi 'khor lo sadab brgyad 'bus//oM ra rtsab
108b3(tsa): pa ma dab brgyad sul bzhi gang bar mdzes//dngas ma bzhi yis rtsa sbubs na//dmar len 'bru brgyad 'bru gnyis gang //lte ba drug bcu rtsa bzhi la//mgo dgu'i rtsa ba bcu drug dang //drug po gnod sel drug bcu gcig/'bru gnyis
108b4(tsa): gnas mthun lto sbyor bka mi rlung 'khor lo gsang dang bskor//rgya gam dag rang rtsibs drug la ka yaM ram dbus gnyis bag chags drug/rtsa gsum srog shing bde chen las//skyen gyis sbyor na gang zhig 'baraka tsit+ta'i steng na thig
108b5(tsa): le las hU~M ka kun gyi byed pa bskul zhing 'phro//yaM las yis bdo ba brod//bde ba lus la 'bar zhing gang //phyag chags sa bon drug bsreg nas//gyen du 'bar zhing phyag rgyar gsal//yum yang rtog med de bzhin nyid//gangs
108b6(tsa): lam khrus byas dgram ba kr-i la//rtsa lam 'od 'phro bag chags sbyangs ka b+ha ga'i gnas rang gi gnasu pho brang gti ba//rang gi bde ba dbab cing bskyil//rtsa lam bgrod pa shugs kyis gang //bde ba'i yis shugs kyis myong //sems kyi/
108b7(tsa): dmigs pa rtog ma mnyam//rdo rje'i bar du thun mongs bde//thun mongs bum pa'i bgrod pa thob//rdo rje'i rkang pa mchog dga' bskyed/khyad par gsang ba'i rim pa thob//rdo rje gsang ba'i rim pa thob//rdo rje gsang ba'i b+ha ga la//bde ba'i yis rang nyams/
109a1(tsa): @ltar sangs ha sa yi bde ba bskyes/bde ba chen po nyid du rdzogs//ba ba'i lis b+ha gar gnos//rtog med pa dang mnyam par gnas//yum gyi bde ba'i yis la//mnyam pa'i nyid kyi ro yis cho ga/dga' bralas
109a2(tsa): ba ro dang ldan//mnyam pa nyid kyi ngang du rdzogs//b+ha gar bskyal ba chos kyi sku//mda' brgyad gang zhing phyag rgya du gsal//bde ba'i yis long spyod sku//rtswa lam shen spros byang chub skus//na sa'i lam brgyud gnyis don spyang //sbrul
109a3(tsa): bsu 'gro don mdzad pa'i sku//bde ba'i yis mkho'i bcud //'dod chags chen po'i tshul gyisu//yis 'khor lo yongs rdzogs bsdu//rdo rje gsang ba'i lam la drang //bsgrub par bya ba'i lus la gzung //'dod chags nyi
109a4(tsa): na yai sa te//phyag rgya rten pa rang grol ba'i//'chi med tshe'i dbang thob bstan//bskul ba gcig gi man ngag yin//bde ba'i ro yis gang zhing 'bar//yub gnyisu snyam par gnas//gnas pa'i bstan pa man ngag yin//mchod
109a5(tsa): dang rdo'i bzlas pa las//dbug dang tshor ba zhen chags bral//nyams myong bstan pa'i man ngag yin//chos sku 'gag med lhun gyis grub//ye shes chen po'i phyag rgya la 'tshal//bya ba chen por tha~Md rdzogs//'chi 'pho med par
109a6(tsa): lhun gyis grub//rten dang mi gnas drang por gnas//rdo rje'i sU ru rangs gsal ba//sku la mi bzang rgyan 'khor lo//mi gamo mo spyod chos nyid rgyas//'khor 'das snong srong dang grol yin//'di ni bde dang smin pa'i las//sbyor med skyong
109a7(tsa): 'odas sbyor ba'i mchog 'di la khyed dang spo bzhes//kha rog dbyibs dang mtshan ma ru//brtag shing zhen pa yod ma yin//thig le gcig gi ngang las 'das//mi g.yog yos med g.yo ba med//sa sbyangs med sa yi sacho//zil gyis
109b1(tsa): gnon pa'i sa ru bstan//sa byengs ni rdo rje'i sa//'gyur ba med par lhun gyis grub/ma brtsal bgrong thob med pa'i lam//bde la ma 'jog po rgyan ldan la//rang rgyud pa'i 'khor lo la//'khol lo'i gnasu 'khor lo bstan//ngas
109b2(tsa): tshig 'khor lo bstan par gsungs//lha yi 'khor lo leT thob nasaka yis 'khor lo dbang bskur bya//rig pa'i 'khor los thog tu dbab//shugs kyi 'khor lo 'phen zhing 'joms//'khor lo rang gnas sbyor bar bshad//dpal
109b3(tsa): tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las bsam gtan rgyud kyi 'khor lo bstan pa'i le'u ste/ny+tsi gcig pa'o////de nas yang bac+doMs kyis srin mo lcags thog mgo dgu ma'i sbyor ba 'di gsung so//rgyal ba'i bka' las 'das bya dang /
109b4(tsa): mtshams mi da lnga la spyod pa dang //dgon pa'i gnasu phra ma dang //ram chos dge bcu spong ba dang /dzin pa'i don po log lnga myong//de ni spyan gyi smod pa po /du khrod shrIn+de der ba ru/mchog su tshang ba'i gnas batshal la//deng gsuM sngun gyi glod be bca//de sang rnas pa gnyiste/lus srog phye dang lkoganas du
109b5(tsa): dang po'i sbyor ba 'di lta ste//rgya shog dur khyod ro ras laka/sme ba lnga yi thang bcad la//ni raM ru yis mdog bton lo//thaMg sko re ris pa bzhi ru bskor//dbu su gru gsum brnag pa'i gzugs//rus khams ming dang snying po brika zla khams ming dang
109b6(tsa): snying po bri//zla khams dbang bsdus bsrung ba'i lhaka/phyi rim rgya gram phan pa'i sngags//rtsa bar dril ba'i 'bru brgyad dgod//yang logs bcus 'khor lo'i rtsibs//bla deng srog 'gugs grag po dgod//tha ma'i rtsibs chen
109b7(tsa): brgyad gnyis la//mtshon ma'i rtse mo'i sngags mgo nang //lus srog 'byed ma nang bdag 'god//bar du 'khor yug lto sbyor ming //bkod pa bag mor lto bar bas//gr-i khrag dang khrag dug mu bkrug kha sbyor mtshod gyi rgya tham pa
110a1(tsa): d+hA grI pa pa'i bye shA /myed shegs ga tu zlog glul na tu pa po du g+sa zu sa pa b+h+yo khrag gang nga du gongs sgyu ga smra d+hag ga tu kA grus pa /
110a2(tsa): zla ba'i zhag/khyong rgyan la la de bzhin sas//lugs skyems kyi ro rtse dang/sung kar bral chen sa//mau sa lang dru ga pa ba shams//sngags pas gsas gsum dzI zlog/marnyan nga
110a3(tsa): sprul pa'i ma mo srog gcong maka mgo dgu phyag kyang bco brgyad la/klu dang rag po thugs khur bstan//rang dgu'i steng du byur gu gi nags/dur d+hu khwa dang gnad kyi dbu nang grol glo snying za zhing rngam//khor gyi dgu gsum dud 'groH/
110a4(tsa): dngus dom dang dred dang chu srin dbuka rdza dang khrag dang nang grol za/ rtsa ba'i dbu gsum khro mo steka ga las dkar g.yon dmar dbus mthing la//mche ba zungs yag mi ru 'gyul//spyan nas khrag 'dzag dug rmyangs shangs//sgyu /
110a5(tsa): zhing sngags thong ba sbrulo/sku skad re ste dril bu khrag byug gA yas dbun 'arnams pa ni/mngon du sgrol ba'i sgying bram 'dzin//g.yon pa dgu na ther zeng spyod//sdug dang b+h+yo ka bal klung sen//dI bo basnrel mo khyi nag pag+sa
110a6(tsa): nag g.yas la dug theg par//zhabs brgyad rdegs pa'i swA da khang rdzi sa/'gro bsgrol gyi dgar zhums 'gyings//skra dang kha bru me rub //de la bka' bad+mo bskul zhing mngag/'khor lo zeng ba sbyin pa 'o la/dang po zun
110a7(tsa): de dag grangs rag//rgya mtsho gnyis kyi langs gru/ /gsum b+ha gyis rdzas kyim zin pag sa mtshan ma rag rdzams rmangs 'am ldag cing 'tshal/drug zis tha ma tshe srog srung /bdun pas dgang nag mtshas pa
110b1(tsa): brgyad pas srog gi mi dmar gsang //dgu bas dngas snyigs rgyud la dbye//las grub mtha' bu gnan gzir bya/khrag ram srin mo'i sgros sa mnan//gnan pa'i steng sna dmigs pa la//gas bu'i thun zar yang yang ba dag
110b2(tsa): bac+doMs kyis nyin gyi sku gsungs thugs rdo rje las drug sngags grub pa 'di ston to//e raM bai raM khr-i shig+h+ra tsa~Mt+ta rngubs ts+tsha la breng dza//oM bai raM khI tsit+ti d+hubs//dgra bo'i srog la shun rmugs shing shug cad//e dza raM wAM shig hU~MH
110b3(tsa): rngubs dgra bo'i srog la phreng breng yaM yaM tsu ri li b+h+yo//tsit+ta gzir zhums snyam pa yA nan//de nas bac+doMs kyis srin mo las la bskul ba 'di gsungaso//tshe bdag thugs kyi yang sbrul lAk+Sha srin po gcod par byed pa yi//chos
110b4(tsa): sku gnyi su med pa las//'jam dpal tshe bdag nag po'i sgra/thugs kyi sprul pa mgo ban+d+ha pa/yang sbrul dang ni sha za'i chags//me phyas 'joms pa'i mthu po che/dug dang khrag go mtsho khyil ras//dag rgyas sun nag kyis na
110b5(tsa): sri mo kha' 'dar mgo dgu pa ka/bdud dang mu stegs bram du khol//srid pa'i ma mo las la 'gyed tu drag du bskul ba mchis tsam ni//stong gsum dasha kha rol cing gsol//nga yi sha bskul glang na yod//stong gsum zos kyang nga
110b6(tsa): ma 'grangs//nga yis khrag skal gang na yod//spyod gsum khrag la myos shing 'thung //dgra bo'i sha dang khrag la rol//rtag tu bskul ba mchis tsam nA//ri rab rtse las rnyil zhing 'ong //rgya mtsho gting nas rlob cing 'od //
110b7(tsa): bo'i sha las rol las shog/dgra zo'i khrag la myos la shog/chag tu bskul pa ma chis tsam na//rang ga rang gis za zhing 'ong ka rang khrig rang gis 'thung zhing 'ong //rang sha zes kyang ngami 'grangs //ngang khrag 'jungs kyang nga mi sa/
111a1(tsa): @#dgra bo rig dang khrag la rol/rnag tu bskul bas mchis tsam na//gnam sa ri lung bzlog cing 'ong ka/ram nyams dgu la zor thun chog/drag tu bskul bas mchis cas na//lus can sa lus drag tu 'khol//
111a2(tsa): dam las 'das pa'i tsit+ta 'byin//dgra bo'i khong nas tsit+ta 'byin//drag tu bskul bas mchis tsam na//snang srid b+h+yo bzlog me yis sreg/dgra bo'i lus srog bag chags srog/drag tu bskul ba mchis tsam na//lus can b+hU la spod cing
111a3(tsa): rgyug/sprul pa'i khyi mo nag mo dgu/dgra la sprod la bla la grol//drag tu bskul ba mchis tsam na//steng gsum nad kyi dra bar 'chang //dam nyams dgra la rims nad pog/drag tu bskul ba mchis tsam na//snang srid lus sreg dngas
111a4(tsa): snyigs 'byed/ka/dgra bo'i lus srog dngas snyigs 'phye//tshe bkrag bsdus la rtsa rgyud chod//srin mo lcags thag mgo dgu ma//spyan nas khrag 'dzag ku lu luka bshangs nas dug khrag bune pu na//snyan nas 'brug s+tha di ri ri//zhal gyi/
111a5(tsa): rngam sgras stong gsum 'thung //g.yas pa dgu na snying sbram dgu//tho gas nas dgra bo'i tsit+ta 'byin//g.yon pa dgu na tha~M daM pa dgu/dgra la khrag mda' dug zor 'phenaka dgra bo'i khong du dug mda' rgyo ga//klad pa 'thon la
111a6(tsa): klad khrag myos//lus srog phrol la tsit+ta rngubs//khrag mda' dug thun phur zor rgyob//sngags kyi sku dgra sgrol las shog byas la pho nya bla nged kyi sngags bzlas la dgra la s+tha do//'khor lo thun sdebs las kyi mchog/
111a7(tsa): de mas bya ba rjes kyis/srin mo gzir te nye bai las la bsngags pa 'di gsungs//srog mkhar rgya tshil se dra mar la//khung gu'i bdud rtsis legs sbrus la//srin molo sder mo'i gong bzhin bca'/kha khyer gsum ldan
111b1(tsa): keng du po'o//snying po 'khru ga nyis 'bru dgu gzhug /drag sngags bzlos la stobs kyis gzir/srin mo bos la khrag gis bran//phur bas bsdigs la dbang du bsdug srin mo bka' nod zhabs 'og gzhug/de nas yang bac+doMs kyis/
111b2(tsa): nyid kyi sku gsung phrugs rdo rje las//gzir dang spogs pa'i drag sngags 'di ston to//e ka ya ma baM raM rag mo yaM hUM thums dzaka/dun dun trig nan/khr-i bai r-i ti shag tsit+ta/dgra bo'i srog la hU ra thu mA smA/mA ra ya rbad/snying kha'i
111b3(tsa): snying po sngags gton la//ling ka'i ming rus brnag pa bzhugs//zhag gsum thun bra bas ro khab ma na na//'byung ba dkrugs pas las rnams 'grub//rag mo dkar mo'i spogs par ba shad//de nas bac+doMs kyis 'bras bu gzhom
111b4(tsa): 'i sbyor ba 'di gsungaso//da me'i thong nang du me long bas nga //gser dngul zangs lcags brdo nag ba zhag/ra daM mar nal bu khyi nag snying //ste nga du gling bzhi rtsi bas brgyad dag bka/de steng srin mo'i sku ba zhag kha ka ma thing nag
111b5(tsa): dar gyi bka ba la bzhag/khrag sna gsum dang dgra bsres la//ling ga mi dra byang ga yag ru ra gzhug/sdug ma tshig gsum smin pa la//ltas ngan sgo bzhis bsgron la gzhag/zhag lnga'i bar du drag sngags bzlas//gser de zhu zhing
111b6(tsa): 'phel 'chang//mda' lcags blugs la dgra le brdeg/srin mo drag tu bskul la 'phang //drag sngags sbyor ba gnyi su bya//khaM raM khr-i shig tsit+ta rngubs 'ur tog phreM shar dza la raM sme sha hri la//hu ra khe zho yeng phro shar srid b+h+yo/
111b7(tsa): ma byung sgrogs na sngags 'di 'adz+ra//khwa tsa d+ha migag rungs ri bro dril cig shing nga la 'ang pa/kla ka rkyog srin po'i las 'di rgyas par shing //dpal ma bdag gi gangs rgyud nag po las phog sengs yang 'du gyi /
112a1(tsa): @ste nyi shu gnyis pa'o//de nus yang ba'arjes kyis lus kun gyi gza' bathung dug shin rdo rje ba bcwa khri'i rgyu 'dis smra /du grod phru pe smras sa
112a2(tsa): gcad la//brten brdzas dam rdzas dri bo'i snying //thod pa ri mo bde ldad der//drag po'i hom khung gru gsum tsang ka brnag pa'i rten gzugs rgyu rkyen drug/ba yi lci bas sa mar drug/'gar ta'i A ma'i 'khar rgongs la ka lIng la
112a3(tsa): sor brgyad dbang po dod//nar khrag tsha ba dug chu sku//ri sho dang ni grog lA//de yi ming dang rus bris laka/snying khar glan la sngon mar bsdam//thod par bcug la don du bzhag/zhing ras ro ras zhabs ma la//rmi ba lnga yi thang /
112a4(tsa): ba bya//bcod pa'i 'khor lo cha gnyis bris//dbus nas thig bskor bzhi ru bskor//dbusu gru gsum zla gam gsum//phyi rim 'khrul mig rgya gram ldan//yang logs rtsibs chen bcus bca'//mtha' ma gdug pa'i
112a5(tsa): ra byas bskor//srid pa rus kyi lte bar bca'//de la gnas pa'i rim pa ni ka dbu su brnag pa'i snyi po gzugs//zla gas pa gsum la bsrung ba'i lhaka rgya gram rtsa ba'i drag sngags lus//yang logs rtsibs
112a6(tsa): la spu gri spad//gdug pa'i gseb tu rtsa ba'i sngags//mtshams dang mu re na ming rus khams/bkod pa tsha ba dug chu skra ka thod pa'i steng du 'khor lo bca'//drag sngags smod pa zhag dgu bzlas//rang yang zil gnon/
112a7(tsa): sku ru gas la//bye ba dgu khri bdud du skyong ka zhing snol ru tra'i gdan stengs na//chu glang sngon po sbrul ris can//zod pa nor bris rgyan pa la/nyi zla dang bcus gdan la gnasas/sku ming ga sde rag gtu mi khro brjid/sku grong rma cin/
112b1(tsa): sta ga shi zhing /ka/sbrul dang thod pa rin po che//dbu dang lha tshigs phyag zhabs rgyan//zhal gsum phyag drug zhabs bzhir bgrang /ka/g.yas dkar g.yon dmar dbus mthing zhal ka g.yas pa gsum na ral gri dang kabe con lcags kyi
112b2(tsa): ri rab bsnams ka ga yon pa gsum na lcags kyu dang //nags pa rdo rje rgya gra ma kha snams//zhal dang sbrul dang mi ro 'brul//stod nas me 'bar 'bag chags lding //smad nas khrag 'dzag 'dab chags rol//thugs ka ra nyi ma yang
112b3(tsa): dag sngags ka/dregs pa'i tshul du las la brtson//spang ba'i drag sngags snum par bzlas//mtha' ru thod pa zhabs 'og gzhug lcags phur gshin rje'i skur sgoms la//kazir ba khri yi snying khar gdabaka pho nya sa bkug la
112b4(tsa): rten la stim ka 'khor lo ling kha thod par bca'//rdzas kyi ling kha phag par dril//dar dang re ba skud pas bsdams//gnyan ra gdug pa'i klu la gtad dbang gzugs 'o dod chos nges gdab/'byung ba pho brang don yod bsam//
112b5(tsa): mnan sngags brgya rtsa brgyad kyis bcag/las kyi kha gso thun dang sbyar//drag sngags brdud la lci bar bzlas//bla rtags byung na drag sngags brdeg/bla chen bzhi la rnal ma brdeg/sprul pa brgyad po dbus gnas po//ya du sprul
112b6(tsa): bcus mtha' gnaso//chags sdang lnga lnga 'byung ba dkrug/gzir dong mnan pa khe gso zer//ngag sngags spu gri bdun du sbyor//de yis ma lus 'joms shing 'pho//de nas yang bac+doMs kyis nyid kyis sku gsung thugs rdo las/
112b7(tsa): drag sngags 'di ston to//e/ma hU ra ya ma thi ma//dgra bo'il baM ri la dun dza ka baM myags mA ra ye rbad//tsa tsha dron ril baM la seg la myags//mA ra ya myags tsa dza ka dun ril baM srog la rba da//srog dun baM myags//
113a1(tsa): tsa la dun rbad/mA rA ya dza/srog la sbur nas/srog la thun b+h+yo/ bre shag rbad/sbyor ba de yi ma lus kun//'joms shing rangs snyigs 'byed par ba shad//de nas bac+doMs kyis las sna tshogs la bskul ba
113a2(tsa): 'di gsungaso//bskal pa brgyad khri bzhi stong 'das pa'i pha rol na ka 'byung ba brtan bzhi rngams kyi 'og shed no//rgya mtsho chen po'i gI tang rum naka mun khang lcags khang sgo med na//ru dra dregs pa'i gdan stengs naka shin rje bye ba dgu khri bzhun
113a3(tsa): sku mdog mthing nag rngam pa la//dbu gsum phyag drug zhabs bzhi bgrang ka ga yas dkar g.yon dmar dbus mthing zhal//phyag drug phyag mtshan 'bar ba ni//g.yas kyi dang po ral gri 'bar//dgra bo'i srog rtsa gcod cing gsol//g.yas kyi
113a4(tsa): bar ma zhing dbyug bsnams//khams gsum ma lus rdul du rlog/tha ma lcags kyi ri rab bsnams//dgra bgegs las ngan gcod cing gzir//g.yon gyis dang po be con snams//dgra bo ma lus mgo lus 'joms//bar ma lcags
113a5(tsa): dkyu dgra snying 'byin//tha ma zhags pas dgra bo 'gugs//dgra bo las ngan sdig can rnams//khyams khang gnas khrid las shog sku la lcags kyi tshem tshem gsol//zhing dang thod pa sbrul gyis brgyan//su smang lcags lpags zhing chu
113a6(tsa): brgyan//stod nas nga rgyal me chen 'bar//smad nas 'dod chags khrag tu 'bar//rked nas zhe sdang sbrul gyi brgyan//zhal nas dug gis drangs pa 'don//shangs nas phrag dog tshub mar 'khyi'o//snyan nas stong gsum 'brug sgra sgrogs//thug sa
113a7(tsa): las sprul pa'i phyogs bcur 'gye ka pho nya bye bas dgra bo 'gugs//yang sbrul dgu khris sreg dbugs 'phrog/las kyi mthara' bsdud khyod yin gyis//dgra bo dam nyams sdig can rnams//lag du gtad do she ra re//'di ni bka' btsogs
113b1(tsa): dmu 'jab yin//'di las sangs rgyas mi gnas kyis//'di las dam tshig gnyan mi gnas/'di la snying rje'i sa med kyis//rgya mtsho chen po'i ting du spros//ljags kyi rab 'og tu gnon//bye ba dgu NAM g+hA lA b+h+yo//l+h+wa stu smra/
113b2(tsa): btson rar dgra bo chug/dgra bo'i pho rgyud mo rgyud mchod//bcol ba'i las rnams grub par mdzod//bye ba dgu khri'i sbyor bar za shang //dpal tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las las kyi mtha' sdud kyi le'u ste/nyi shu gsum pa'o///
113b3(tsa): de nas yang bac+doMs kyis sbyor sgrol gyi las la gol ba 'di gsungaso//sbyor dang sgrol ba'i las rnams ni//rtso lo bsgrub rtags dang dpe la mtshon//rig pa gnyis med ma rtogs na/ gol bas mu stegs par 'gyur//bsnyen bsgrub
113b4(tsa): sngags don yang rdzogs pa//bya byed thob bya'i thob byed la//rang nyams 'khrul ba rang la ldog/de yi rim pa 'di lta ste/sbyor ba mi shes sbyor byed pa//gnyi su med pa'i sbyor shes na//rtags la don gyi ngo bor 'dzin pa yis/
113b5(tsa): mi ngal gyis grong khyer rgyud pa'i bdud kyi zin//thabs la ma gsang ba'i spyod pa rten shes na//rang rgyud 'khor lo rtsa las bde bos gang bskulo 'bar bab dkyil bya med don dang ldan//snang srid chags pa'i spyod pas sems
113b6(tsa): s+phar la//gnyis med phyag rgya lhun grub 'bras bu smin//bde ba chen po rang lang shar bar ba shang //sgro la ba mi shes sgrol byed pa//skye shi med pa'i don dang mi ldan no//rtsol bas sgrol ba mu stegs 'khrul pa ste//las kyi srin
113b7(tsa): pos bcings pa'i bdud kyis zin//bya med rdzogs pa'i srog kyang sbya du med//srog tu 'dzin pa'i logs rtog 'khrul pa yin//rang grol gnyis med rang shugs ru grol//yai sa chen po'i phyag rgyar rang grol naka khams gtsu
114a1(tsa): sen log pa'i srog cang na//snying byang tshogs ldan 'bras bur gyur gyis grug//sgru bu med pa'i 'phrin lus ma shes na//dpe la mtshon byed rang bzor skyong ba dag lus kyi rnas sgrim mnyam pa'i co/
114a2(tsa): rang chung //rtsol ba byas pas rtag pa'i bdud kyis phin//'phrin las lhur grub bya du med shes sa/la srin kun gyi seng dang ma chad smyos na/khas la ya dgas shing bye nya kun bkyo yang //rlung dor med pas 'bras bus rgyas ti
114a3(tsa): ma brtsa lnga ma khasgrub rdzogs te ye sangs rgyas//bsnyen bsgrub brtsel med sngags don ma shes na//phyi nang rtags la bsnyen zhing bsgrub pa//mi dge sogs la rtsol ba'i rang la 'khor//mnar med dmyal ba'i gnas la
114a4(tsa): the tshom med//snyen bsgrub rtsol med sngags kyi don shes na//srid gsum rtsol 'gro 'dus pa'i srog medangs la//lha srin kun gyis srog snying dril ba'i mchog/drag po e dang yan lag sbyor ba ni//rdo 'das pa yis/
114a5(tsa): rol par shes//gang ma tho cir spyad mtha' gnyis grol//sgron du med pa'i don dang mi ldan na//mtshan ma'i khro bo gzhan nus rtsol bsgom pa//su stegs 'khrul pa'i spyod las cag par thal//chos nyid mi dmigs stong pa
114a6(tsa): na mkha' las//skye med dang las skye ba'i cho 'phrul du kA rang rig phyag rgyu lhun gyis grub pa'i skuka gnyis med yis rdzogs ljongs spyo ba/dag chad mtha' gnyis grol bas sangs rgyas sku//phung po mig gnas sang+ya
114a7(tsa): gnas pa'o//rtogs sbyong 'das po la sangs rgyas lhun gyis grub//'di mi de rang 'chang ngan me de rtag 'phang mtha'//na mkha' rin tshe de rgya mtsho lhun po ltar//don gnyis smin pas yon ci 'phang snang //ma bcings grol da
114b1(tsa): 'grol ba'i 'bras bu smin//sdang sring lakhrul pa dug lnga'i lhag rus nas//sangs rgyas drag sngags ye shes nyi mngag //bdud bzhi'i gan su chos su bde bas bcos/waM med sangs rgyas byed men rang la stsag/sbyor sprob bya rgya/
114b2(tsa): rtag chad mtha' las 'das//gnyi su med par lhun gyis grub par bshad//dpal tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las bya brtsal bral ba'i le'u ste/rje bzhi pa'o//ka de yang bac+doMs kyis rgyud kyi don 'khor la//gdams pa/
114b3(tsa): 'di gsungaso//gsang ba'i gsang rgyud rig pa'i gnas//bde ba'i yis rang la 'char//bde ba'i yis rang gsal bka/bde ba chen po mnyam pa'i ngang //bde ba'i dbang phyug 'gags med// lhun gyis grub pa'i bde bar gnas//'di
114b4(tsa): ngag bde ba'i lam ca no//rgyas dang bsdud pa'i bye brag gis//sde pas dang rkyang pa shis tshad gsumaka/nye dang 'gyang dang dbang dang ldan//de yi rig pa 'di lta ste//gzugs brnyan 'khor lo chags gang la//lus dang spu gri mtshams dag
114b5(tsa): tu/mu khyud bzhi yi 'khor lo la//gang bsgrub gsad pa'i sngags kyis mdzes//khro bo nAng la ma mo dbus//gnod sbyin phyi ru shes par bya//rang gi 'khor lo lto bsdebs pa'o//bstan pa lhun po gsal ba yi//tsit+ta gsang ba'i
114b6(tsa): ba~M brang na//brgyad dang bcus rtse dbus hUM//khro bo'i sngags dang ma mo dbus ka gnod sbyin gyi ru shes par bya//man ngag gsang ba'i sdebasu bya//drag po'i lus la sbyin pa'i sngags//khro lus la ma mo gdags//de bzhin dregs pa'i
114b7(tsa): gang chub ste //de bzhin spad dang gsad pa sbyar//spong ba zhal gyis sde bsu gsungs /ka/rang gshed drag pos gsad pa'i sngags//lus dang spu gri'i mtshams ngag tu//rang sngags nang la gshedas byig drag po gsad pa dang rang sngags sdes/
115a1(tsa): las kyi gshed bsngags brgyab m+b+ha la/de dag rangag skye dgas ma byems yahA/de bzhin rdzun pa sngags sa rdzugs/du phus la snru sa lag/sku khri sbyong ba shes pa //las kyi kyai phunan+tu/
115a2(tsa): bape man ngag gnad mdar sbyor ba yin//lus la smyu gri sngol mar sdig/dgra bo 'dul ba'i d+hI dgal yin//las kyi zhan bcug 'gyur te//dug 'gyur stong ba stobs dpag med//sngags ni lcen kun rdzogs pas//lha dang bdag /
115a3(tsa): las 'khor kun//rang rang so ser sdebs pa 'khrug/bde byed tshangs pa'i gnas las 'phang //rig pa bde ba'i klong du bsdus//yos nyi mo'i 'od las 'phros//gang grub las kyi pho nya la//mngag pa zad pa'i mtha' du 'gyur//dgra bo'i srog
115a4(tsa): la tsu ri li li//pho nya bla deng brgya brgya ma ngag/de yis thaMd 'joms shing 'gugs ka/drag po sngags kyi gsang bsdebs ni//dang po lus srungs rtsa ba stongs//de 'og kha 'ching gzir ba stong //mkha'agro kha gzan dgra la 'phyo//mthal du srog
115a5(tsa): rtsa gzungs thag gcod//gnan pa gzir dang bur ba gdagaka/drag po sngags kyi spyi sdebs yin//dor nang gi drag po yin//lus ni gsang zhing lci bar gnas//mtsham su snying po pan d+hu ling //gzir bya gang migs gsal bar thod//mtha' du glu gr-i
115a6(tsa): gan bdang gcod//drag cing 'jal la 'ab ba ning gangs//bsung bkun la shes pa yin//lus theg 'byung ba drug pa'i sngags//lus kyi gsal la dag par bzlas//snying po ras khams med bcas pa //gcad pa lnga ya~M bu pa
115a7(tsa): lce la rig pa yid la gsal//yul gyi rnam pags par rtag/ming sdug mi 'gugs rang sa rarigs lo//gcod byed spu gri rno dpal mtshon//gang yang rdo myur tshad ldan pas//me dril drag la par zhing 'dril//de yai kar nag rten la'i rog
115b1(tsa): bsrung dang gong cha sha bag rus//drag cing dkar nag sgo phyi nas//rtsa bzhi gcad nas rtsa mdud bgrol ka/dngas ma bzhi bsdus thig le 'bAr ka rA ga gzugs can stong bdag med ka snyigs ma la gnyis me 'bar khrag/re dbus skal
115b2(tsa): ba nI dang sdigs//sngags kyi me gsang sdig sbal gshegs//yi ge hur zhums dza yi phrog/skye ba'i cho 'phrul e las sduka srid pa'i khyams gang gan su bya/la khrag lus la na za rol ka gsang ba sngags kyi drag sngag
115b3(tsa): yin du dpal tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las//rgyud kyi don 'khor la gdames pa'i le'u ste/lde lnga pa'o///de nas yang bac+doMs kyis ltas ngan dang cho 'phrul dbab pa 'di gsung so//sra ma dang phyi ba'i thod pa ru/
115b4(tsa): byi la ze mod smin bya'i khrag/spu dang sha sbyar zhabs mas dri la//de bzhin gar 'bebs ming dang sngags//khrag gis bris la thod par gzhug/khri bdun bzlas la zhing ras dri lakagaso ba de sros la dmigs pa'i 'gram la gzhug/
115b5(tsa): zla ba bdun na ltas ngan dbang//sems kyi lta la che dmigs bya//sbrul dang sbal pa'i gso ba shug lak+Sha dgra yig chu dug khrag bsre//gdug pa'i sa la gzugs byas te ka dug chus sbrus la de nyid gzugs//gang dmigs bcas pa/
115b6(tsa): sngags kyis dril//gso bo te sbyangs la tsha ba sku//drag sngags tshad ldan lam ma ma gzhug/gdugu pa gzugs kyi tshe'i phul 'bung //bong pa rung ma gnyis btsal la//dab pa rnal gri khrag kha mig gdong //dug dang skye ba lung da
115b7(tsa): bsrun gang dmigs ming dang phe ba la gzhug/mkhor gyi dris dang bang 'og swa//zod pa 'thabs dang rgyug pa 'thung //khrag dang 'ug pa srin bya'i snying //sha khrag dang bcas bag zan dug//dgra yi khangs tsha ba sbyar//kha sno ba khrag bsdus

116a6(tsa): las byung ba//dges pa gar dbus gdug pa 'joms bya khyud rgyal po snying po rdzogaso//dun gyi yais las byung ba//srog dang dbang po las bsdus nas//las kyis tshe bdag srog bdud 'joms//snying gi yis las byung ba//gdug pa
116a7(tsa): gnasu 'joms dang 'pho//khyung mo smug nag snying pa rdzogs//khrag gi yais lngas byung ba/dregs pa gnod sbyin gdug pa 'joms//gsang bdag rdo rje'i snying po rdzogs//ra yi yis las byung ba//srog la skyems la gnasu spor//
116b1(tsa): zhe nga gdag par snang ba rdzogs//ma yi ye sa las byung ba/b+h+yo ny+tsa lus la sangag pa ste//b+h+rUM bya'i phung po bdu sh+lo rnams/ma ses las byung ba/snang grang sangs rbA bsdus nas tsha //sring pa sa skul d+hu
116b2(tsa): por rdzogs//ya yi yis las byung ba//las kyi rlung gis 'phrul zhing skyes//ya mA ra tsa'i snying por rdzogs//rmugs kyi skyes las byung gA/khams gsum dbang las bsgyur nas//rngubs kyi yis las byung ba/cha grang nang sran
116b3(tsa): 'bras bu rgyas//mugs kyi yis las byung ba//pho nya sha za rang dbang bskor//dza yi sreg byad drod dbang bsgyur//r-i gyis spor byed drug drug sreg/sha ni srin pos bde bar bsgo la//ru yis chos nyid gsal bar byed//thum gyis ma
116b4(tsa): sdang gnasu 'joms//dpal tshe bdag gi dgongs rgyud nag po las sngags kyi yon gyi le'u ste/sde bdun pa'o////de nas yang bac+doMs kyis srog dmar len gyi rtsa ba 'bru bzhir bsdus pa'i sbyor ba 'di gsungaso//dur khrod dben pa'i
116b5(tsa): bde gan su//rang sems dben pa'i tshams gcad la//dpa' brtul sngags pa'i drag sngags bzlas//dur khrod rgyud la tsakra bcar//mu khyud bzhi dang ldan par skor//lte ba grub gsum ma ra nA~M//gnyis pa rgya gram e dun ka y+ya ma//gsum

116b7(tsa): lho sde bas dgod//tha ma'i mtshon cha'i brtsibs bcu la//myogs dang cad dang khyer dang byung //mugs dang ting ling dang shag/ling dang zad rnams rdzogs par dgod//srid par rus sbal lto na gnas//kha sbyar rdzas kyi sbal bar gzhug
117a1(tsa): @mtshon 'akrung lnga dang phag po'i lte ga/gzi la chog par skye khar gdug/sod pa bcas la klu la gcig gtan dang mchod rte nag go g+ha/g+haN+Tad pa'i las tshogs gong gi suganyen sa spyan bAH/

118a6(tsa): gri khrag khyi khrag rta nag khrag/dong bung sngags ba stis zong du 'phang //sngo snga ma ma khrag dug snga go//zhabs dmar sngags sdom thal gyi rdzag/lcags pur seng ldeng rdzas dgod rnams/dug giso dang thung drug sngags dkrug/spogs pa drag pos
118b1(tsa): gzir ba'i rdzas//dgun zla gsum pa'i la nyin zhag cig/chu dril khri tshe zhig tu stis//dpying zla gsum la zor dril sdig/zhag re dong du thod par stis//dbyar zla gsum gyi nya stong la/ zhagasum nya ya ta ma gzhug/yul dbus klu dang
118b2(tsa): rten la//thod can skyang thod sbrul bzhug dang //ston zla nya stong rnams bzlog brdeg/brgya pa bcu par khrag la stes//stong gi bar du rdzas rnams bstam//khri bar phur ba dgu rdzaswa sbyis//drag sngags brnal bzlog 'byung ba dkrug/khor bring
118b3(tsa): thob dang gzir la sbyar//de yis thaMd ma lus 'joms//sman dang rtsi shing lo thog la//drag po gnam lcags ser po 'bebs//tshes brgyad sa sros bzhi yi 'jing //nya yI spyod khrod bcug yol//nyi shu dgu yi mdung gang la//da
118b4(tsa): pa gsum pa'i sbyor ba btsam//grub na d+ha ma sngags kyi gso//de nas bac+doMs kyis nyid kyi sku gsung thugs rdo rje las drug sngags 'di ston to//e dun g+ha g+ha/U kha~M khr-i br-i hri pho bo//shar shar dril dag tu phog/m+b+hi ka dun N+ye/
118b5(tsa): shar hri la b+h+yo//kau dum ra pob//ka e l-i dun nA ga snying gzer phreM ya hri le shar//chu mig lho rten la dmigs shing //zer thab sbyor ba tha~Md pa zangs//me lag mi stsogs thal 'jims bor//gzugs brnyan gnyis ba ba rten
118b6(tsa): bzhag la/'byung ba dkrugs la khri bzhi sgom//gang migs kyu gdus phreng ba gdag/chu dgad drag po'i re bskyes 'dra//rang lang+ga ring la gab par ma shad//de nas bac+doMs kyis//drag po gnas thogs 'phangs pa'i sbyor ba 'di gsug
118b7(tsa): de bzhin gnas dang mtshas sa gcad la//zod rgod dri thog nyams can du//se longang+ga gser dang theg lcags zangs//rdo nag lung ka rgyal po srungs//rta nag rabs 'chang khye rag khrag/dun sna dang bcas ste 'og sten yul dang bcas pa'i

119a6(tsa): zang sbyor bar bshang //de nas yang bac+doMs kyis skyes ngan gyi sbyong ba 'di gsung so//skyil dang byi la l+ya thod dang //sbrul dang srin bya zag gA dang //gzugs dang mid ras bcas la bsang//rdege sngags khri bdun dag wa sku//chos brgyad rtso/

120a1(tsa): da ra yin//gi sbyo ste kye 'di//khye gas ka stongs grub pa / kyi g+sa k+Sha // ga las shA de las yid//'di la kunas sgru pa phywa //'dung pa'i ngos pa na

121b1(tsa): dbugs dang ma bral drang ba'i dbang po nyams//'khor rnams srog dang ldan par mdzod ka zhes gsol ba btab po//de nas bac+doMs kyis 'khor rnams brgyal ba las bskyung ba'i phyir//dbyugs 'byung ba'i ting 'dzin la snyoms par bzhug so//

121b3(tsa): pa nyams//rdzu 'phrul rmad du byung ba ngo mtshar che//rang ngam srog gi snying po rnams//bstan pa bsrung phyi pha dngos sol dbul//srog gi snying po 'di bzhes la//dkyir chen po bsrung bar gyis//zhes zhus pas//bcom ldan dgyes
121b4(tsa): pa chen por gyur te/zhi khro dang /gshin rje dang /ma mo dang /bdud pho mo dang /srin po pho mo dang //rgyal po dang /gi nga pho mo dang /btsan dang /srog bdag dang /sa bdag dang /klu dang gnyan dang /lha ma yin sde brgyad kyis srog gi

122a4(tsa): hr-id kyi dkyir brtag/lhun gyis grub pa'i dkyor 'di lta ste//gru bzhir rin chen rgyu dang ldan//thod pa'i rkang rus brtsig par bcas//sgo bzhi dur khrod 'khyams dang 'gram//rta babs bzhi dang rta ra na//lhun po bzhi dang ldan pa'i gnas//nor

122b1(tsa): bzhir gshed po yub bcasaka rtsibs bcu phyogs skyongs rtsibs chan bdud ka gu chang ro langs lce sbyang gnas//khyams la sha za po mo'i tshogs//sgro bzhir sgo ma de bzhin gnas//phyi khyams 'khor yug sde du rgya rdegs//
122b2(tsa): 'bum phrag g.yog dang yang g.yog bcas ka drug khri drug stong 'khod pa yis ka rang sngags srog gi 'brel pa'o//dkyor bdag po gnas pa ni ka 'khor du bdag po 'dus pa yis//'khor gyi ya ma rA dza la sogs pa

123a1(tsa): 'chang rgyal po//'gro ba'i don dug shin rje gshed/sku gsung thugs kyi byid las mchog bsdus pa//mi dge 'tshams med dman pa kur sgrol ba 'ikA las kyi rgyal po sbyor ba'i sman pa'i rtson/
123a2(tsa): 'byor chen gdug pa gnod tshul dregs pa yis ka yan lag bcus bzhi dang ga yang bzhi dang //gzer thun drag po'i bsam pa rgyas par mdzod//zhes zhus pa gsan nas//snying po byang chub sems 'dus nas/byin
123a3(tsa): rlabs ldan pas gsang bcud bstan//'jig rten khams na 'dus pa yis//'gro ba ma lus dregs pas myos//bstan pa rin chen rgyas lam gcig/lus kyi sbyor ba brgya phrag brgyad//yan laganyis brgya stong
123a4(tsa): drug/rtsa ba'i lo grangs bdun cu bzhika/sgrub pa dngos bzhi lnga dang gcig/bgrod pa'i rim pa drug dang ldan//yan lag lnga dang bcas pa bzhi//rten gyi sbyor ba las bcus//'dren pa'i gsang baM la ma bcus ldan//

124a1(tsa): ga gI/bdag dbang stobs kyi me bdag ni//rtsa ba bskyed dang spyan drang dang //gzugs gsol phyag dang phyag rgya dang //mchod dang stobs rang bzlas pa dang /bskul pa'i dus kyis lus bsgrung/

124b2(tsa): med 'gru ba//de phyir tshe srogs bdag por gsungs//mgo dgu yon 'di lta ste//mgo dgu theg pa dgu mtshon nas//gab pa dgu'i don dang ldan//bco brgyang phyag gi ljo na la sa ka zhing bcu'i srog dang dngas ma brgyud

159a2(tsa): kyi dang po ra gyi ba rnams//dgu bo mas na ga skyed cing yo //g.yas kyu ra pa zhing phyug bsgrums/khrums klusum pa lus mu du ts+tsha k+Sha/sa lpags kyi ras bsdusdus// phyogags las bad gan

159b1(tsa): bye ba dag khri'i sbyor mtha At+ma//e rlung sbyis byung 'khrugs pa'i rgyud las/las kyi mtha' 'asgrud kyi lho'u skye so ts+tshang ba'i//de nas sa kyis nyid kyi las sau r+ya dag po gsang bya'i las kyi rim brtson rgyu

271a6(tsa): bo'i thod pa dang /bya dang khyi dkar dang spre'u dang /byi la dang ra dka ro//dpal drag sngags zla gsang shan pa spu gri'i rgyud las/gtad rdzas dang sgrib rdzas kyi le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas srog rten bca' ba ras sam rgya shog
271a7(tsa): la 'khor lo rtsibs brkyang du bya'o//dbus zlum por bzhag/'khor lo la bod sngags bri//khyung bskor du nang sngags bri//me ris bskor te dbu suM bla rdo klubs la//bu mo mtshan ldan ma'i snying lpag su phur pa dang //ga bur bcug/
271b1(tsa): la dbusu bzhago//dpal drag sngags zla gsang shan pa spu gri'i rgyud las/srog rten bca' ba'i le'u skye go bzhi pa'o////de nas lag na rdo rjes 'di skad ces gsol to//kyai bac+doMs drag sngags mgor du dril bdagan pa spu
271b2(tsa): gri'i rgyud las//dang po ji ltar byung zhing gang la bsgyur//the ma nar dus ji ltar yin//yongasu rdzogs pa'i gtang ba gang //zhes pas nyon cig/thugs rje chen po dang ldan chos kyi sku//mnyam pa chen po kun bzang sku re yis//bstan
271b3(tsa): pa rin po che la rab tu dgongs//dus ngan las kyis mnang pa re zhing //bcu gdul sla du//gung kum 'dra las chu nag lta bur sprul//dregs pa brtul te dur khrod por gsungs//dpal drag sngags zla gas shan pa spu gri'i rgyud las/le'u skye
271b4(tsa): go lnga pa'o////dus ni lnga brgya phrag bcu tsam na//rig 'dzin kun gyi dngos grub yol tsam ni//dus gsum sa so sprul pa 'jig rten pa//rdegs pa'i sngags 'di nyid dang //de'i dus su dregs pa can khyed kyis//rna myo
271b5(tsa): dam la gnas pa rnams//bsrung ba dang /bskyab pa dang /las bya ba ci bcol pa dang /'khor dang nor spel ba dang /ci la ci skos pa yang byed la//lha srin khyed yang de ltar gyis shig/mthu stobs kyi bdag pos dbang mdzod la//gang zhig
271b6(tsa): slob dpon slong ba//dam la mi gnas pa//grags pa can/'ang don la tshogs pa/de dag la lha srin gyi chadra bas ched cig/dam tshig dang ldan pa rnams kyi las mi byed na//phyag na rdo rjes mgo chos shig/ces gsung //
271b7(tsa): dpal dran sngags bla gsang shan su gri rgyud las//pus gang dang dang lha srin la bka' sgo ba'i le'u ste go drug pa 'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das nyid kyis 'di skad ces gsungs so//chos nyid rang bzhin
272a1(tsa): senas rgyuran grub pas//ye shes dkyil 'jong byu rjen po shA /gdug pa gsun phyir rdegs pa'i phyog sbyang blos//bskrun sgyu phru d+ma khyod nyid dru/

272a3(tsa): par bsod do//gtsobo nyid kyis be/d+ha sul nyin par ring dam rang gis bca so//dpal rlug sngags shan pa bru g+h+ri'i rgyud las+ho//rgyud gtad cing rjes yi rang ba'i le'u skye go bdun pa'o//
272a4(tsa): shan pa spu gri'i sngags rgyud rdzogs s+ho///rgya gar gyis mkhan po ni//bo'i rgyal po ba su d+hi re dang /zod kyi lo ts+tsha ba bdag 'dra sbubs kyi phan chung yang dpung sangs

272a6(tsa): gsang rmod pa dam yin no///rgya rgya rgya rgya/sarba mang+ga baM//
273a1(tsa): /rgyu gar skad du/Ar+ya many+dzu shrI sa pa Na pU ta/su Na NA t+yai she kA ti ka/kro ta b+hi dz+ya a~M rgyan shib kA na sA/bong skad du/'phags pa 'as dpal gsang ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal g+ho khA khog sna ga brtags/khyim/
273a2(tsa): rgyal po sgrub pa'i lung //khro bo rnang rgyal ba'i rgyud kyi ma'i yang bye ma zhes bya ba//brjes gshin rje gshed la byug 'tsha lo//'jam pa'i dbyangs la phyag 'tshalo//de nas 'jam dpal dbyangs kyis ni//khro bo rI ming ni gshin
273a3(tsa): gshed//mtshon cha me ltar 'bar bas 'khrigs//drag la mthu rtsol drag ldan pa//de mthong nas ni khro bo gzhan//thams cad kun gyis la go cha shong //de bzhin du ni 'jig rten po//lha rnams lus kyis 'dud par byed
273a4(tsa): gzhan yang thal mo sbyar byas te//skrag nas stong pa mang po byed//lha rnams mang po ji snyed kun//'jas dpal khro bo'i gzi brjid kyis ka gzir zhing du ni rab smras pa ka btsun mgon po bdag cag gis//sangs rgyas chos dang /

273a6(tsa): ngas na//de tshe 'jam dpal dbyangs kyis ni//khro bo blar yang zhi bar 'gyur//gshin rje gshed kyi ces bya ba yang /khro bo chen po zhi ba lA/ka/de nas skyabasu dong ba yis//'jam dpal dbyangs lha rnams kun gyis rab smras pa kaM/

273b1(tsa): gsum ngon du byung //sangs rgyas kun gyis sngon tu byung //nga ni dus gsum phyis kyang 'byung /ka/nga ni dus gsum lta par byung // ni lta brgyab mchi cho//pa'i bstan pa 'byung bar byed//bstan pa bam pa cung chub pa sa
273b2(tsa): pa ri bstan pa 'byung bar byed//las ngan 'tshams med byed pa'i tshe//de tshe ba'i bstan pa 'byung //zas ngan lci ba za ba'i tshe//de tshe nga'i bstan pa 'byung //gos ngan dug hrul gon pa'i tshe//de tshe pa bstan pa 'byung //
273b3(tsa): nga ni dus gsum 'byung ba'i sku//bstan pa thaMd nub pa'i tshe//de tshe nga'i bstan pa 'byung //ji ltar bur ni zhi 'gyur yang //nga ni thad zhi ba'i sku//ji ltar bur ni khros 'gyur yang //thaMd khros pa pari sku//lha rnams
273b4(tsa): thams cad khyed nyon cig/gzi brjid chen po khyed rnams kun//gang tshwa gsum la skyabs song ba//de'i tshe mI zing dang //khro bo'i yid la gnas par gyis//dkon mchog gsum la gnas par gyis//de'i
273b5(tsa): bka' yang brkyang ba dang //si naM kun la snying brtse ba'i mying //nges pa'i yid ni zhi 'gyur gyis//khyed rnams sgrubasu dong ba dang //byang chub sems dang ldan pa'i//thad don ni grub pa'i chos//yi ge 'bru ni dza
273b6(tsa): bdun pa'o//de snying po yid las byung //lha rnams kun gyis bzung bar gyis//gshin rje gshed po bdag nyid kyis//snying po rab tu gsungs pa na//de nas sa yang g.yos brda gyur//rgya mtsho yang ni rab tu lazlan
273b7(tsa): gnod sbyin dang ni srin po dang //sha za mapa pa sogs phung bar 'gyur/lha rnams kun gyis phyag 'tshalal+li/yan lag kun nas pa spu langs//gshin rje gshed po khro bo yis//gtso bo'i snying po bzung //den so 'i
274a1(tsa): chos srung ba'i phyir//khro bo nir rgyal ba'i yis//rtog pa 'di ni de dag laM//kA shin+da rje gshed kyis rgyas par gsungs//de nas khro bo rnams rgyal gyis//rtog pa khro bo nyid gsungs pa/snyi
274a2(tsa): Sh+Ta sems ldan pa'i//lha rnams kyis thor dkar gyur nas//dkon mchog gsum ni bskyung ba'i phyir//sau yid ni rtog par bshad//de dag gi ni rtsa ba'i rgyud//blo nyid kyis rab gsungs pa//khro rno rgyal zhes bya 'idi/
274a3(tsa): 'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten pa'i//skyon du byug byas khro yis//zhal nas ji bzhin thos nas kyang //khro bo nyid kyis gsungs pa'i//rgyud 'di byung chub rnams kyi mnan//snying rje de bzhin rgyas pa dang //rnam mang po byin/
274a4(tsa): gyis brlab//'jam dpal gyi rtsa ba'i rtog pa//khro bo rna ra rgyal ba'i rgyud 'byung zhes bya baji skabs ste/le'u dang po'o////de nas khro bo rna ra rgyal//'jam dpal khro bos bshad bA yis//rtog pa rgyu du byung ba'i/
274a5(tsa): mchog/mdor ni rab tu bshad par bya//de la 'di'i dkyil 'khor ni//dngos grub thad sbyin mdzad pa'i/'jig rten gsum po 'khrug par mdzad//yi dgas mang po zhes bya'o//tha daM du ni dkyil 'khor 'di//
274a6(tsa): gnas 'a nI gang du grags 'gyur ba//sngags pras thog ma nyid du ni ka dwe'i gnas ni rtog par bya//shing cig pa 'am dur khrod dam//khyim stong pa ni gzhi mdo dang //mchod rten dang na mu gnyis dang //thibs
274a7(tsa): po steng pa la srang rnying //skyed mos tshal dang ri'i rtse//chu mo'i 'grom dang rinags tshal//yang ni rgya mtsho'i 'gram da ni//lce chen gyi ni gnas kyang rung ka ba lang lhas sam g.yul gyis//khyad par du ni
274b1(tsa): yul gtsang bar/gang du yid ni dga' bar yang //der ni dgyir rAg tu byA//de nas rig 'dzin nan tan gyis//sa ni yang dgu sbyang byas te/pa ma med cings gas pa//mnyam zhing zug rngo med pa la//nu dang byung /
274b2(tsa): byang gi mthong ba dang //kha dog snum par lhag par bzang //bA'i lci d+hus byug par bya//ci rigs par ni khrus byas la//slob dpon gyis ni khrus byas la//skyug par 'dug skye mnyam gzhag nas//khro'o'i skyong
274b3(tsa): bas byin gyis brlabs//yi dregs phal chen zhes bya ba'i//dkyor gsang mchog nub mo byi bar bya/khyi ras sam ni ras bal la//thig spyod sum sgru ba mdzes pa yi swA//grwa bzhi'i sa dang bsgo bzhi'i
274b4(tsa): ba ra/mkhas pas dkyor thig btab ste//thog mar shar dang byang nas ni//lho dang nub kyi bar du yo//thing skyong dang ni phyogs mtshams nas//de nas kyang ni drang bar bya//shar dang lho'i mtshams dag la//nub dang byung
274b5(tsa): gi mtshams dag tu//thig gi rab tu tshang byas ste//gru bzhi'i bar ni thigs bdab por/rim pa rtsi gau pa 'dzes po la//sgo bzhi dag gis mdzes par bya//lho phyogs kyi ni sgo che la//lhag ma rnams kyis gcad par bya//
274b6(tsa): dkyi dang ni mtsham pa'i//dbusu badz+ra bri bar bya//pad+mo dag gi phyogs bzhi'i skor//the tshom meng+ga bar mnyamA b+hAr bya//mdzes byed sgro'i d+ye m+b+ha/'gram pa 'dab ma tshad mnyam//nye ba 'di ni mdzes byed kyong /
274b7(tsa): dzes byed dang mi 'dra bar bya/tshig da du kying nga//sun gyo khrig gis ba pa ras kyang //phyi nas tshul mazhi rim pa 'dzin gyis//du ma zad tha'i bas bri ba ni//na tu gar byang la sogs pa'i rnon
275a1(tsa): @#de dag dang //mkhas pas be con phyag rgya yis//phyogs kyi sgor ni yi dwags bzhag/gzhan yang yi dwags lnga pa yis//pad+mo'i steng du bsam par bya//de bzhin sku'i nang dag dang //rdzogs rnams
275a2(tsa): de dag thad du//mi rom mgo sbed yi dag rnam sak+Sha/dur khrod thal ma ngar te bri//de phyir yi dgas pal chen yin//'khrugs pa la sogs rim pa nas//khru brgyad po'i bar du rten//de las lhag par yod mi bruM//
275a3(tsa): gang bar ni bri bar bya//zhi ba dang ni rgyas pa dang //mi skyong bsgrub pa khru do bstan//sIni kun ni bskrad pa dang //de bzhin dbang dgug pa laka las rnams kyi ni mchog tu gsungs//khru ldi nang dang mi nad dang //dgra rnams med par
275a4(tsa): gsad pa'i mchog/khru drug bgegs rnams thaMd dang //gdon rnams nges par bzlog par byed//khru bdun pas ni lha'i rnams//dbang dang dgug pa'i las rnams byed//khru brgyad pas ni dngos grub kun//smin byed mchog
275a5(tsa): du shes par bya//de ltar ci rig bris nas ni//de nas slong byas nyi reg byas//chu'i phyir ni chu'i snod//chus ni rab tu dbang du gang bar bya//de bzhin me tog don du yang //myog sbu gu dri zhim dang //stog 'phreng ba snod mtsho gzhan//
275a6(tsa): legs par bgang zhing mdzes par bya//ri'i don du tsan dan mchog/tsan dkar po dmar po blang //a ka ru dang sbyar ba'i//gu gul bdug pa'ang de bzhin ni//mar gyi me'i mor me dang //mkhas pas snying po 'jam pa'o//

275b1(tsa): lub sems la dmigs nas ni/ka/ri mo yi ni las rnams dag/gal te 'bri bar mi nus na//yi dgas pa la chen dbus dag tu//pad+ma sngar bris gang yin pa//de'i steng du yi dwags ni//mkhas pas yid kyis bsam par bya//yi//
275b2(tsa): dgas kyis ni sngags mchog gis//bsam pa dag ni byas nas ni//grwa'i byug pa bzhi po yang //sngags pa nyid kyis bsam par bya//gal te 'bri bar nus nA ni//sngags nyid steng du bzhag par bya//sgo'i yi dgas bzhi po la//gal te bri bar
275b3(tsa): ma nus na//de bzhin yi dwags 'jigs sngags kyis//byin gyis brlob par bya ba'i phyir//rkang lag mgo bo brag pa dang //gzhan yang de bzhin lag pa'i phreng //thod pa keng rus mi lpags rlon//ro'i phreng ba bsam par bya//de'i nang du rang
275b4(tsa): sngags kyis//byin gyis rlab tu gzhugs par bya//de ltar cho ga bzhin bsams nas//mjug tu khro bo gshin rje gshed//sngags brjod pas ni spyan drangs nas//rang gi snying pos bzhug stsol//khro bo 'khrugs pas 'jigs mdzad pa/

275b7(tsa): drug po yang ga/thong ba shag ti the ma ra//sdigs dang phyag ma nags po dang //phyag na dubyug the bos ma par bya/mi mgo rmon dang skam po dang //yi dgas 'phreng bas rnarang brgyar//b+hagri wAT ba na bar gso ga/su yang me la gsum khyems
276a1(tsa): zhal gyi dbyings ni dum te//b+hU ni rgya mtsho 'khrug pa 'dra//nam cig cand cing zhal cag zhi//rnam cig 'jigs par bzud ba'i na ga//zhal cig zhal ni gas pa ste//lcags dag kyang ni bskyed par mdzas/

276a3(tsa): sbyin par byed//de nas tsAn dmar phul ba//blo ldan gyi ni khro bo la'o//pri rI bgo dro lha bshos dag/tsan gyis ni gtor ba'o//me tog dang ni 'phreng ba rnams//maug sngags kyis dbul bar bya//me tog sngags kyi rim bzhin du//
276a4(tsa): me tog cho ga bzhin phul nas//ji ltar snying po'i sngags kyis kyang //gtso bo'i khro bo snying ka dang //khro bo'i ni spyi bor yang //snying po rnar bkod par bya//tshogs ma de'i mtshogs ma dang //de'i gtsug dang spyi btsug gzhan
276a5(tsa): de'i lus la cho ga dang //mtshon cha'i gnasu mtshon cha'o//me tog yod kyis bzhag pa ni//ji ltar gsungs pa bzhin du bya//de ltar mchod nas 'dug pa ni//sngags kyis btab ste nga la 'bul//de nas ma
276a6(tsa): mar rab 'bar ba//phyogs brgyad du yang bgor par bya//'byung po'i grwa tor ma khro bo la//phul nas yi dwags phal chen gyis//phyi rol du ni mkhas pa yis//sme gtor rnams ni sbyor bar bya//khro bo'i gtso la de
276a7(tsa): phyogs te/de nas maung ni bdug pa yul/de'i phyi rol chubacas sdod/me tog gis ni bkang ste bzhag/chog lus gyis srung ba'i//slob ma dag ni bris nas kyang //thams cad sngags kyi
276b1(tsa): 'dzin byas ste/khra ma ya ni snying na yis//lag gnyis me tog byin nas ni//gos kyis ni g.yog par bya//dkon mchog gsum ni dran rdzas te//slob dpon gyis ni gzhug par bya//rang ni snying po me chog nyid/
276b2(tsa): la ni 'khor du bzhug/de nas be con zhes bya ba//sngags pas de'i phyag rgya bstan//dkon mchog gsum ni gsang ba yis//dam tshig de la sbyin par bya//khro mo gtso bo'i sngags kyis ni//sngags zhes pas ni dang
